### [1] [GeoDrive-Bench: Benchmarking Region-Specific Multimodal Reasoning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02774)
*Yingzi Ma,Chaowei Xiao,Ming Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoDrive-Bench基准，通过5,053个跨六国的人类验证多选题，评估VLM在感知、预测、规划和区域推理等四个驾驶任务上的跨区域驾驶推理能力，并设计了蒸馏算法注入区域交通规则知识，实验表明当前VLM缺乏鲁棒的区域感知驾驶智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在自动驾驶中表现出色，但对处理区域特定交通规则的能力研究不足，导致在多样化的全球环境中部署存在不确定性；需要系统评估VLM在地理文化背景下的驾驶推理能力。

Method: 1) 构建包含5,053个跨六国人类验证QA对的数据集，覆盖多样化驾驶文化；2) 划分感知、预测、规划和区域推理四个任务；3) 设计蒸馏算法将区域交通规则知识注入VLM内部表示。

Result: 在9个SOTA VLM上测试发现，各任务在地理驾驶文化间存在显著性能差异；提出的基线模型在跨区域地理文化推理方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 当前VLM仍缺乏鲁棒的区域感知驾驶智能；GeoDrive-Bench可作为可部署自动驾驶基础模型的诊断和训练导向测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) for autonomous driving have shown promising performance, but their ability to handle region-specific traffic rules remains underexplored, raising uncertainties about their deployment across diverse global settings. We therefore introduce GeoDrive-Bench, a novel benchmark that enables the systematic investigation of VLMs' geo-culturally grounded driving reasoning. We curated 5,053 human-validated multiple-choice QA pairs across six countries covering diverse driving cultures. Specifically, we emphasize four driving tasks: perception, prediction, planning, and region reasoning. Each question requires models to infer the correct driving behavior from visual evidence and local traffic conventions without explicit country labels. Beyond evaluation, we further design a distillation algorithm that injects region-specific traffic-rule knowledge into the internal representations of VLMs, enabling models to better align visual scene understanding with local driving policies. Experiments on nine state-of-the-art VLMs show substantial performance variations across geo-driving cultures for each task, while our proposed baseline models exhibit improved geo-cultural reasoning across regions. These results suggest that current VLMs still lack robust region-aware driving intelligence and highlight GeoDrive-Bench as a diagnostic and training-oriented testbed for deployable autonomous driving foundation models.

</details>


### [2] [Any2Poster: Any-Source Poster Generation Across Modalities and Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02915)
*Amogh Vinaykumar,Aiden Li,Suozhi Huang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Any2Poster Bench——一个面向任意来源海报生成的多模态、多领域基准测试，以及配套的端到端参考智能体Any2Poster Agent，用于解析异构源、规划布局、渲染并迭代优化海报，在信息保真度和视觉传达两方面均实现了可复现的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海报生成的评估方法存在显著局限：仅限于论文输入、狭窄领域或表层视觉相似度度量，难以全面衡量自动海报生成的真实进展。因此需要一个跨多种输入模态、多种内容领域、同时评估信息保真度和视觉质量的统一基准。

Method: 1) 构建Any2Poster Bench：覆盖8种输入模态（PDF、URL、PPTX、DOCX、Markdown、LaTeX、Notebook、视频）和5个内容领域，采用基于quiz的事实保留与理解探针，以及基于VLM的视觉质量、布局、可读性、内容完整性和逻辑流判断进行评估；2) 提出Any2Poster Agent：端到端参考智能体，解析异构源→组织关键内容→规划海报布局→渲染海报→利用视觉反馈迭代优化。

Result: Any2Poster Agent在Any2Poster Bench上跨输入模态平均准确率达87.25%，跨内容域达87.28%。在PaperQuiz式评估中，相比PosterAgent-4o，总体准确率从51.06–51.33%提升至72.58%，密度增强得分从116–121提升至145.16。

Conclusion: Any2Poster Bench和Any2Poster Agent共同提供了一个可复用的评估资源和具有竞争力的基线，为研究多模态、领域通用的海报生成提供了标准化平台，推动了该领域的系统性进展。

Abstract: Visual posters are a compact medium for communicating dense information, yet progress on automatic poster generation remains difficult to measure because existing evaluations are often restricted to paper-only inputs, narrow domains, or surface-level visual similarity. We introduce Any2Poster Bench, a benchmark for any-source poster generation that evaluates systems across eight input modalities--PDFs, URLs, PPTX, DOCX, Markdown, LaTeX, notebooks, and videos--and five content domains. Any2Poster Bench pairs each source with quiz-based probes of verbatim factual retention and interpretive understanding, together with VLM-based judgments of visual quality, layout, readability, content completeness, and logical flow, enabling reproducible assessment of both information fidelity and visual communication. To instantiate and validate this benchmark, we further present Any2Poster Agent, an end-to-end reference agent that parses heterogeneous sources, organizes salient content, plans poster layouts, renders posters, and iteratively refines them using visual feedback. On Any2Poster Bench, Any2Poster Agent achieves 87.25% average accuracy across input modalities and 87.28% across content domains. On PaperQuiz-style evaluation, where prior paper-to-poster agents are directly comparable, Any2Poster Agent improves over PosterAgent-4o from 51.06-51.33% to 72.58% overall accuracy and from 116-121 to 145.16 in density-augmented score. Together, Any2Poster Bench and Any2Poster Agent provide a reusable evaluation resource and a competitive baseline for studying multimodal, domain-general poster generation.

</details>


### [3] [ATLAS: A Large-Scale Evaluation Benchmark for Adversarial LiDAR Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02924)
*Mellon M. Zhang,Siddhant Panse,Zimo Fan,Akshal Dhal,Rishit Sarkar,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATLAS是首个大规模、基于物理的黑盒LiDAR感知攻击评估基准，揭示标准基准表现优异的模型在点注入攻击下反而比弱模型更脆弱，且该脆弱性源于训练中的标准采样增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR评估仅使用干净基准数据，无法覆盖真实世界中外部实体通过物理操纵传感器诱导的黑盒感知失效。先前研究多关注硬件、几何和算法防御及早期检测器，现代感知系统的鲁棒性仍未被探索。

Method: ATLAS（Adversarial Temporal LiDAR Attack Suite）是首个大规模物理真实LiDAR感知基准，模拟点注入和点移除两种主要攻击模式，覆盖真实驾驶序列，并提供可扩展的生成代码支持可重复评估。

Result: 发现鲁棒性不对称：标准基准表现强的模型更抗点移除攻击，却对点注入攻击比弱模型更脆弱。该脆弱性可追溯到训练中的标准对象数据库采样增强，导致架构无关的鲁棒性失效。

Conclusion: 当前训练实践会引发架构无关的鲁棒性失效，未来LiDAR感知开发需明确将黑盒传感器鲁棒性作为考量因素，并研究两种攻击模式的缓解方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving perception is typically evaluated on clean benchmark data, yet real-world deployment requires robustness to rare, structured, and potentially adversarial sensor anomalies. This gap is especially critical for LiDAR, where external actors can physically manipulate the sensing process to induce black-box perception failures without accessing the model. Existing LiDAR benchmarks provide little visibility into this failure mode. Prior adversarial LiDAR studies have largely centered on attack hardware, geometric and algorithmic defenses, and early-generation detectors, leaving the robustness of modern perception systems unexplored. To address this evaluation gap, we introduce ATLAS (Adversarial Temporal LiDAR Attack Suite), the first large-scale, physically grounded evaluation benchmark for LiDAR perception models under black-box sensor attacks, simulating the two primary attack modes -- point injection and point removal -- across real driving sequences. Evaluating a broad cross-section of current state-of-the-art LiDAR perception models, ATLAS reveals a surprising robustness asymmetry: models with stronger performance on standard benchmarks tend to better withstand removal attacks, yet are actually more vulnerable to injection attacks than weaker models. We trace this vulnerability to standard object database sampling augmentations, revealing how current training practices can induce architecture-agnostic robustness failures, and study initial directions for mitigating both attack modes. We release the ATLAS generation code to support extensible, reproducible evaluations as attack capabilities evolve, helping make black-box sensor robustness an explicit consideration in future LiDAR perception development.

</details>


### [4] [The Road Ahead in Autonomous Driving: The KITScenes Multimodal Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02956)
*Richard Schwarzkopf,Fabian Immel,Alexander Blumberg,Jonas Merkert,Nils Rack,Kaiwen Wang,Fabian Konstantinidis,Julian Truetsch,Carlos Fernandez,Annika Bätz,Kevin Rösch,Marlon Steiner,Willi Poh,Yinzhe Shen,Royden Wagner,Felix Hauser,Dominik Strutz,Jaime Villa,Gleb Stepanov,Holger Caesar,Ömer Şahin Taş,Frank Bieder,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KITScenes Multimodal是一个欧洲自动驾驶数据集，包含高保真传感器（高分辨率相机、远距离激光雷达、4D成像雷达）和最完整的HD地图，提供在线HD地图构建、长距离深度估计、新视角合成和端到端驾驶四个基准测试，覆盖街道布局不规则和混合交通模式的欧洲城市。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集在传感器保真度、地图完整性或地理多样性方面存在不足，限制了embodied AI的空间学习进展。

Method: 构建了同步传感器套件（高分辨率全局快门相机、超400米远距离激光雷达、4D成像雷达、冗余GNSS/INS定位）和最完整的HD地图（所有交通元素三维映射，包含拓扑连接性）。数据集在街道布局不规则和混合交通模式的欧洲城市中录制。

Result: 实现了四项基准测试（在线HD地图构建、长距离深度估计、新视角合成、端到端驾驶），通过开源软件自动驾驶试验验证了HD地图的完整性。

Conclusion: 该数据集补充了现有数据集，显著扩大了地理多样性，为embodied AI的空间学习提供了新的研究机会。

Abstract: Existing autonomous driving datasets have enabled major progress, but fall short in sensor fidelity, map completeness, or geographic diversity. We present KITScenes Multimodal, a European dataset built around high-fidelity sensors and maps. Our fully synchronized sensor suite combines high-resolution global-shutter cameras, long-range lidar beyond 400m, 4D imaging radar, and redundant GNSS/INS localization. Our HD maps are, to our knowledge, the most complete of any sensor dataset, validated through autonomous driving trials on open-source software. For the first time in a public dataset, all driving-relevant traffic elements, such as traffic lights, are mapped in 3D to a reprojection-accurate level with full topological connectivity. Recorded in cities with irregular street layouts and mixed traffic modes, our dataset complements existing datasets by broadening the available geographic diversity. We also introduce four benchmarks, each advancing spatial learning for embodied AI: online HD map construction, long-range depth estimation, novel view synthesis, and end-to-end driving. Project page: https://kitscenes.com/

</details>


### [5] [Towards Compact Autonomous Driving Perception with Balanced Learning and Multi-sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02979)
*Oskar Natan,Jun Miura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧凑型深度多任务学习模型，用于在单次前向传播中同时完成自动驾驶感知任务（语义分割、深度估计、LiDAR分割、鸟瞰图投影），通过自适应损失权重算法解决任务不平衡问题，并融合RGB相机、动态视觉传感器和LiDAR的多模态输入，在参数更少的情况下实现更好的性能和更快的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知需要处理多种任务（语义分割、深度估计、LiDAR分割、鸟瞰图投影等），传统方法通常需要多个独立模型，存在参数冗余、计算效率低、GPU内存占用大等问题。此外，多任务学习时各任务的学习难度和重要性不同，导致训练过程中出现任务不平衡问题，影响模型性能。

Method: 1. 设计紧凑型深度多任务学习模型，在单次前向传播中同时完成多种感知任务；2. 提出自适应损失权重算法，根据各任务的学习状态动态调整损失权重，解决任务不平衡问题；3. 采用数据预处理和中间传感器融合技术，整合来自RGB相机、动态视觉传感器（DVS）和多位置LiDAR的多模态输入；4. 通过消融研究验证模型各组件的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该模型在CARLA模拟数据集（3个）和nuScenes-lidarseg真实数据集（1个）上均保持一致的性能；相比近期模型组合，在参数更少的情况下实现了更好的性能；推理速度更快，GPU内存利用率更低；模型各组件的消融研究证实了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了紧凑型多任务学习模型在自动驾驶感知领域的有效性，能够在减少参数和计算资源的同时保持或提升性能，为未来自动驾驶感知系统的设计提供了新的思路和实现方案。开源代码供研究社区使用。

Abstract: We present a novel compact deep multi-task learning model to handle various autonomous driving perception tasks in one forward pass. The model performs multiple views of semantic segmentation, depth estimation, light detection and ranging (LiDAR) segmentation, and bird's eye view projection simultaneously without being supported by other models. We also provide an adaptive loss weighting algorithm to tackle the imbalanced learning issue that occurred due to plenty of given tasks. Through data pre-processing and intermediate sensor fusion techniques, the model can process and combine multiple input modalities retrieved from RGB cameras, dynamic vision sensors (DVS), and LiDAR placed at several positions on the ego vehicle. Therefore, a better understanding of a dynamically changing environment can be achieved. Based on the ablation study, the model variant trained with our proposed method achieves a better performance. Furthermore, a comparative study is also conducted to clarify its performance and effectiveness against the combination of some recent models. As a result, our model maintains better performance even with much fewer parameters. Hence, the model can inference faster with less GPU memory utilization. Moreover, the result tends to be consistent in 3 different CARLA simulation datasets and 1 real-world nuScenes-lidarseg dataset. To support future research, we share codes and other files publicly at https://github.com/oskarnatan/compact-perception.

</details>


### [6] [MUSE: A Unified Agentic Harness for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03005)
*Jianglin Lu,Hailing Wang,Xu Ma,Qihua Dong,Mingyuan Zhang,Yizhou Wang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MUSE是一种无需重新训练的执行框架，通过改进模型周围的执行架构来增强冻结的MLLM的能力，在多个视觉任务基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLMs取得了快速进展，但在人类能轻松完成的任务（如网格迷宫导航、拼图选择）上仍表现不佳。研究提出一个互补问题：在不重新训练模型的前提下，通过改进模型周围的执行架构，能从冻结的MLLM中激发多少能力？

Method: 提出MUSE（Multimodal Unified Structured Execution）框架，它封装了任何现成的MLLM，包含可组合的模块：任务表示、视觉处理、感知工具使用、结构化解析、确定性验证和验证器引导修复，完全无需模型重新训练。

Result: 在涵盖视觉空间规划、视觉感知、多模态推理和细粒度视觉区分的多样化基准测试中，MUSE在所有场景下都为裸模型提供了持续提升，特别是在具有挑战性的实例上提升最大。进一步分析发现，许多MLLM失败源于架构层缺陷而非模型本身缺陷，可通过验证器引导修复解决。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了“代理多模态执行架构”作为关键但被忽视的设计维度的重要性，提出了超越以模型为中心优化的正交改进路径。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still fail on tasks that humans solve effortlessly, such as navigating a grid maze from a screenshot or selecting the correct puzzle piece. Rather than retraining the model, we ask a complementary question: how much capability can be elicited from a frozen MLLM purely by improving the execution scaffold around it? We introduce MUSE, a multimodal unified structured execution harness that wraps any off-the-shelf MLLM with composable modules for task representation, visual processing, perception tool use, structured parsing, deterministic verification, and verifier-guided repair, without any model retraining. We evaluate MUSE across diverse benchmarks spanning visual spatial planning, visual perception, multimodal reasoning, and fine-grained visual discrimination, using multiple state-of-the-art MLLMs. MUSE delivers consistent gains over the bare model in all settings, with the largest jumps on challenging instances. Further analysis reveals that many MLLM failures arise from harness-level shortcomings rather than fundamental model deficits, and can be addressed through verifier-guided repair without touching the model. These findings highlight the agentic multimodal harness as a critical yet underexplored design dimension, offering an orthogonal avenue for improving MLLMs beyond model-centric optimization.

</details>


### [7] [NVIDIA OmniDreams: Real-Time Generative World Model for Closed-Loop Autonomous Vehicle Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03159)
*NVIDIA,:,Aarti Basant,Amlan Kar,Despoina Paschalidou,Fangyin Wei,Francesco Ferroni,Guillermo Garcia Cobo,Haithem Turki,Huan Ling,Jaewoo Seo,James Lucas,Jay Zhangjie Wu,Jialiang Wang,Jonathan Lorraine,Jun Gao,Kai He,Katarina Tothova,Kevin Xie,Michał Tyszkiewicz,Qi Wu,Riccardo de Lutio,Ruilong Li,Sanja Fidler,Seung Wook Kim,Tianchang Shen,Tianshi Cao,Tobias Pfaff,William Lew,Xindi Wu,Xuanchi Ren,Yifan Lu,Yuxuan Zhang,Zan Gojcic,Zian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniDreams，一个基于Cosmos扩散模型进行中期和后训练的基础生成式世界模型，能够自回归地生成动作条件下的实时驾驶视频，用于自动驾驶策略的安全闭环评估与训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶能力的提升，长尾场景下驾驶策略的安全评估成为关键瓶颈。现有的基于重建的神经模拟器虽然具备照片级真实感，但受限于初始采集数据，难以泛化到高度动态或新颖的场景（如极端天气、不可预测的动态智能体行为）。

Method: OmniDreams基于Cosmos扩散模型进行中期和后训练，利用21k小时的驾驶场景数据，自回归地根据历史帧、当前模拟器状态和即时驾驶动作生成照片级真实感的传感器观测视频。该模型部署于闭环系统中，与Alpamayo 1策略模型和AlpaSim编排器配合工作。此外，还从OmniDreams后训练了一个世界-动作模型（WAM）。

Result: OmniDreams能够合成传统模拟器难以捕捉的复杂未观测现象（极端天气、不可预测的动态行为）。基于OmniDreams后训练的WAM在Physical AI自动驾驶NuRec数据集上表现优异，超越了基于VLA的Alpamayo 1.5研究策略模型，且仅使用1/5的参数量。

Conclusion: OmniDreams作为一个实时生成式世界模型，不仅可作为高响应性的闭环仿真环境用于自动驾驶策略的训练与评估，还具备作为策略架构骨干网络的潜力，为下一代自动驾驶提供了可扩展且全面的解决方案。

Abstract: As autonomous vehicle capabilities advance, the safe evaluation of driving policies in long-tail scenarios remains a critical bottleneck. In closed-loop simulation, the driving policy model actively interacts with the environment, where its actions dynamically update the simulator state and directly influence the next set of generated sensor observations. While recent reconstruction-based neural simulators offer photorealism, they are fundamentally constrained by their initial captured data and struggle to generalize to highly dynamic or novel scenes. To overcome these limitations, we introduce OmniDreams, a foundation generative world model mid- and post-trained from the Cosmos diffusion model to autoregressively generate action-conditioned videos in real time. By leveraging the rich visual priors of Cosmos and mid- and post-training on 21k hours of driving scenarios, OmniDreams synthesizes complex, unobserved phenomena that are hard for traditional simulators to capture, such as extreme weather and unpredictable dynamic agent behaviors. Crucially, it autoregressively conditions its photorealistic sensor generation on past frames, the current simulator state, and immediate driving actions. Deployed in a closed-loop system with the Alpamayo 1 policy model and AlpaSim orchestrator, OmniDreams acts as a highly responsive, reactive environment, providing a scalable and comprehensive solution for training and evaluating next-generation autonomous driving policies. We additionally show preliminary results indicating that a world-action model (WAM) post-trained from OmniDreams achieves strong performance on the Physical AI Autonomous Vehicles NuRec dataset, surpassing the VLA-based Alpamayo 1.5 research policy model while using only 1/5 the total parameters. These results highlight the potential for a real-time world model like OmniDreams to also serve as a backbone for policy architectures.

</details>


### [8] [SRENet: Spectral Re-Entry Network for Point Cloud Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03160)
*Qiuxia Wu,Jiarui Lan,Wenxiong Kang,Zhiyong Wang,Kun Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SRENet，一个基于频谱感知的框架，通过小波分解显式学习点云序列中动作的全局运动上下文和细粒度时序动态，在MSR-Action3D、NTU-RGBD和NTU-RGBD120三个数据集上达到了最先进的动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云序列的 Human Action Recognition 面临两大挑战：点云的不规则结构和时序不一致性使得时空表征学习困难，尤其难以同时捕获全局运动上下文和细粒度时序动态。现有方法缺乏从频率角度对运动信息进行显式建模的能力。

Method: SRENet包含三个核心设计：(1) Spectral Decomposition Block (SDeBlock)：沿时间和空间轴进行小波分析，将特征解耦为低频和高频分量，并施加频率特定的注意力机制；(2) Spectral Re-entry Block (SReBlock)：进行二次时序分解，恢复残余动态并重新对齐在语义融合过程中被扭曲的时序频率结构；(3) 频谱感知学习策略：通过对比损失和课程学习调度（从低频到高频逐步过渡，对应从粗略到精细的运动模式）增强频率子空间的判别能力。

Result: 在MSR-Action3D、NTU-RGBD和NTU-RGBD120三个基准数据集上进行了大量实验，SRENet取得了最先进的性能，验证了频率建模在点云动作理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: 从频率视角对点云序列进行显式建模是有效的。SRENet通过小波分解将运动信息解耦为不同频率成分，并结合频谱感知学习策略，能够同时捕获全局运动上下文和细粒度时序动态，显著提升了点云动作识别的性能。

Abstract: Recognizing human actions from point cloud sequences is critical for 3D perception driven applications such as autonomous driving and human-computer interaction. However, the irregular structure and temporal inconsistency of point clouds pose unique challenges for spatio-temporal representation learning, especially in capturing both global motion context and fine-grained temporal dynamics. We propose SRENet, a spectral-aware framework designed to explicitly learn both global context and fine-grained temporal dynamics of motion from a frequency perspective for action recognition. SRENet introduces a Spectral Decomposition Block (SDeBlock) that performs wavelet-based analysis along temporal and spatial axes, disentangling features into low- and high-frequency components with frequency-specific attention. To recover residual dynamics and re-align temporal frequency structures distorted during semantic fusion, a Spectral Re-entry Block (SReBlock) performs secondary temporal decomposition. Furthermore, a spectral-aware learning strategy is devised to enhance discriminability in both frequency subspaces via contrastive loss and a curriculum schedule that gradually shifts focus from low- to high-frequency spaces in line with coarse to detailed motion patterns. Extensive experiments on MSR-Action3D, NTU-RGBD and NTU-RGBD120 demonstrate that SRENet achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating the effectiveness of frequency modeling in point cloud-based action understanding.

</details>


### [9] [Ask When It Pays: Cost-Aware Open-Ended Interaction for Instance Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03175)
*Xunyi Zhao,Sihao Lin,Gengze Zhou,Zerui Li,Shijie Li,Wei Tao,Jiajun Liu,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将交互式实例目标导航（IGN）重新定义为代价敏感的不确定性缩减问题，提出基于信息增益的问题分类法与权重，构建了评估交互效率的新基准和加权成功率指标，并设计了零样本多模态大语言模型导航器，仅在预期不确定性缩减值得交互代价时才发起查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式实例目标导航方法允许智能体向预言机（oracle）提问以消除歧义，但未区分轻量级澄清与路线级指导，导致智能体通过反复高信息量提问提高成功率，而非高效地解决核心歧义。此外，现有基准未建模不同问题类型的代价，也未评估智能体使用交互的效率，无法支持代价敏感交互的研究。

Method: （1）将交互式IGN重新建模为代价敏感的不确定性缩减问题，目标是选择信息增益最大、代价最小的问题；（2）对现有导航语料进行信息增益分析，识别能降低导航不确定性的线索，得到紧凑的问题类型集合及数据驱动的权重；（3）基于该分类法构建诊断交互行为与效率的基准，并设计加权成功率（Weighted Success Rate）指标，按问题代价惩罚每次查询；（4）提出零样本多模态大语言模型（MLLM）导航器，在每个决策步骤选择性查询，仅当预期不确定性缩减超过交互代价时才提问。

Result: 构建了一个代价敏感交互导航基准及配套评估指标，使交互效率可被量化诊断；提出的零样本MLLM导航器能够在交互代价与不确定性缩减之间做出理性权衡，选择性提问而非过度依赖预言机。

Conclusion: 交互式实例目标导航应从"多提问提高成功率"转向"高效、代价敏感地消除不确定性"。本文提出的问题分类法、评估基准、加权成功率指标及选择性查询导航器，共同为研究和评估代价敏感的交互导航行为提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: Instance Goal Navigation (IGN) requires an embodied agent to find a specific object instance among distractors from an underspecified natural-language description. Such ambiguity often cannot be resolved from perception and language alone, making interaction with an oracle a natural mechanism for disambiguation. Prior interactive methods allow oracle queries but treat lightweight clarification and route-level guidance alike, letting agents boost success rate through repeated high-information questions rather than by resolving the underlying ambiguity efficiently. We recast interactive IGN as a cost-sensitive uncertainty-reduction problem, where the agent should ask the question whose answer provides the largest reduction in navigation uncertainty relative to its penalty. To this end, we apply an information-gain analysis on existing navigation corpora to identify which cues reduce navigation uncertainty, yielding a compact set of question types and data-derived weights.However, existing interactive navigation benchmarks do not model the cost of different question types or evaluate how efficiently agents use interaction, making them unsuitable for studying cost-sensitive interaction. Based on this taxonomy, we construct a benchmark for diagnosing interaction behavior and efficiency, together with a Weighted Success Rate metric that penalizes each query by its derived cost. We further propose a zero-shot MLLM navigator that selectively queries at each decision step only when the expected uncertainty reduction justifies the interaction cost.

</details>


### [10] [MemoGen: Can Past Experience Improve Future Text-to-Image Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03243)
*Wenshuo Chen,Kuimou Yu,Bowen Tian,Jianfei Song,Shaofeng Liang,Haozhe Jia,Kan Cheng,Haosen Li,Kaishen Yuan,Lei Wang,Jiemin Wu,Songning Lai,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MemoGen，一个无需训练的框架，通过为现有文生图模型添加智能体演化层，将生成经验（任务理解、参考选择、视觉反馈、成功策略和失败教训）存储为可复用的经验记忆，使系统能够在不更新底层生成器参数的情况下，通过多轮演化持续改进生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代文生图模型在处理需要隐式视觉约束、关系推理或外部知识的提示词时仍然不可靠。现有的检索增强和智能体生成方法虽然通过获取外部知识来缓解这一问题，但将每次生成视为孤立事件，未能系统性地保存过去的成功或失败经验以供未来使用。

Method: 提出MemoGen框架，包含以下步骤：(1) 显式推断视觉需求；(2) 在必要时检索外部证据和参考；(3) 将其转化为可执行的生成约束；(4) 评估生成结果；(5) 将任务理解、参考选择、视觉反馈、成功策略和失败教训存储为经验记忆。在演化轮次间，智能体检索相关经验以改进未来相似生成任务，选择性修复失败案例同时保留成功案例，实现无需参数更新的测试时自我演化。

Result: 在知识密集型和推理导向的基准测试上进行了广泛实验。仅经过两轮演化，基于开源Qwen-Image骨干网络的MemoGen在WISE和Mind-Bench基准上超越了Nano Banana Pro和GPT-Image-1等强商业系统，证明了显式经验记忆可以作为可靠的文生图持续学习信号。

Conclusion: 显式经验记忆可以作为文生图系统中强大的持续学习信号，无需更新生成器参数即可实现测试时自我演化，显著提升了生成可靠性，甚至使开源模型能够超越商业闭源系统。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image models have achieved strong visual synthesis, yet remain unreliable when prompts require implicit visual constraints, relational reasoning, or external knowledge. Existing retrieval-augmented and agentic generation methods mitigate this issue by acquiring external knowledge, references, or refined prompts for the current request, yet they typically treat each generation as an isolated episode and do not systematically preserve past successes or failures for future use. In this work, we ask whether a text-to-image system can continually improve from its own generation experience without updating the underlying generator. We propose MemoGen, a training-free framework that augments existing image generators with an agentic evolution layer. For each task, MemoGen explicitly infers visual requirements, retrieves external evidence and references when necessary, translates them into executable generation constraints, evaluates the generated result, and stores task understanding, reference choices, visual feedback, successful strategies, and failure lessons as reusable experience memory. Across evolution rounds, the agent retrieves relevant experience to improve similar future generations, selectively repairing previously failed cases while preserving successful ones, thereby enabling test-time self-evolution without parameter updates. Extensive experiments on knowledge-intensive and reasoning-oriented benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of this paradigm: after only two evolution rounds, MemoGen built upon the open-source Qwen-Image backbone surpasses strong proprietary systems such as Nano Banana Pro and GPT-Image-1 on WISE and Mind-Bench, showing that explicit experience memory can serve as a powerful continual learning signal for reliable text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [11] [TASE: Truncation-Aware Semantic Embeddings for 3D Scene Understanding and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03314)
*Tim-Felix Faasch,Jochen Kall,Lucas Nunes,Jens Behley,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TASE是一种基于预训练2D语义特征的可控3D场景编辑方法，通过构建截断感知嵌入空间，实现对3D场景的文本驱动编辑，显著提升了大几何修改场景下的编辑质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真语义3D场景表示在机器人、自动驾驶和仿真等领域至关重要，现有的可控编辑方法支持有限，无法满足开发者针对特定场景灵活调整的需求。

Method: 将预训练的2D语义特征投影到截断感知嵌入空间，优化特征空间使得减少通道数获得更抽象表示、保留通道数保持细粒度细节；使用尺度和平移等变性损失改善多视图一致性；提出编辑扩散模型的微调阶段以缓解几何变化导致的伪影。

Result: 实现了对3D场景的文本驱动编辑，提供对编辑强度的显式控制，能够进行比先前方法更大幅度的修改；在涉及大几何修改的编辑任务上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TASE通过构建截断感知嵌入空间，使3D场景编辑更加灵活可控，特别是在大几何修改场景中表现出色，为开发者在特定目标场景中调整应用提供了有效工具。

Abstract: High-fidelity semantic 3D scene representations are crucial for numerous applications, including robotics, autonomous driving, and simulation. Beyond this, the ability to edit such representations enables developers to adapt these applications more easily to specific target scenarios. Current approaches provide limited support for controllable editing. We introduce TASE, a method that projects pretrained 2D semantic features into a truncation-aware embedding space to enable flexible 3D scene editing. Our method explicitly optimizes a feature space in which progressively reducing feature channels yields increasingly abstract semantic representations, while retaining more channels preserves fine-grained detail. Additionally, we improve multi-view consistency of the features using a scale- and translation-equivariance loss. The resulting truncation-aware embedding space enables text-driven edits to 3D scenes, providing explicit control over how strongly edits adhere to the original scene content and allowing more substantial modifications than prior methods. Moreover, we propose a finetuning stage for the editing diffusion model to mitigate artifacts caused by geometric changes. Experimental results demonstrate competitive performance in 3D scene editing, substantially outperforming prior methods on edits involving large geometric modifications.

</details>


### [12] [Enginuity: A Dataset and Benchmark for Vision-Language Understanding of Engineering Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03410)
*Abhishek Kumar,Isha Motiyani,Tilak Kasturi,Ethan Seefried,Prahitha Movva,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文介绍了Enginuity，首个开放数据集和基准，用于评估VLM在工程图上的能力，包含结构化零件表提取和自由形式VQA两个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程图通过密集空间布局、领域符号和交叉引用编码信息，是VLM的独特挑战；现有数据集多关注流程图/科学图表/商业文档，缺乏工程图领域的公共基准。

Method: 在U.S.军事服务手册语料库上定义两个任务：任务1（结构化零件表提取）、任务2（自由形式视觉图表问答VQA）。评估4个前沿VLM（GPT-5.2 Chat、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemma 4、Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct），使用零样本和思维链提示。

Result: 任务1：Recall@all达0.61-0.87，但Token F1pen仅0.03-0.18，暴露零件识别与描述保真度差距；任务2：所有模型在事实推理上存在一致差距；分析显示token重叠指标低估技术描述能力2-6倍。

Conclusion: 论文发布数据集、注释、评估工具和每样本模型输出，支持对VLM工程内容能力的可重复研究，并建议采用LLM-as-judge进行领域特定评估。

Abstract: Engineering diagrams pose a distinct challenge for vision-language models: unlike natural images or general documents, they encode information through dense spatial layouts, domain-specific symbols, and cross-references between visual callouts and structured parts tables. Despite their centrality to service, repair, and design workflows, there is no public benchmark for measuring VLM capabilities in this domain; existing datasets primarily focus on flowcharts, scientific figures, or business documents. To address this gap, we introduce Enginuity, the first open dataset and benchmark for evaluating VLMs on complex engineering diagrams. We define two tasks over a corpus of U.S. military service and repair manuals: structured parts-table extraction (Task 1) and free-form visual diagram question answering (VQA)(Task 2) for benchmarking. We evaluate four frontier VLMs (GPT-5.2 Chat, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemma 4, Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct) under zero-shot and chain-of-thought prompting. On Task 1, models reach Recall@all of 0.61-0.87 but Token F1pen of only 0.03-0.18, exposing a systematic gap between part identification and description fidelity. Task 2 reveals a consistent factual-reasoning gap across all models. A supporting analysis shows that token-overlap metrics under-report model capability on technical descriptions by 2-6x relative to semantic similarity, motivating LLM-as-judge calibration for domain-specific evaluation. We release the dataset, annotations, evaluation harness, and per-sample model outputs to support a reproducible study of VLM capability on engineering content.

</details>


### [13] [From 3D Perception to Safety Reasoning: A Graph-Based Framework for Real-Time Underground Mine Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03460)
*Pasindu Ranasinghe,Simit Raval,Dibyayan Patra,Bikram Banerjee,Ismet Canbulat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向地下煤矿的连续监测框架，通过3D语义感知、不确定性异常检测、规则检查、设备端LLM推理和GraphRAG记忆分析，将彩色3D点云转换为结构化安全推理输出，以识别即时危险并解释长期安全模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下煤矿作业空间狭窄、照明差，存在设备违规、结构不稳定和盲区遮挡等难以预测的危险。传统监控系统（固定摄像头和基于规则的邻近性警报）缺乏3D场景理解和上下文记忆，无法识别复杂或演化的危险。

Method: 该框架将彩色3D点云转换为结构化安全推理输出，融合3D语义感知、不确定性异常检测、规则危险检查、设备端LLM推理和基于GraphRAG的记忆分析。场景和时间图作为显式知识结构，连接感知输出。利用真实巷道扫描、受控物体放置和高保真长壁模拟生成多样化危险场景，采用自监督预训练解决标注数据稀缺问题。

Result: 感知模型达到92.7%准确率，30 FPS，低内存占用。规则检查覆盖率为57%，结合上下文LLM推理提升至76%，结合基于记忆的推理提升至93%。不确定性导出的异常信号支持对超出预定义类别的分布外危险的解释。

Conclusion: 基于图的知识表示与3D感知和分层安全推理相结合，为地下矿山监测的智能决策支持提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Underground coal mining requires personnel and heavy equipment to operate within shared, confined, and poorly illuminated spaces where hazards such as equipment proximity violations, structural instabilities, and occluded blind spots are difficult to anticipate. Conventional monitoring systems, including fixed cameras and rule-based proximity alerts, can detect predefined events but lack the 3D scene understanding and contextual memory needed to identify complex or evolving hazards. This paper presents a continuous monitoring framework that converts colourised 3D point clouds into structured and traceable safety reasoning outputs. The framework combines 3D semantic perception, uncertainty-based anomaly detection, rule-based hazard checks, on-device LLM reasoning, and GraphRAG -based memory analysis to identify immediate hazards and interpret longer-term safety patterns. Scene and temporal graphs serve as the explicit knowledge structure, linking perception outputs across reasoning stages. To overcome the scarcity of labeled underground data, real roadway scans, controlled object placement, and high-fidelity longwall simulation were combined to generate diverse hazard scenarios, while self-supervised pretraining improved segmentation from limited annotations. The perception model achieved 92.7% accuracy at 30 FPS with low memory usage. Across 115 hazard scenarios, rule-based checks achieved 57% coverage, increasing to 76% with contextual LLM reasoning and 93% with memory-based reasoning using historical records. Qualitative results show uncertainty-derived anomaly signals support the interpretation of out-of-distribution hazards beyond predefined classes. Overall, graph-based knowledge representation combined with 3D perception and layered safety reasoning provides a practical foundation for intelligent decision support in underground mine monitoring.

</details>


### [14] [UnsOcc: 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction in Unstructured Scene via Rendering Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03581)
*Ye Wu,Ruiqi Song,Baiyong Ding,Nanxin Zeng,Junjie Cheng,Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UnsOcc，一个针对非结构化场景（如露天矿场）的多模态3D语义占用预测框架，通过基于渲染的跨模态融合模块（RenderFusion）和基于高斯泼溅的细节感知辅助监督方法（GSRefinement），有效解决了非结构化场景中场景稀疏和长尾分布问题，在自建露天矿数据集和nuScenes数据集上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化场景（如露天矿场）中存在不规则障碍物和稀疏的场景布局，使传统的3D目标检测等感知方法失效。虽然3D语义占用预测能提供稠密的空间表示，但直接应用于非结构化场景仍面临两大挑战：(1) 场景稀疏性阻碍了有效的跨模态融合；(2) 更为严重的长尾分布进一步降低了预测性能。

Method: 提出UnsOcc框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) RenderFusion——基于渲染的融合模块，通过双向渲染监督增强跨模态特征对齐；(2) GSRefinement——基于高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）的细节感知辅助监督方法，将稀疏的3D占用预测投影到稠密的2D语义分割图，从而对长尾类别实现有效监督。此外，还构建了一个专门的露天矿非结构化场景数据集。

Result: 在自建的露天矿数据集和公开的nuScenes数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法显著优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: UnsOcc框架通过渲染驱动的跨模态融合和高斯泼溅辅助监督，有效提升了非结构化场景下3D语义占用预测的鲁棒性，为自动驾驶在复杂非结构化环境中的感知提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Unstructured scenes present unique challenges for autonomous driving, as irregular obstacles and sparse scene layouts undermine the effectiveness of traditional perception methods such as 3D object detection. 3D semantic occupancy prediction has emerged as a prominent focus due to its ability to provide dense spatial representations by assigning semantic labels to individual voxels in 3D space. However, directly applying 3D semantic occupancy prediction to unstructured scenes remains challenging because scene sparsity hinders effective cross-modal fusion and the more severe long-tail distribution in these scenarios further degrades prediction performance. To validate the effectiveness of our approach, we construct a dedicated dataset of unstructured scenes collected from open-pit mines. Based on this, we propose UnsOcc, a multi-modal 3D semantic occupancy prediction framework that improves robustness in unstructured environments. At its core, we introduce a rendering-based fusion module, RenderFusion, which enhances cross-modal feature alignment through bidirectional rendering supervision. Furthermore, we propose GSRefinement, a detail-aware auxiliary supervision method based on Gaussian Splatting that projects sparse 3D occupancy predictions into dense 2D semantic segmentation maps, enabling effective supervision for long-tail categories. Extensive experiments on both the open-pit mine dataset and the nuScenes dataset demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [15] [\textsc{CR-Seg}: Attention-Guided and CoT-Enhanced Coarse-to-Refined Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03564)
*Yifan Cao,Xiaocui Yang,Faxian Wan,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CR-Seg的粗到细推理分割框架，通过注意力引导和全局到局部链式思维来提升复杂语言描述目标的分割效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割方法在跨模态对齐或空间提示中存在局限，导致分割效果不佳。

Method: 设计了EAP模块提取注意力图和点，结合SAM进行掩码细化，并引入全局到局部链式思维（GLCoT）缓解推理与答案不一致问题。

Result: 在推理分割基准测试中验证了CR-Seg的有效性。

Conclusion: CR-Seg通过两阶段粗到细推理显著提升了复杂语言描述目标的分割性能。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation aims to segment target objects described by complex language through joint visual-textual reasoning. Existing methods typically rely on either learned semantic tokens to bridge Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and segmentation models, suffering from difficult cross-modal alignment, or explicit spatial prompts such as bounding boxes, which may lose holistic response semantics. To address these limitations, we propose Attention-Guided and CoT-Enhanced Coarse-to-Refined Reasoning Segmentation, termed CR-Seg, a two-stage framework for coarse-to-refined reasoning segmentation. Specifically, we design an Extract Attention Maps and Points (EAP) module to extract attention maps for coarse target localization and select informative points, both of which are fed into SAM for mask refinement. To alleviate reasoning--answer inconsistency, we further introduce Global-to-Local Chain-of-Thought (GLCoT), which guides the model to reason progressively from global scene context to local target details. Extensive experiments on reasoning segmentation benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of CR-Seg.

</details>


### [16] [SimuScene: Simulation-Ready Compositional 3D Scene Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03994)
*Inhee Lee,Sangwon Baik,Sungjoo Kim,Hyeonwoo Kim,Hyunsoo Cha,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimuScene是一个基于物理反馈的3D场景重建框架，通过在生成过程中引入物理引擎作为诊断工具，解决单图像重建中物体间穿透、悬浮和下沉问题，生成物理稳定的仿真场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像重建的3D场景在物理仿真中存在物体穿透、悬浮和下沉等问题，现有方法仅作为后处理布局修正，未解决几何误差根源。

Method: 将物理引擎作为生成过程中的诊断工具，通过重力模拟将穿透和支撑失败转化为定量校正信号，驱动重力轴拉伸和隐式形状重采样，形成物理反馈循环。

Result: 在物理稳定性和几何对齐基准上达到最先进性能，成功应用于类人控制和机械臂操作任务。

Conclusion: 物理信息反馈循环可有效缓解累积重建误差，生成物理稳定的组合式3D场景，验证了SimuScene在机器人操作中的实用价值。

Abstract: Reconstructing interactive, simulation-ready 3D scenes from a single image is a critical bottleneck for robotic manipulation. While recent single-image lifters recover plausible per-object shapes, composing them yields scenes that collapse under physical simulation due to interpenetrating, hovering, or sinking objects. Existing physics-aware methods address this strictly as a post-hoc layout correction, leaving the underlying geometric errors unresolved. To address this, we introduce SimuScene, a compositional 3D reconstruction pipeline that puts physics in the loop of shape and layout estimation. Rather than using physics merely for layout cleanup, we utilize the physics engine as a diagnostic measurement tool during the generative process itself. By diagnostically simulating reconstructed objects under gravity, we convert penetration and support failures into quantitative correction signals that drive gravity-axis stretching and amodal shape resampling. This physics-informed feedback loop mitigates accumulated reconstruction errors and produces a stable, simulation-ready compositional 3D scene. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on physical stability and geometric alignment benchmarks. We further highlight SimuScene's utility by deploying reconstructed environments in humanoid control and robot-arm manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [17] [Eliciting Complex Spatial Reasoning in MLLMs through Wide-Baseline Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03577)
*Hao Zhong,Muzhi Zhu,Shenyan Zeng,Anzhou Li,Cong Chen,Hua Geng,Duochao Shi,Wentao Ye,Tao Lin,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReasonMatch-Bench，一个针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在宽基线匹配（WBM）任务上的系统化评测基准，并提出了一种可扩展的数据生成管线和动态对应强化学习（DCRL）方法，显著提升了MLLM在空间推理和宽基线对应任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽基线匹配（WBM）需要整合几何理解、视角变化感知、细粒度感知和遮挡推理，是多模态大语言模型在物理环境中空间推理能力的挑战性测试。然而，当前MLLMs缺乏针对这些能力的系统化评测和训练框架。实验表明，现有最强基线在困难子集上仅达到37.2 F1，而人类标注者可达84.0 F1，差距巨大。

Method: 1) 提出ReasonMatch-Bench：按视角位移和匹配粒度分层，覆盖室内、室外和以物体为中心的场景的评测基准；2) 构建可扩展的数据生成管线，从大规模视频-3D语料（RGB-D视频和SfM重建）中自动提取宽基线视角对，提供多样且可验证的监督信号；3) 提出动态对应强化学习（DCRL），结合图像级视角渐进和点级对应课程学习，通过可验证奖励改进WBM训练，无需显式思维链（CoT）监督。

Result: DCRL在ReasonMatch-Bench上取得显著提升，并能迁移至相关空间推理基准；同时在多个通用视觉理解基准上保持性能，部分基准上还有适度提升。现有MLLMs在细粒度宽基线对应任务上仍远落后于人类水平（37.2 vs 84.0 F1）。

Conclusion: 本文填补了MLLMs在宽基线匹配任务上缺乏系统评测和训练框架的空白。通过ReasonMatch-Bench揭示了当前模型的不足，并通过可扩展数据生成管线和DCRL方法有效缩小了与人类表现的差距，同时具备良好的迁移能力和通用性能保持。

Abstract: Wide-baseline matching (WBM) requires integrating geometric understanding, viewpoint changes, fine-grained perception, and occlusion reasoning, making it a challenging testbed for spatial reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) deployed in physical environments. However, current MLLMs lack systematic evaluation and training frameworks for these capabilities. We introduce ReasonMatch-Bench, a benchmark stratified by viewpoint displacement and matching granularity across indoor, outdoor, and object-centric scenarios, and show that current MLLMs still struggle with fine-grained wide-baseline correspondence: on a difficult 90-sample subset, human annotators achieve 84.0 F1, while the best existing baseline reaches 37.2. To bridge this gap, we build a scalable data-generation pipeline that automatically extracts wide-baseline view pairs from large-scale video-3D corpora, including RGB-D videos and SfM reconstructions, yielding diverse and verifiable supervision. We further propose Dynamic Correspondence Reinforcement Learning (DCRL), which combines Image-Level Viewpoint Progression and Point-Level Correspondence Curriculum to improve WBM training through verifiable rewards without explicit CoT supervision. Extensive experiments show that DCRL substantially improves ReasonMatch-Bench and transfers to related spatial benchmarks, while maintaining general visual understanding performance with modest gains on several benchmarks.

</details>


### [18] [World Models Meet Language Models: On the Complementarity of Concrete and Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03603)
*Yucheng Zhou,Wei Tao,Yiwen Guo,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了"受控具体推理"（Controlled Concrete Reasoning）框架，将世界模型的视觉未来模拟能力与多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的抽象推理能力相结合，并提出了PF-OPSD（特权未来在线策略自蒸馏）训练方法，使模型学会在推理过程中调用、验证和整合视觉未来模拟，在两个新基准上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型能够生成具体的视觉未来推演，MLLM能够进行抽象推理，但两者各有局限：世界模型生成的推演是随机性的，可能视觉上合理但任务上不正确；MLLM缺乏对视觉未来的具体模拟能力。因此，如何判断视觉模拟何时有用、推演是否可信、以及如何将其整合到最终答案中，是一个亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 1）提出"受控具体推理"范式，模型学习调用、验证和整合视觉未来模拟与抽象推理；2）构建两个基准数据集——VRQABench（可控空间前瞻）和OpenWorldQA（开放域物理预测）；3）提出PF-OPSD训练方法：训练阶段使用真实未来视频和答案作为教师端的特权上下文，评估在线策略的具体推理轨迹，部署时的学生模型在测试时不观察真实未来。

Result: PF-OPSD在VRQABench和OpenWorldQA上分别比基线方法高出10.6%和10.9%，同时增强了对噪声或冲突推演的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型的视觉模拟与MLLM的抽象推理有机结合，并采用特权未来自蒸馏训练策略，可以有效提升模型对未来结果的预测和推理能力，同时保持对不可靠视觉推演的鲁棒性。

Abstract: World models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide complementary capabilities for predicting future outcomes from static visual observations. World models can generate concrete visual rollouts of possible futures, while MLLMs can reason abstractly over questions, goals, and rules. However, generated rollouts are stochastic and may be visually plausible but task-incorrect, making it necessary to determine when visual simulation is useful, whether a rollout is credible, and how it should influence the final answer. We formulate this problem as controlled concrete reasoning, where a model learns to invoke, verify, and integrate visual future simulation alongside abstract reasoning. To study this setting, we construct two human-verified benchmarks, VRQABench for controllable spatial lookahead and OpenWorldQA for open-domain physical prediction, and propose Privileged-Future On-Policy Self-Distillation (PF-OPSD). During training, PF-OPSD uses ground-truth future videos and answers only as teacher-side privileged context to evaluate on-policy concrete-reasoning trajectories, while the deployable student never observes true futures at test time. Experimental results show that PF-OPSD outperforms baseline by 10.6% and 10.9% on VRQABench and OpenWorldQA, respectively, while increasing robustness to noisy or conflicting rollouts. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/yczhou001/PF-OPSD.

</details>


### [19] [SkelHCC: A Hyperbolic CLIP-Driven Cache Adaptation Framework for Skeleton-based One-Shot Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03610)
*Yanan Liu,Anqi Zhu,Jingmin Zhu,Jun Liu,Hossein Rahmani,Mohammed Bennamoun,Farid Boussaid,Dan Xu,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SkelHCC框架，一种基于双曲几何和CLIP驱动的骨架动作识别一次性学习方法，通过显式层次双曲嵌入和免训练LLM引导的多粒度投票缓存机制，在多个基准数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性骨架动作识别面临极端数据稀缺的挑战。现有方法主要基于欧几里得嵌入和低层运动线索，难以建模骨架数据的树状层次结构（关节-部位-身体层级），限制了跨模态对齐和对未见动作类别的泛化能力。因此需要一种能够同时捕捉人体运动层次化、组合化结构并与高层动作语义有效对齐的表示学习方法。

Method: SkelHCC框架包含两个核心模块：（1）显式层次双曲CLIP模块（EH-HCLIP），将骨架序列和动作语言嵌入到共享的双曲空间中，利用双曲几何的负曲率和指数体积增长特性，自然编码人体关节-部位-身体的层次结构，生成结构一致的跨模态表示；（2）免训练的LLM引导多粒度投票缓存模块（LMV-Cache），通过上下文感知推理支持高效的一次 shot自适应。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D 60、NTU RGB+D 120和PKU-MMD三个基准数据集上的实验表明，SkelHCC持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SkelHCC通过将双曲几何与CLIP跨模态对齐相结合，有效解决了骨架数据的层次结构建模问题，同时利用LLM引导的缓存机制实现了高效的少样本自适应，为一次性骨架动作识别提供了新的统一框架。

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition aims to understand human behaviors from body joint sequences and is especially challenging in the one-shot setting, where only a single labeled exemplar is available for each novel action. A key challenge is learning representations that capture the hierarchical and compositional structure of human motion while aligning effectively with high-level action semantics under extreme data scarcity. Existing approaches, largely based on Euclidean embeddings and low-level motion cues, struggle to model the tree-like organization of skeleton data, limiting cross-modal alignment and generalization to unseen action categories. We propose SkelHCC, a unified skeleton hyperbolic CLIP-driven cache adaptation framework for one-shot skeleton-based action recognition. SkelHCC introduces an Explicitly Hierarchical Hyperbolic CLIP (EH-HCLIP) module that embeds skeleton sequences and action language into a shared hyperbolic space. By leveraging the negative curvature and exponential volume growth of hyperbolic geometry, EH-HCLIP naturally encodes the joint-part-body hierarchy of human anatomy and yields structurally consistent cross-modal representations. To support efficient one-shot adaptation, SkelHCC further integrates a training-free LLM-guided Multi-granularity Voting Cache (LMV-Cache) for context-aware inference. Experiments on NTU RGB+D 60, NTU RGB+D 120, and PKU-MMD demonstrate that SkelHCC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [20] [VidMsg: A Benchmark for Implicit Message Inference in Short Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03635)
*Issar Tzachor,Michael Green,Rami Ben-Ari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VidMsg是一个用于评估短视频隐含信息理解能力的基准数据集，包含400个YouTube视频（9个主题领域、52条信息），通过“信息优先”流程构建，支持双向信息-视频检索和诊断性问答，实验表明当前视频语言模型在处理该任务时存在明显短板，需要更强的语用推理和上下文理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频理解不仅涉及识别可见物体和动作，还隐含视频创作者的意图和信息；现有评估方法多聚焦于表层内容，缺乏对隐含信息（即“信息”或“目的”）的系统性评估，因此需要构建一个专门针对隐含信息理解的基准数据集。

Method: VidMsg采用“信息优先”的构建流程：首先由大语言模型将目标信息转化为间接搜索场景，据此检索候选视频；随后人工标注员筛选出既传达预期信息又不显式的视频。该基准包含400个YouTube视频，涵盖职业与金融、教育、健康与福祉、文化、安全、可持续性、生活方式等9个主题领域和52条细粒度信息，设计用于双向信息-视频检索，并提供诊断性多选题问答任务。

Result: 对当前视频语言和检索模型的实验表明，强模型在VidMsg上表现不佳，因为该任务需要语用推理、上下文线索整合以及区分语义相近的信息。研究者提出了基线方法VidVec-Msg，可提升面向信息的检索性能，但仍存在显著改进空间。

Conclusion: VidMsg揭示了当前模型在短视频隐含信息理解上的局限性，强调该任务对语用推理和上下文整合能力的高要求，为未来视频理解和检索研究提供了明确的方向和挑战。

Abstract: Understanding short online videos involves more than identifying visible objects and actions; video makers often include an underlying message or purpose in the clip. We introduce VidMsg, a benchmark for evaluating implicit message understanding in short, internet-native video clips. VidMsg contains 400 YouTube-derived clips across 9 practical topic areas and 52 fine-grained target messages, covering domains such as career and finance, education, health and well-being, culture, safety, sustainability, and lifestyle. VidMsg is constructed through a message-first pipeline: an LLM first translates target messages into indirect search scenarios, which are used to retrieve candidate clips. Human annotators then retain clips that convey the intended message without being overly explicit. VidMsg is designed primarily for bidirectional message-clip retrieval for scalable applications such as video search and recommendation, where systems must capture holistic video understanding. In addition to retrieval, VidMsg includes a diagnostic multiple-choice QA benchmark, where models select the intended message of a clip from semantically related alternatives. Experiments with contemporary video-language and retrieval models show that strong models often fail on VidMsg, because the task requires pragmatic inference, integration of contextual cues, and discrimination among semantically close messages. We also introduce VidVec-Msg, a baseline method that improves message-oriented retrieval while leaving substantial headroom for future work.

</details>


### [21] [Ultralytics YOLO26: Unified Real-Time End-to-End Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03748)
*Glenn Jocher,Jing Qiu,Mengyu Liu,Shuai Lyu,Fatih Cagatay Akyon,Muhammet Esat Kalfaoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YOLO26是一个统一的实时视觉模型家族，通过双头无NMS设计、移除DFL、MuSGD混合优化器、渐进式损失和小目标标签分配策略等创新，在检测、实例分割、姿态估计、分类和旋转检测等多任务上实现了精度-延迟帕累托前沿的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有YOLO检测器仍存在多项局限：推理时依赖非极大值抑制（NMS）、因分布焦点损失（DFL）导致检测头过重、训练周期长、最小目标可能无法获得正标签分配。本文旨在通过协调架构与训练创新，系统性地解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 双头设计实现原生无NMS端到端推理，完全移除DFL，获得更轻量检测头和无约束回归范围；2) 训练流程整合MuSGD混合优化器（源自大语言模型训练的Muon-SGD混合方案）、渐进式损失（Progressive Loss，将监督逐步转移至推理时使用的检测头）、STAL标签分配策略（确保小目标正样本覆盖）；3) 针对实例分割、姿态估计和旋转检测设计任务专用头与损失；4) 提供开放词汇扩展YOLOE-26，支持文本提示、视觉提示和无提示推理；5) 覆盖五个规模（n/s/m/l/x）。

Result: 在COCO数据集上，YOLO26全规模实现40.9-57.5 mAP，T4 TensorRT延迟为1.7-11.8 ms，在精度-延迟帕累托前沿上超越先前实时检测器。YOLOE-26x在LVIS minival上通过文本提示达到40.6 AP。跨任务（检测、实例分割、姿态估计、旋转检测、分类）和跨规模均获得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: YOLO26通过架构与训练的协同创新，成功解决了实时视觉模型在NMS依赖、检测头冗余、训练效率和标签分配等方面的关键瓶颈，在多任务、多规模上实现了统一的高性能实时视觉感知，并通过YOLOE-26扩展至开放词汇场景，推动了实时检测器的精度-效率边界。

Abstract: Real-time vision demands models that are accurate, efficient, and simple to deploy across diverse hardware. The YOLO family has become widely deployed for this reason, yet most YOLO detectors still rely on non-maximum suppression at inference, carry heavy detection heads due to Distribution Focal Loss, require long training schedules, and can leave the smallest objects without positive label assignments. We present Ultralytics YOLO26, a unified real-time vision model family that addresses these limitations through coordinated architecture and training advances. YOLO26 uses a dual-head design for native NMS-free end-to-end inference and removes DFL entirely, yielding a lighter head with unconstrained regression range. Its training pipeline combines MuSGD, a hybrid Muon-SGD optimizer adapted from large language model training; Progressive Loss, which shifts supervision toward the inference-time head; and STAL, a label assignment strategy that guarantees positive coverage for small objects. Beyond detection, YOLO26 introduces task-specific head and loss designs for instance segmentation, pose estimation, and oriented detection, producing consistent gains across tasks and scales. The family spans five scales (n/s/m/l/x) and supports detection, instance segmentation, pose estimation, classification, and oriented detection in a single pipeline, with an open-vocabulary extension, YOLOE-26, for text-, visual-, and prompt-free inference. Across all scales, YOLO26 achieves 40.9-57.5 mAP on COCO at 1.7-11.8 ms T4 TensorRT latency, advancing the accuracy-latency Pareto front over prior real-time detectors, while YOLOE-26x reaches 40.6 AP on LVIS minival under text prompting. Code and models are available at https://github.com/ultralytics/ultralytics.

</details>


### [22] [SLU-2K: A Question-Based Benchmark for Semantic Evaluation of Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03788)
*Zeno Testa,Antonino Furnari,Lorenzo Baraldi,Natalia Díaz-Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了从手语翻译（SLT）到手语理解（SLU）的评估范式转变，构建了SLU-2K数据集（2,350个封闭式视频问答对），用于评估系统从手语视频中恢复关键语义信息的能力。实验表明，现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）表现接近随机水平，最先进的翻译系统在语义理解上仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语翻译评估依赖BLEU和ROUGE等表面形式指标，仅奖励词汇重叠，无法直接衡量翻译是否保留了源手语序列的语义含义。这与手语翻译在辅助技术中的最终目标不一致，因此需要一种基于语义理解的评估方式。

Method: 1）提出从SLT到SLU的评估范式转变，重点关注语义理解；2）基于PHOENIX-2014T和CSL-Daily数据集，设计自动化数据生成流水线，构建SLU-2K数据集，包含7个类别的封闭式视频问答对（动作、位置、数字、物体、人物、时间、天气）；3）在SLU-2K上评估主流多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）和两个代表性系统（MMSTL和SpaMo）。

Result: 1）多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在SLU-2K上表现接近随机水平，表明当前AI系统缺乏手语语义理解的系统整合；2）在领域内数据上精心微调的最先进翻译系统仍存在显著语义差距，准确率范围为56.7%至75.2%。

Conclusion: 当前SLT评估协议高估了系统的真实理解能力，未来进展不应仅以流畅性和n-gram重叠来衡量，还应纳入语义正确性指标。SLU-2K为手语语义理解评估提供了新的基准，推动了该领域向更深层次语义理解发展。

Abstract: Sign Language Translation (SLT) is typically evaluated with surface-form metrics such as BLEU and ROUGE, which reward lexical overlap but do not directly measure whether a translation preserves the meaning of the source sign sequence. This is in contrast with the final objective of integrating SLT in assistive technology. In this work, we shift the focus from Sign Language Translation (SLT) to Sign Language Understanding (SLU), with particular emphasis on semantic understanding. Specifically, we evaluate systems based on their ability to correctly recover, from the input video, key semantic aspects of the original sentence, such as actions taking place and facts about people and objects. To enable this evaluation systematically, we propose SLU-2K, a dataset of 2,350 closed-ended video question-answer pairs based on the popular PHOENIX-2014T and CSL-Daily datasets. To obtain SLU-2K, we propose and extensively evaluate an automated data generation pipeline which produces questions across 7 categories, namely actions, locations, numbers, objects, people, time, and weather conditions. We show the potential of SLU-2K by evaluating popular Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and two representative state-of-the-art systems, MMSTL and SpaMo. Our results show that MLLMs reach near-random performance, highlighting the need for a more systematic integration of SLU in current AI systems. Furthermore, state-of-the-art translation systems carefully fine-tuned on in-domain data still exhibit a substantial semantic gap, with results ranging from 56.7% to 75.2%. These findings suggest that current SLT evaluation protocols overestimate true understanding and that future progress should be measured not only by fluency and n-gram overlap, but also by semantic correctness. Code, prompts, and benchmark files are available at https://github.com/ZenoTsT/SLU-2K

</details>


### [23] [Training-Free Multi-Concept LoRA Composition with Prompt-Aware Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03792)
*Georgios Tsoumplekas,Stella Bounareli,Vasileios Argyriou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多概念定制中LoRA模块间相互干扰导致视觉质量下降和身份保真度降低的问题，提出基于提示词感知的重要性加权策略（W-Switch和W-Composite）来优化多LoRA模块输出的组合，并通过新的基于图像的相似度评估框架在ComposLoRA测试床上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽能实现单概念定制，但多概念定制时简单组合多个LoRA权重或输出会导致概念间干扰，造成视觉质量下降和身份保真度降低。

Method: 提出W-Switch和W-Composite两种方法，利用提示词中触发词的语义影响来动态分配每个LoRA模块的权重，实现提示词感知的重要性加权组合。

Result: 在ComposLoRA测试床上取得一致改进，在视觉质量、身份保真度和组合性方面均优于现有最先进方法，并通过LLM评估和用户研究进一步验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了多概念定制中的LoRA模块干扰问题，通过提示词感知的权重策略实现了高质量、高保真度的多概念图像生成。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) successfully enables personalization in text-to-image generation by adapting pre-trained diffusion models to specific visual concepts and styles. However, extending such models to multi-concept customization remains challenging. Naively combining multiple LoRA weights or their outputs often leads to interference among concepts, resulting in degraded visual quality and reduced fidelity to the reference images of individual concepts. This paper proposes a simple yet effective approach for multi-concept customization by optimally combining the outputs of multiple LoRA modules. We leverage the relative importance of each concept during generation, as inferred from its corresponding prompt tokens and introduce two methods, W-Switch and W-Composite, that employ a prompt-aware importance weighting strategy in which each LoRA is weighted according to the semantic influence of its trigger words in the target prompt. In addition, we extend existing quantitative evaluation metrics by proposing a new image-based similarity evaluation framework that assesses image fidelity and identity preservation through comparisons between real-world reference images and automatically segmented concept regions from generated images. We evaluate our approach on the ComposLoRA testbed and demonstrate consistent improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods in terms of visual quality, identity preservation and compositionality. Qualitative evaluations, including a Large Language Model (LLM) based assessment and a user study, further validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods and align with the newly introduced quantitative image-based metrics. Our code is available at https://github.com/GeorgeTsoumplekas/Prompt-Aware-Multi-LoRA-Composition.

</details>


### [24] [Visual Instruction Tuning Aligns Modalities through Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03871)
*Luis Palacios,Lorenzo Basile,Diego Doimo,Alberto Cazzaniga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉指令微调如何将视觉特征嵌入LLM的层级抽象结构中，发现多模态整合主要发生在LLM的中间语义层，而非早期单模态处理层；仅微调中间层即可保持视觉基准性能并减少训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉指令微调已被广泛用于使预训练LLM处理图像与文本信息，但视觉特征究竟如何嵌入LLM的层级抽象结构中尚不清楚。作者希望揭示多模态整合在LLM内部的具体发生机制和位置。

Method: 在多种视觉-语言架构上，使用探测分析和因果干预方法研究指令微调后视觉特征在LLM各层的分布；通过比较语义等价的视觉与文本表征的几何结构，分析微调对抽象阶段的扩展与强化作用；最后通过仅限制微调中间层的实验验证局部对齐的功能性作用。

Result: （1）指令微调主要将视觉特征直接嵌入LLM的中间语义层，绕过早期单模态处理层；（2）中间层是视觉-语言处理的语义核心，对多模态基准性能起关键作用；（3）微调扩展并强化了已有的抽象阶段，使视觉特征与既有文本特征对齐；（4）仅微调中间层即可保持与全量微调相当的视觉基准性能，同时减少训练时间。

Conclusion: 多模态整合是一种局部现象，由LLM内部抽象引擎的重新利用所驱动。视觉指令微调的核心机制是在中间语义层实现视觉与文本特征的对齐，而非从头学习多模态表示。这一发现为高效的多模态微调策略提供了理论和实践依据。

Abstract: Visual instruction tuning effectively adapts a pre-trained Large Language Model (LLM) to process image information alongside text. Yet, it remains unclear how visual features are embedded into the layer-wise hierarchy of abstractions of the LLM backbone. Across a diverse set of vision-language architectures, we show that instruction tuning primarily serves as a bridge, embedding visual features directly into the intermediate semantic layers of the LLM, bypassing the early layers devoted to unimodal processing. With probing analyses and causal interventions, we show that these intermediate layers are the semantic core of vision-language processing and play a critical role in the performance on a broad set of multimodal benchmarks. In addition, by comparing the geometry of semantically equivalent visual and textual representations, we find that fine-tuning extends and strengthens the existing abstraction phase, aligning visual features with pre-existing textual ones. Finally, we confirm the functional role of this localized alignment by restricting fine-tuning to intermediate layers alone: this strategy preserves the performance of full fine-tuning on vision-centric benchmarks while reducing training time. Our results suggest that multimodal integration is a localized phenomenon driven by the repurposing of the internal abstraction engine of the LLM.

</details>


### [25] [OVO-S-Bench: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Streaming Spatial Intelligence in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03890)
*Yifei Li,Pengyiang Liu,Yuhang Zang,Zhongyue Shi,Qi Fu,Hongye Hao,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OVO-S-Bench，一个面向流式空间智能的全人工标注基准，包含348个视频上的1680个问题，评估了38个多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在流式自我中心视频中的空间推理能力，发现最强模型仍落后人类专家27分，且空间推理瓶颈主要集中在异中心映射任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人和自动驾驶等多模态智能体需要从连续的自我中心视频流中推理空间布局，且常需利用当前视野之外的证据。然而现有基准要么离线评估完整视频，要么关注事件而非空间结构，缺乏对流式空间智能的系统性评测。

Method: 构建OVO-S-Bench基准：由12名训练有素的标注员（同时互为盲审交叉审核者）耗费约804人时进行多轮质量保证，完成348个视频、1680个问题的人工标注。每个问题包含查询时间戳和证据区间，评估时模型仅能看到查询时间点之前的前缀视频。问题涵盖四个递进抽象层次：瞬时自我中心感知、时空上下文跟踪、空间模拟与推理、异中心映射。

Result: 在38个商用和开源MLLM上评测，Gemini-3.1-Pro得分为59.2，落后人类专家（86.6）27分；异中心映射是主要瓶颈。值得注意的是，经过流式和空间微调的MLLM表现反而不如其原始骨干模型；思维链推理在缺乏视频流支撑时反而放大了空间错误。

Conclusion: OVO-S-Bench揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在流式空间推理方面的显著不足，尤其是异中心映射能力，为下一代流式空间多模态模型提供了一个高难度的评测平台。

Abstract: Multimodal agents in robotics, AR, and autonomous driving must reason about places and layouts from continuous egocentric streams, often using evidence outside the current view. Existing benchmarks either evaluate offline over full videos or target events rather than spatial structure. We introduce OVO-S-Bench, a fully human-annotated benchmark for streaming spatial intelligence, comprising 1,680 questions over 348 source videos. Annotation involves 12 trained annotators, each also serving as a blind cross-reviewer, across roughly 804 person-hours of multi-round quality assurance. Each question carries a query timestamp and an evidence interval, and at evaluation, the model sees only the prefix preceding the query. Questions span four levels of increasing abstraction: instantaneous egocentric perception, spatiotemporal context tracking, spatial simulation and reasoning, and allocentric mapping. Across 38 proprietary and open-source MLLMs, Gemini-3.1-Pro trails human experts by 27 points, 59.2 vs. 86.6, with allocentric mapping as the dominant bottleneck. Notably, streaming and spatially fine-tuned MLLMs underperform their own backbones. We further find that chain-of-thought reasoning amplifies spatial errors when ungrounded in the stream. By exposing these limitations, OVO-S-Bench establishes a demanding testbed for next-generation streaming spatial MLLMs.

</details>


### [26] [PatchScene: Patch-based Voxel Diffusion for Large-Scale Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03915)
*Qingdong Xu,Jiajun Zhu,Shilin Zhu,Xinjing He,Chao Lu,Huanran Wang,Jiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PatchScene是一种基于扩散模型的大规模激光雷达场景补全框架，采用基于补丁的体素扩散范式，实现了精细几何生成和时空一致性重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖全局潜在表示或密集体素网格，无法有效生成精细几何结构，且缺乏时空一致性。

Method: 提出基于补丁的体素扩散范式，引入置信度引导的时空融合机制，设计环状流扩散策略，从近程到远程区域逐步传播高保真信息。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI基准测试中达到最先进性能，在20米训练范围下无需重训即可泛化到50米场景，展现出强大的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: PatchScene为自动驾驶应用提供了高效、可扩展的场景补全解决方案，具备优异的几何精度和时序一致性。

Abstract: We propose PatchScene, a novel diffusion-based framework for large-scale LiDAR scene completion. Unlike existing methods that rely on global latent representations or dense voxel grids, PatchScene adopts a patch-based voxel diffusion paradigm that explicitly generates fine-grained geometry within localized 3D regions. To ensure coherent reconstruction at both spatial and temporal scales, we introduce a confidence-guided spatio-temporal fusion mechanism that integrates overlapping patches and adjacent frames in a unified generative process. Furthermore, we design an Annular-Flow diffusion strategy that leverages the radial density pattern of LiDAR scans to progressively propagate high-fidelity information from near-range to far-range regions, enabling spatially unbounded scene completion. Extensive experiments on the SemanticKITTI benchmark demonstrate that PatchScene achieves state-of-the-art performance across all standard metrics, surpassing previous approaches in both geometric accuracy and temporal consistency. Remarkably, the model trained on 20 m LiDAR ranges generalizes effectively to 50 m scenes without retraining, highlighting its strong scalability and generalization capability for real-world autonomous driving applications.

</details>


### [27] [Benchmarking Visual State Tracking in Multimodal Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03920)
*Sihyun Yu,Nanye Ma,Pinzhi Huang,Hyunseok Lee,Shusheng Yang,June Suk Choi,Ellis Brown,Oscar Michel,Boyang Zheng,Jinwoo Shin,Saining Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Visual State Tracking benchmark (VSTAT)，一个面向多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的视频状态跟踪基准，包含834个视频片段和1500个问题，发现当前最先进的MLLMs在视觉状态跟踪任务上远低于人类水平，主要瓶颈在于视觉感知而非文本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解需要人类持续跟踪实体、状态和事件随时间的变化，这种视觉状态跟踪能力是视频理解的基础。然而，当前对多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的评估尚未充分探索这一能力，因此需要一个专门的基准来诊断MLLMs在视觉状态跟踪方面的表现。

Method: 构建VSTAT基准，包含834个来自合成和真实世界的视频片段，配以1500个问题。这些问题无法从任何单一帧或短视频段中回答，需要模型对整个视频流进行连续感知和事件整合。作者还通过将MLLMs的思维链(trace)与底层视频流进行对比分析，探究模型失败的原因和时机，并评估了基于MLLM的视频代理和编码代理等智能体方法。

Result: 最先进的MLLMs在VSTAT上的表现远低于人类水平，仅略高于答案先验基线。分析发现，MLLMs在文本推理和跟踪方面表现正确，但在视觉感知需要跟踪的事件时失败。此外，最新的智能体方法(包括MLLM视频代理和编码代理)也无法有效解决这些失败，在VSTAT上仍然表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在视觉状态跟踪这一核心视频理解能力上存在显著不足，瓶颈在于视觉感知能力而非文本推理能力，且现有的智能体方法尚不能弥补这一缺陷，表明视觉感知是未来需要重点突破的方向。

Abstract: Understanding a video requires more than recognizing isolated moments, as humans continuously track entities, states, and events over time. This capacity for visual state tracking is fundamental to video understanding, yet remains underexplored in current evaluations of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). We introduce Visual STAte Tracking benchmark (VSTAT), a video-based benchmark designed to diagnose visual state tracking in MLLMs. VSTAT consists of 834 clips drawn from both synthetic and real-world videos, paired with 1,500 questions that cannot be answered from any single frame or short segment, requiring continuous perception and integration of events across the entire video stream. Despite their strong performance on existing video benchmarks, we find that state-of-the-art MLLMs perform far below humans and only modestly above answer-prior baselines. To analyze this gap, we compare MLLMs' thinking traces with the underlying video stream to understand why and when MLLMs fail on VSTAT. We find that MLLMs reason and track correctly in text, but fail at visually perceiving the events they need to track. Finally, our preliminary evaluation suggests that recent agentic approaches, including MLLM-based video agents and coding agents, do not readily resolve these failures, still falling short on VSTAT.

</details>

### [28] [IdiomX A Multilingual Benchmark for Idiom Understanding, Retrieval, and Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02584)
*Ayman Ali Sharara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IdiomX 是一个大规模多语言成语理解、检索和解释基准，包含 190K+ 上下文示例、12K+ 成语及英阿法三语对齐语义表示，定义四任务基准覆盖检测、检索与解释，实验表明上下文Transformer提升检测，混合检索显著增强检索效果，且成语解释可建模为语义检索任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成语表达含义非组合、依赖语境且跨语言对齐困难，现有资源在规模、语境多样性和多语言覆盖上受限，限制了现代语言模型对成语的理解与利用。

Method: 通过可复现的多阶段流水线构建：词汇资源提取→大规模归一化→受控大语言模型增强→结构化验证，整合英阿法三语语义表示、成语与字面用例标签及丰富语言学元数据。

Result: 数据集包含 190K+ 上下文化示例、12K+ 成语及英阿法对齐语义表示与用例标签；定义四任务基准（成语检测、语境到成语检索、阿英成语检索、成语解释）；实验显示上下文Transformer提升检测，混合检索架构显著增强单语与跨语言成语检索；成语解释可建模为语义检索任务，提升可解释性。

Conclusion: IdiomX 提供可扩展基准，支持从检测到检索再到语义解释的成语研究路径，并可作为模块化框架扩展至更多语言与比喻推理任务。

Abstract: Idiomatic expressions remain a persistent challenge for natural language processing because their meanings are often non-compositional, context-dependent, and difficult to align across languages. Existing idiom resources are often limited in scale, contextual diversity, or multilingual coverage, restricting their utility for modern language models. We introduce IdiomX, a large-scale multilingual benchmark for idiom understanding, retrieval, and interpretation, constructed through a reproducible multi-stage pipeline combining lexical resource extraction, large-scale normalization, controlled large language model enrichment, and structured validation. The resulting dataset contains over 190K contextualized examples spanning 12K+ idioms, with aligned English, Arabic, and French semantic representations, idiomatic and literal usage labels, and rich linguistic metadata. Building on this resource, we define a unified four-task benchmark covering idiom detection, context-to-idiom retrieval, Arabic-to-English idiom retrieval, and idiom interpretation, extending evaluation from figurative recognition to semantic grounding and explainable meaning retrieval. Experiments show that contextual transformer models substantially improve idiom detection, while hybrid retrieval and reranking architectures significantly strengthen both monolingual and cross-lingual idiom retrieval. Results further demonstrate that idiom interpretation can be effectively modeled as a semantic retrieval task, introducing interpretability as a complementary benchmark dimension. Overall, IdiomX provides a scalable benchmark for studying idiomatic language as a progression from detection to retrieval and semantic interpretation, and offers a modular framework extensible to additional languages and figurative reasoning tasks

</details>


### [29] [Greener Than Humans? Environmental Attitudes in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02741)
*Stefanie Kunkel,Tilman Hartwig,Marcus Voss,Emma K. Schütt,Angelika Gellrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个评估大语言模型（LLMs）环境认知、情感和行为建议的基准，对31个主流模型进行系统测试，发现LLMs整体倾向于环保进步立场，但对人格设定提示和意识形态引导表现出显著敏感性，引发了对模型可控性和规范可靠性的担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于可持续发展相关的决策支持、报告和公共传播中，但目前缺乏关于其输出中嵌入的环境态度的系统性证据。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统评估LLMs中内含的环保价值观及其在实际部署中的可靠性。

Method: 1）基于既有环境意识调查问卷和可持续行为测量指标，构建环境认知、情感和行为建议的评估基准；2）将该基准应用于31个广泛使用的商业和开源LLMs；3）比较模型之间以及模型与德国人类调查基准之间的差异；4）在不同提示条件下（包括人格设定提示和意识形态引导提示）评估模型响应的鲁棒性。

Result: 1）许多LLMs比普通受访者更倾向于环保进步态度，表现出更高的环境情感和认知水平，并推荐具有较大CO₂减排潜力的行为；2）环保倾向与模型来源、规模或发布背景之间无系统性关联；3）模型对上下文敏感，人格设定提示可控制其立场，且表现出迎合用户指定意识形态的"谄媚性偏移"（sycophantic shifts），引发对模型可操纵性和规范可靠性的担忧。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个可复用的LLMs可持续发展价值对齐评估框架，并强调随着AI系统日益嵌入可持续发展转型和公共决策过程，治理、透明度和批判性监督的重要性。研究警示了LLMs在环保议题上的可操控风险，呼吁加强对其规范可靠性的关注。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in sustainability-related decision support, reporting, and public communication, yet little systematic evidence exists on the environmental attitudes embedded in their outputs. This paper develops a benchmark for evaluating environmental cognition, affect, and behavioural recommendations in LLMs and applies it to 31 widely used proprietary and open-weight models. Drawing on questions from established environmental awareness surveys and additional sustainability-related behavioural measures, we compare LLM responses 1) among models and 2) between models and human survey benchmarks from Germany. We assess their robustness across prompting conditions. We find that many LLMs align more closely with environmentally progressive attitudes than the average survey respondent, exhibiting higher levels of environmental affect and cognition and recommending behaviours associated with substantial potential CO2 reductions. At the same time, we observe no systematic relationship between sustainability-oriented responses and model origin, size, or release context. However, models exhibit contextual sensitivity, controlled by persona-based prompting and show sycophantic shifts mirroring user-specified ideological positions, which raises concerns about steerability and normative reliability in real-world deployments. Our findings provide a reusable evaluation framework for assessing sustainability-related value alignment in LLMs and highlight the importance of governance, transparency, and critical oversight as AI systems become increasingly embedded in sustainability transformations and public decision-making.

</details>


### [30] [On the Persistent Effects of Lexicality in Large Language Mod](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02750)
*Hammad Rizwan,Muhammad Umair Haider,Nishant Subramani,Mona T. Diab,A. B. Siddique,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLMs）表示中词汇重叠与语义内容的关系，发现词汇影响贯穿模型深度，并揭示中间深度区域存在表示质量下降的过渡现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM提取的表示在下游应用中至关重要，但这些表示的结构往往受词汇重叠而非语义内容影响，研究者对其关系及对下游任务影响的理解仍有限。

Method: 采用对抗性语义压力测试，结合信息理论视角量化词汇重叠对语义内容的影响，并以摘要和模型编辑为案例研究展示下游应用影响。

Result: 发现词汇影响贯穿模型深度，在各种架构、训练方案和目标函数中持续存在；在模型中间深度区域，词汇和语义信号同时退化，表明存在对表面形式和意义都较差的过渡区域。

Conclusion: 词汇重叠对LLM表示的深度影响具有普遍性，需要重新评估当前模型训练和评估方法，特别是在下游应用如摘要和模型编辑中应考虑这一因素。

Abstract: Representations extracted from large language models (LLMs) play an important role in many downstream applications. However, the structure of these representations is often influenced by lexical overlap rather than semantic content. Our understanding of the relationship between this lexical influence and semantic content, and its implications for downstream tasks, remains limited. In this work, we investigate representations to quantify the effect of lexical overlap relative to semantic content. We consider several adversarial semantic stress tests and further connect our findings to the information theory perspective. We find that lexical influence extends across the depth of models, consistently across architectures, training regimes, and objective functions, including the models trained for semantic similarity. Moreover, we observe a mid-depth region in which both lexical and semantic signals degrade simultaneously, indicating a transitional regime where representations are poor for both surface form and meaning. We further demonstrate the effect of lexical influence on downstream uses of LLMs using summarization and model editing as a case study.

</details>


### [31] [Fixing FOLIO and MALLS: Verified Annotations and an LLM-assisted Framework to Focus Human Relabeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02837)
*Andrea Brunello,Cristian Curaba,Luca Geatti,Michele Mignani,Angelo Montanari,Nicola Saccomanno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统审计了NL-to-FOL基准数据集（FOLIO和MALLS），发现约39%的FOLIO和36%的MALLS条目包含错误的FOL形式化标注，并开发了LLM辅助的人工验证框架，可帮助人类在审查不到24%的样本后达到90%的数据集准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NL-to-FOL转换是神经符号AI系统和自然语言推理（NLI）的基础，但现有基准数据集从未经过严格审计，标注错误会严重影响模型评估结果。

Result: FOLIO中有39%的条目包含错误FOL形式化、16.4%的歧义NL句子、8.4%的错误NLI标签；MALLS中有36%的条目包含错误FOL形式化、48%的歧义NL句子。使用修正标注后，三个SOTA LLM的准确率提升9-22个百分点。LLM辅助框架可在审查不到24%的样本后达到90%数据集准确率，而人工无指导审查需要超过70%的样本。

Conclusion: 现有NL-to-FOL基准数据集存在大量标注错误，直接影响了模型评估的可靠性。论文提出的LLM辅助人工审核框架能有效提高数据集质量维护效率，为神经符号AI和NLI领域提供了高质量基准数据。

Abstract: Accurate translation from Natural Language to First-Order Logic (NL-to-FOL) underpins neurosymbolic AI systems and Natural Language Inference (NLI), making the quality of NL-to-FOL benchmarks essential -- yet these datasets have never been rigorously audited. Our first contribution is to present a systematic human inspection of the validation split of \textsf{FOLIO} and a subset of \textsf{MALLS} test instances, finding that approximately 39% and 36% of entries, respectively, contain incorrect FOL formalizations (i.e., ground truth labels), with additional rates of ambiguous NL sentences (16.4% and 48%) and incorrect NLI labels in \textsf{FOLIO} (8.4%). Our second contribution is to develop and release corrected ground truths for such datasets, showing that annotation errors distort model evaluation on a reference benchmark task: testing three state-of-the-art LLMs (Gemma~4 31B-it, Qwen3-30B-A3B, and GPT-4o-mini) with the corrected ground truths yields accuracy gains from +9 to +22 percentage points. Motivated by these findings, we propose an LLM-based framework to support humans in manual reviewing NL-to-FOL datasets. By directing reviewers toward the most error-prone instances, we empirically show that it is possible to achieve 90% dataset accuracy after reviewing fewer than 24% of instances, compared to over 70% required by unguided review. We release all human-verified annotations and the code for our framework.

</details>


### [32] [Adaptive Latent Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02871)
*Dongwon Jung,Peng Shi,Yi Zhang,Junshan Zhang,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Adaptive Latent Agentic Reasoning (ALAR)双模式框架，通过在常规决策步骤中使用紧凑的潜在推理，仅在需要深度思考时切换到显式思维链(CoT)，从而在保持任务准确率的同时大幅减少LLM智能体的token生成量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过生成扩展的思维链(CoT)来提升性能，但在LLM智能体场景中，每个决策步骤都生成冗长的文本推理，且在不同轮次间几乎均匀分配推理努力，导致多轮智能体轨迹中存在严重的效率低下问题。

Method: 提出ALAR双模式框架：1) 利用智能体的动作作为监督锚点来学习潜在(latent)推理；2) 通过优化使模型在潜在推理足以完成任务时使用潜在推理，仅在遇到困难决策时才切换到显式CoT推理。常规步骤使用紧凑的潜在推理，关键步骤选择性升级为显式推理。

Result: 在智能体搜索和工具使用基准测试上，ALAR在保持相当或更优的任务准确率的同时，显著减少了生成token数量——搜索任务中减少高达43.6%，工具使用任务中减少高达84.6%。

Conclusion: ALAR通过减少不必要的文本推理、同时保留对困难决策步骤的显式深思熟虑，有效改善了LLM智能体的准确率-效率权衡，证明了自适应推理模式分配在智能体场景中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models improve performance by generating extended chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, but this behavior becomes inefficient when applied to LLM agents. Current LLM agents often generate verbose textual reasoning at every decision step and allocate reasoning effort nearly uniformly across turns, leading to substantial inefficiency in multi-turn agentic trajectories. We propose Adaptive Latent Agentic Reasoning (ALAR), a dual-mode framework that uses compact latent reasoning for routine turns and selectively escalates to explicit chain-of-thought when deeper deliberation is needed. ALAR learns latent reasoning by using the agent's actions as supervision anchors and is further optimized to use latent reasoning when it is sufficient for task success and reserve explicit CoT for harder decisions. Experiments on agentic search and tool-use benchmarks show that ALAR maintains comparable or better task accuracy while substantially reducing generated tokens by up to 43.6% in search and 84.6% in tool use. These results demonstrate that ALAR improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of LLM agents by reducing unnecessary textual reasoning while preserving explicit deliberation for harder decision steps.

</details>


### [33] [Linear Probes Detect Task Format, Not Reasoning Mode in Language Model Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02907)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究质疑线性探测方法声称大语言模型对推理类型学习到不同表征的有效性。通过对Qwen3-14B模型在演绎推理、归纳推理和溯因推理三类任务上的测试发现，虽然探测准确率达到100%，但这种分离完全是由格式混淆驱动的，而非真正的推理结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探测方法常被用来证明大语言模型对不同的推理类型学习到了不同的表征，但这一假设的有效性需要得到更严格的验证，特别是需要排除格式混淆等因素的干扰。

Method: 在Qwen3-14B模型的第32层（共40层）使用线性探测器测试三种推理类型：LogiQA 2.0（演绎推理）、ARC-Challenge（归纳推理）和αNLI（溯因推理）。通过交叉验证准确率、几何分离性分析、残差化格式特征、追踪锚点相似度分析和因果引导实验等方法进行评估。

Result: 线性探测器在层32达到100%的交叉验证准确率且几何分离良好，但该分离完全是由格式混淆（来源身份、选项数量、响应长度）驱动的。去除这些格式特征后准确率降至偶然水平。追踪锚点相似度显示任务间推理高度共享（42.5% vs 33.3%的随机水平），因果引导实验表明几何结构与推理模式之间没有功能联系（p=0.286）。

Conclusion: 高探测准确率反映的是任务格式而非计算结构，这为机制解释学中的格式去混淆提出了常规要求，表明需要重新评估线性探测方法在推理类型区分方面的有效性。

Abstract: Linear probing of large language model (LLM) hidden states is widely used to claim that models learn distinct representations for different reasoning types. We test this by probing Qwen3-14B on three benchmarks spanning the classical trichotomy: LogiQA 2.0 (deductive), ARC-Challenge (inductive), and $α$NLI (abductive). At layer 32 of 40, linear probes achieve 100\% cross-validated accuracy with well-separated geometry (intrinsic dimensionalities: 20.6, 28.5, 33.6; convex hull contamination $\leq$1.5\%). However, this separation is entirely driven by format confounds. Residualizing source identity, option count, and response length reduces accuracy to chance. Trace-anchor similarity indicates largely shared reasoning across tasks (42.5\% agreement vs.\ 33.3\% chance), and causal steering with random controls ($n=20$) shows no functional link between geometry and reasoning mode ($p=0.286$). Thus, high probe accuracy reflects task format rather than computational structure, motivating routine format deconfounding in mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [34] [The Ghost Annotator: a Framework to Explore Human Label Variation in Content Moderation through Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02911)
*Mirko Lai,Alessandra Urbinati,Simona Frenda,Fabiana Vernero,Marco Antonio Stranisci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合保形预测（Conformal Prediction）与协同过滤式标注者表示的框架，用于建模LLM与人类标注者之间的关系，量化模型预测偏离所有人类标注的情况，并揭示模型在人口统计学维度上的结构性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注模型性能，而对不确定性估计的关注较少，尤其是在LLM越来越多地被用于生成标注数据的场景下。缺乏对LLM与人类标注者之间一致性与分歧模式的深入分析，以及模型在不同社会人口统计轴上的行为差异研究。

Method: 1) 将保形预测与协同过滤式标注者表示相结合，建模LLM相对于人类标注者的行为；2) 利用非一致性分数（Non-Conformity Scores）提出"幽灵预测"（Ghost Prediction）指标和"幽灵标注者"（Ghost Annotator）表示，量化模型预测偏离所有可用人类标注的情况；3) 计算余弦相似度以探索模型行为在不同社会人口统计轴上的差异；4) 在四个内容审核数据集上评估了来自不同规模和家族的四个LLM。

Result: 1) 所有模型的不确定性随标注者分歧的增加而增加；2) 较大的模型在对齐不到任何人类标注的文本分类上往往更加自信（即过度自信）；3) 幽灵标注者框架揭示了一致且稳健的人口统计学偏差模式，表明这种结构性偏差可能源于预训练语料库。

Conclusion: 该框架有效揭示了LLM在标注任务中的不确定性特征和系统性偏差。大模型在缺乏人类标注支持的样本上表现出更高的过度自信，且所有模型均存在与人口统计学相关的结构性偏差，这些偏差可能根植于预训练数据中。这对LLM作为自动标注工具的可靠性提出了重要警示。
