Abstract: Current research primarily focuses on model performance, while comparatively less attention has been devoted to uncertainty estimation, particularly in settings where LLMs are increasingly used to generate annotated data. We introduce a framework combining conformal prediction with Collaborative Filtering-style annotators' representation to model LLM behavior in relation to human annotators and to analyze patterns of agreement and disagreement. Using Non-Conformity Scores, we introduce the Ghost Prediction metric and the Ghost Annotator representation to quantify cases in which model predictions diverge from all available human annotations. We compute cosine similarity measures to explore differences in model behavior across sociodemographic axes. We evaluated four LLMs of different size and families across four content moderation datasets. Our finding shows that while we find that all models uncertainty increases with annotator disagreement, larger models tend to be more confident in the classification of texts that are not aligned with any human annotation. Finally, the Ghost Annotator framework reveals a consistent and robust pattern of demographic misalignment, suggesting a structural bias likely rooted in pretraining corpora.

</details>


### [35] [Fast-dLLM++: Fréchet Profile Decoding for Faster Diffusion LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02955)
*Siva Rajesh Kasa,Yasong Dai,Sumit Negi,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast-dLLM++，一种无需训练的扩散大语言模型并行解码加速方法。通过引入Fréchet profile decoding，利用排序后的完整置信度轮廓（而非单一最弱置信度）来选择并行提交的token集合，在保持模型和缓存机制不变的前提下，实现高达37%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型虽然支持并行token生成，但推理瓶颈在于决定哪些masked token可以安全地一起提交。Fast-dLLM采用KV缓存和置信度引导的并行解码，但其解码理论使用同质高置信度假设，实际上将每个候选集合退化为其最弱的token。真实解码步骤中token的置信度是异质的，这一假设导致了未被充分利用的加速空间。

Method: 提出Fréchet profile decoding：从完整排序置信度轮廓中选择并行提交集合，而非依赖单一最差情况置信度。该规则是Fast-dLLM因子选择器的异质置信度推广——在等置信度情况下精确恢复原规则，在置信度不均匀时提供可证明的"异质性奖励"。该方法无需训练，不改变模型、扩散过程和缓存实现，是现有Fast-dLLM解码的直接替换方案。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B模型上对GSM8K、MATH、HumanEval和MBPP四个基准的实验表明，profile感知选择有效提升了准确率-吞吐量前沿，在可比准确率下吞吐量提升最高达37%。理论改进直接转化为实际经验增益。

Conclusion: Fast-dLLM++通过利用异质置信度轮廓中安全并行性的机会，克服了Fast-dLLM同质假设的局限性。作为一种无需训练的即插即用方案，它在多个推理和代码生成基准上显著提升了吞吐量，证明了异质置信度感知解码策略对扩散语言模型加速的重要价值。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models promise parallel token generation, yet inference remains bottlenecked by deciding which masked tokens can be safely committed together. Fast-dLLM addressed this with KV caching and confidence-guided parallel decoding, but its decoding theory uses a homogeneous high-confidence assumption that effectively reduces each candidate set to its weakest selected token. We argue that this leaves speed on the table because real decoding steps exhibit heterogeneous confidence profiles. We propose \textbf{Fast-dLLM++}, a training-free extension that introduces \emph{Fréchet profile decoding}: selecting parallel commit sets from the full sorted confidence profile rather than a single worst-case confidence. The resulting rule is a heterogeneous-confidence generalization of Fast-dLLM's factor selector and it recovers the previous rule exactly in the equal-confidence case and adds a provable \emph{heterogeneity bonus} when the selected tokens have uneven confidences. Fast-dLLM++ leaves the model, diffusion process, and cache implementation entirely unchanged, making it a drop-in replacement for existing Fast-dLLM decoding. Experiments on GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MBPP with the LLaDA-8B model show that the theoretical improvement translates directly into empirical gains: profile-aware selection improves the accuracy--throughput frontier by exploiting safe parallelism that weakest-token rules miss, achieving up to 37\% higher throughput at comparable accuracy. Our anonymous code release is at https://github.com/Ringo-Star/FastdLLM_plusplus.

</details>


### [36] [EURO-5K: When Does Domain Pretraining Matter? Benchmarking Transformers for EU Reporting Obligation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02971)
*Marios Koniaris,Vasileios Kotronis,Eugenia Giannini,Panayiotis Tsanakas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建EURO-5K数据集，比较BERT和LLM在从欧盟立法中提取报告义务的效果，发现参数高效微调在法律预训练下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从欧盟立法中提取报告义务对评估和减少监管负担至关重要，但当前法律NLP方法缺乏专门数据集、明确指导和提取范式/领域适应策略的比较评估。

Method: 1) 创建EURO-5K数据集（136项欧盟立法的句子级报告义务和挑战性负例）；2) 比较判别式token分类模型（BERT）和生成式span提取模型（LLM）；3) 评估全量微调和参数高效QLoRA；4) 与模式提取、依赖提取、少样本提示基线比较。

Result: 全量微调通用/法律BERT表现相似（0.89 F1）；LLM匹配编码器精度；法律预训练对生成式模型增益有限，但对参数受限时有益；所有方法在3K样本收敛；跨数据集评估显示模型作为专门的报告义务提取器而非通用分类器。

Conclusion: 法律预训练加速早期学习且数据需求低，所有方法在3K样本处收敛；研究提供了监管合规自动化的实用工具，包括EURO-5K数据集、训练模型、交互式演示（含可解释性可视化、RDF导出）。

Abstract: Extracting reporting obligations from EU legislation is critical for assessing and reducing regulatory reporting burden. However, distinguishing reporting requirements from structurally similar provisions requires specialised legal understanding. Current legal NLP methods lack specialised datasets with clear guidelines and comparative evaluation of extraction paradigms and domain adaptation strategies. We curate EURO-5K, a corpus of sentence-level reporting obligations and challenging negative examples from 136 EU legislative acts. On this dataset, we train and compare discriminative token-classification models (BERT-style) and generative span-extraction models (LLMs), evaluating both full fine-tuning and parameter-efficient QLoRA against baselines (pattern and dependency-based extraction, few-shot prompting). Results show that fully fine-tuned generic and legal BERT models achieve similar performance (0.89 F1), while fine-tuned LLMs match encoder accuracy for sentence-level extraction. Legal pretraining offers only small gains for generative models. In contrast, it is clearly beneficial when adaptation capacity is constrained, as parameter-efficient tuning of Legal-BERT outperforms its generic counterpart. Learning curve analysis demonstrates that legal pretraining accelerates early learning with minimal data. All approaches converge around 3K samples with diminishing returns thereafter, validating dataset sufficiency. Cross-dataset evaluation on two external regulatory corpora shows that our models behave as specialised reporting obligation extractors rather than generic regulatory classifiers. We release EURO-5K, trained models, and an interactive demo with explainability visualizations and structured RDF export. These demonstrate that both paradigms and parameter-efficient training provide practical tools for regulatory compliance automation.

</details>


### [37] [A Locally Deployed RAG-Based Academic Advising System for Course Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02983)
*Feng Li,Yoritaka Iwata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于本地部署RAG技术的学术咨询系统，结合大语言模型与结构化课程大纲数据，支持课程选择、先修课理解及个性化学习规划，在保护隐私的前提下解决学生选课规划和教育资源不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程顺序对知识技能发展至关重要，但学生独立规划时常受限于信息识别能力和信息过载，导致困惑；同时教育机构因资源有限难以提供充分的学术建议。

Method: 构建本地部署的基于RAG的学术咨询系统，利用大语言模型结合结构化课程大纲数据进行检索增强生成，支持课程选择、先修课理解及个性化学习规划，采用隐私保护方式实现。

Result: 系统设计完成，具备课程选择、先修课理解及个性化学习规划能力，能在保护隐私的前提下辅助学生制定正确的课程序列，缓解学生规划困难和教育资源不足的问题。

Conclusion: 该RAG系统有效解决了学生选课规划和教育机构学术建议不足的问题，通过融合大语言模型与结构化课程数据，在本地部署环境中实现了隐私保护的个性化学习规划支持。

Abstract: The correct sequence of courses in the curriculum based on prerequisites between courses is of great importance for students to develop their knowledge and skills holistically. However, students crafting this sequence in isolation frequently struggle with recognition limitations and information overload that leads to confusion. Simultaneously, education institutions encounter difficulties in providing adequate academic advice for the correct sequence due to limited education resources. To address these challenges, we propose a locally deployed RAG-based academic advising system grounded in syllabus information. By combining large language models with retrieval from structured syllabus data, the system is designed to support course selection, prerequisite understanding, and personalized study planning in a privacy-preserving manner.

</details>


### [38] [Hint-Guided Diversified Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03021)
*Zhiyu Cao,Kaixin Wu,Mingjie Zhong,Peifeng Li,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Qiaoming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hint-Guided Diversified Policy Optimization (HDPO)，一种受人类解决问题过程启发的两阶段强化学习框架，通过"提议-选择-思考"轨迹引导大语言模型生成多样化候选解决方案并选择最可靠的方案进行推理，从而提升LLM的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法仅关注最终结果的正确性，缺乏引导模型考虑多种解决方案的显式信号。而人类在解决问题时通常会评估多种可能的方案并选择最可靠的路径，当前RLVR框架未能显式激励这种认知过程。

Method: HDPO包含两个阶段：1）结构化推理的冷启动阶段（Cold Start for Structured Reasoning），训练模型具备结构化推理能力；2）提示引导的多样化强化学习阶段（Hint-Guided Diversified Reinforcement Learning），激励模型遵循"提议-选择-思考"（propose-select-think）轨迹，先列出所有潜在候选方案大纲作为提示，再选择最可靠的方案进行深入推理。

Result: 实验结果表明，HDPO有效提升了LLM的推理能力，同时增强了候选解决方案的多样性以及LLM识别可靠解决方案的能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入"提议-选择-思考"的结构化推理范式，HDPO弥补了现有RLVR方法在解多样性方面的不足，为提升大语言模型推理能力提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: Recent developments in Large Language Models (LLMs) have showcased impressive reasoning capabilities, with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) being a promising enhancement strategy. However, existing reward mechanisms are constrained to the outcome-level correctness and lack explicit signals to guide the model to consider diverse solutions. In contrast, human problem solving typically involves evaluating multiple potential approaches and selecting the most reliable solution, a cognitive process that current RLVR frameworks do not explicitly incentivize. Inspired by this, we propose Hint-Guided Diversified Policy Optimization (HDPO), allowing the model to first list all potential candidate solution outlines as hints and then select the most reliable one for further reasoning. HDPO comprises two stages of Cold Start for Structured Reasoning and Hint-Guided Diversified Reinforcement Learning to incentivize the model to generate diverse and reliable solutions following the ``propose-select-think'' trajectory. Experimental results show that HDPO effectively boosts LLM reasoning and enhances the diversity of candidate solutions as well as the LLM's ability to identify reliable solutions.

</details>


### [39] [Hallucinations as Orthogonal Noise: Inference-Time Manifold Alignment via Dynamic Contextual Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03022)
*Mingkuan Zhao,Wentao Hu,Tianchen Huang,Yuheng Min,Suquan Chen,Yide Gao,Yanbo Zhai,Shuangyong Song,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何视角的推理时干预方法——动态上下文正交化（DCO），通过将注意力头输出相对于输入残差流进行正交分解，选择性抑制与上下文语义流形正交的噪声成分，从而缓解大语言模型（LLM）的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的幻觉问题（即生成与上下文事实或逻辑约束不一致的内容）是实现可靠部署的持续挑战。现有干预方法往往在抑制幻觉与保留参数化知识之间存在权衡。本文从线性表示假设的几何视角出发，探索幻觉在潜在表征空间中的几何本质，旨在开发一种既能有效抑制幻觉又能保留模型知识的推理时方法。

Method: 本文提出动态上下文正交化（DCO），一种推理时干预方法。其核心思想包括：(1) 基于线性表示假设，将幻觉建模为相对于残差流语义流形的正交噪声；(2) 以输入残差流作为动态上下文锚点，对注意力头输出进行正交分解，分离上下文对齐的语义更新与发散噪声；(3) 采用逐层Z分数抑制机制，基于统计分布选择性衰减正交方向的异常成分。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和70B模型上，于XSum、NQ-Swap、IFEval等基准测试中，DCO在上下文忠实度方面优于现有最先进的干预基线方法。同时，在TriviaQA和TruthfulQA等知识密集型任务上保持高性能，有效缓解了幻觉抑制与参数化知识保留之间的权衡问题。

Conclusion: 本文验证了幻觉的几何解释——幻觉表现为语义流形之外的正交噪声成分。DCO作为一种计算高效的推理时干预方法，通过流形对齐强制约束实现了幻觉抑制与知识保留的平衡，为LLM的可靠部署提供了新的几何框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Hallucination in Large Language Models (LLMs), characterized by the generation of content inconsistent with contextual facts or logical constraints -- remains a persistent challenge for reliable deployment. In this work, we address this issue through a geometric framework rooted in the linear representation hypothesis. We propose that hallucinations manifest as orthogonal noise relative to the semantic manifold of the residual stream. Specifically, we hypothesize that while attention heads ideally propagate information congruent with the context subspace, hallucinations arise when specific heads introduce components orthogonal to this subspace, disrupting the coherence of the latent representation. Based on this formulation, we introduce Dynamic Contextual Orthogonalization (DCO), an inference-time intervention method. DCO utilizes the input residual stream as a dynamic context anchor to perform orthogonal decomposition on attention head outputs. To distinguish between context-aligned semantic updates and divergent noise, DCO employs a layer-wise Z-score suppression mechanism that selectively attenuates outlier orthogonal components based on statistical distributions. Evaluations on Llama-3-8B and 70B across benchmarks such as XSum, NQ-Swap, and IFEval demonstrate that DCO achieves superior contextual faithfulness compared to state-of-the-art intervention baselines. Furthermore, DCO maintains high performance on knowledge-intensive tasks like TriviaQA and TruthfulQA, effectively mitigating the trade-off between hallucination suppression and parametric knowledge retention often observed in existing methods. Our findings validate the geometric interpretation of hallucinations and establish DCO as a computationally efficient approach for enforcing manifold alignment.Our code is available at https://github.com/Harry-Miral/DCO

</details>


### [40] [Conditional Hypothesis Generation for LLM-Based Text Analysis with Researcher-Specified Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03029)
*Paiheng Xu,Jing Liu,Wei Ai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了**条件假设生成**框架，通过引入研究者指定的协变量，引导LLM假设发现聚焦于相关子群体内的实质性差异，而非混淆因素驱动的全局模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的假设生成方法仅选择全局可区分的模式，忽略了协变量（如人口统计、背景因素）对数据的影响。当协变量被忽视时，所选模式可能反映混杂因素而非研究者真正关心的实质性差异，导致假设存在偏差。

Method: 提出两种受计量经济学启发的方法：（1）引入特征-协变量交互项来检测子群体间差异方向反转（sign reversal）的情况；（2）采用组内去均值和逆频率加权来均衡代表性不足的子群体（stratum imbalance）。两者结合形成条件假设生成框架。

Result: 合成实验表明，每种方法在其目标场景下均优于全局基线方法。在两个真实数据集上的专家评估进一步证实，协变量感知的生成能够在相关子群体中发现更有用的假设。

Conclusion: 将协变量纳入LLM假设生成过程可以显著提升假设的针对性和质量，避免混淆偏差，为计算社会科学中跨群体的语言差异分析提供了更严谨、更具解释力的方法。

Abstract: A core goal of computational social science is to discover interpretable differences in how language varies across outcomes of interest, such as political affiliation or instructional quality. Recent LLM-based hypothesis generation methods describe such differences in natural language, but select for globally discriminative patterns without accounting for covariates that shape the data based on researchers' domain knowledge. When covariates are ignored, selected patterns can reflect confounds rather than differences of substantive interest. We introduce conditional hypothesis generation, a framework that incorporates researcher-specified covariates to steer hypothesis discovery toward differences that hold within relevant subgroups. Two challenges arise: the target subgroup may be underrepresented (stratum imbalance), and the direction of a difference may reverse across subgroups (sign reversal). We propose two econometrics-inspired methods: one introduces feature--covariate interactions to detect sign reversals, and the other applies within-stratum demeaning and inverse-frequency reweighting to equalize underrepresented strata. Synthetic experiments show each method outperforms global baselines in its targeted setting, and expert evaluation on two real-world datasets confirms that covariate-aware generation surfaces more useful hypotheses within relevant subgroups.

</details>


### [41] [The Geometry of LLM-as-Judge: Why Inter-LLM Consensus Is Not Human Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03043)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Hamna Hamna,Kalika Bali,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过四种几何度量（分数展度、有效秩、与人类子空间的主夹角、堆叠相关性）系统评估了41个LLM裁判在8种印度语言上的表现，发现LLM裁判之间存在高度一致但与人类仅弱相关的现象，主要源于共享偏差而非共享信号；唯有基于小规模人类锚定数据的后置校准能全面改善对齐，且仍不及人类间的一致性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-judge已被广泛采用，但LLM裁判之间一致性很高而与人类一致性较弱。作者需要验证这一现象究竟反映的是LLM捕获了与人类相同的信号，还是仅仅反映了LLM之间的共享偏差，特别是在印度语言社区数据集这一低资源场景下。

Method: 在4个社区构建的印度语言数据集、8种印度语言、41个LLM裁判上，测量四种几何量并附带bootstrap置信区间：(1) 分数展度（score spread, σ_J/σ_H）衡量LLM是否使用完整评分范围；(2) 有效秩（effective rank）衡量评分子空间维度；(3) 主夹角（principal angle）衡量LLM评分轴与人类评分轴的几何关系；(4) 堆叠相关性（stacked correlations）比较LLM-LLM一致性与LLM-人类一致性。此外测试了微调、偏好优化和后置校准三种干预手段的效果。

Result: 主观评分项上：LLM仅使用不到一半的人类评分范围（σ_J/σ_H≈0.3-0.5）；评分轴与人类几乎正交（87°-89°），远超人类间夹角（78°-81°）；LLM间一致性（r≈0.35）高于LLM-人类一致性（r≈0.27-0.32）。在可验证事实评分项上，各项指标回落至人类范围（夹角58.5°，r_LH=0.519）。微调和偏好优化恢复评分展度（0.32→1.08）但几乎不改变评分轴方向（仍87°-88°）。仅后置校准能同时改善所有四项社区健康指标，使校准后的24B印度语言裁判（r=0.184）超过GPT-5.5（r=0.123），但仍未达到人类间可靠性水平（r=0.474）。

Conclusion: LLM裁判间的高一致性不应被直接视为与人类对齐的证据；只有在LLM评分子空间通过直接几何检查（主夹角、有效秩、展度等）时，共识才反映真实信号。否则，所谓共识仅是在坍缩子空间内的虚假一致。后置校准是目前最有效的对齐手段，但LLM裁判与人类评判之间的差距仍然显著。

Abstract: LMs-as-judges are now standard, yet judges agree strongly with one another while agreeing only weakly with humans. We test whether this reflects shared signal or shared bias by measuring four geometric quantities on the standard LLM-as-judge stack across four community-built Indic datasets, eight Indic languages, and 41 LLM judges: score spread, effective rank, principal angle to the human subspace, and stacked correlations among judges and humans, all with bootstrap confidence intervals.
  On subjective rubrics, judges use less than half the human score range ($σ_J / σ_H \approx 0.3$--$0.5$). Their evaluation axis is nearly orthogonal to the human one and noticeably further from humans than humans are from each other ($87^\circ$--$89^\circ$ versus $78^\circ$--$81^\circ$). Inter-LLM agreement exceeds LLM--human agreement ($r_{LL} \approx 0.35$ versus $r_{LH} \approx 0.27$--$0.32$).
  On a rubric with a verifiable factual answer, the same diagnostics fall back into the human range (axis $58.5^\circ$; $r_{LH} = 0.519$). Fine-tuning and preference optimization recover spread ($0.32 \rightarrow 1.08$) but barely move the axis (still $87^\circ$--$88^\circ$). Only post-hoc calibration on a small human-anchored set improves all four community-health rubrics together, placing a calibrated 24B Indic judge ($r = 0.184$) ahead of GPT-5.5 ($r = 0.123$), yet still short of human reliability (human-human $r = 0.474$ on the verifiable rubric).
  We argue that inter-LLM agreement should be considered evidence of human alignment only when a direct geometric check on the judge's score subspace passes; otherwise, the consensus reflects agreement within a collapsed subspace.

</details>


### [42] [G^2C-MT: Graph-Guided Context Selection for Document-Level Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03078)
*Baijun Ji,Zixuan Zhou,Xiangyu Duan,Yu Liu,Longbo Sun,Rupu Wei,Bohong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出G²C-MT框架，将文档级机器翻译的上下文选择建模为轻量级篇章图上的结构化路径发现问题，通过深度偏置随机游走采样上下文路径以提示LLM进行翻译，在多个LLM和领域上超越了强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级机器翻译需要捕捉长距离篇章依赖关系。现有方法（如基于检索的上下文选择和篇章感知方法）缺乏显式建模文档中远距离段落间结构化篇章依赖的机制，而基于LLM的篇章建模成本高昂。因此，亟需一种轻量且结构化的方法来捕捉段落间的篇章依赖。

Method: 提出G²C-MT框架：（1）将每个段落表示为图中的节点，基于语义相似度、邻接关系和关键词重叠建模节点对之间的关系，构建轻量级篇章图；（2）在图上执行深度偏置随机游走，为每个目标段落采样一条回溯上下文路径；（3）将上下文路径用于提示LLM进行翻译；（4）支持多路径上下文采样，通过聚合多样翻译候选提升对篇章歧义输入的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个领域的实验中，G²C-MT在多个LLM（包括DeepSeek-V3、Gemini-2.5-Flash-lite和Qwen-2.5/3系列）上均超越了强基线方法。

Conclusion: G²C-MT通过将上下文选择转化为篇章图上的结构化路径发现问题，以轻量级方式显式建模段落间的篇章依赖，有效提升了文档级机器翻译质量，同时具备良好的跨模型和跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Effective document-level machine translation (DocMT) requires capturing long-range discourse dependencies. Recent work has explored retrieval-based and discourse-aware context selection. However, these approaches often lack an explicit mechanism for modeling structured discourse dependencies between distant paragraphs in a document. In this paper, we propose G^2C-MT (Graph-Guided Context for Machine Translation), which views DocMT context selection as a structured path discovery problem on a lightweight discourse graph, rather than retrieving unstructured context sets or relying on expensive LLM-based discourse modeling. In detail, we represent each paragraph as a node and model the relationship between each pair of nodes, considering their semantic similarity, adjacency, and keyword overlap. Furthermore, we propose a depth-biased random walk over the graph to sample a backward context path for each target paragraph. The context path will be used to prompt a large language model (LLM) for translation. This framework naturally supports multi-path context sampling, which can improve robustness by aggregating diverse translation candidates for discourse-ambiguous inputs. Experiments conducted across various domains show that G^2C-MT outperforms strong baselines on multiple LLMs, including DeepSeek-V3, Gemini-2.5-Flash-lite, and the Qwen-2.5/3 series.

</details>


### [43] [Can Factual Opinions Be Edited (Manipulated) in Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03096)
*Yuanpu Cao,Ziyi Yin,Fenglong Ma,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM中事实性观点编辑的安全风险，作者提出FOE基准测试（包含261位公众人物、19个议题类别、2178条完整观点记录），发现现有编辑方法难以处理事实性观点，仅实现表面修改且无法保持观点与证据的一致性；提出自生成证据对齐方法，无需显式指令即可实现观点-证据对齐，为理解LLM中事实性观点编辑的安全影响奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM知识编辑方法主要针对原子事实，忽视了操纵公众人物对议题的事实性观点的显著风险，此类操纵可能重塑公众形象、影响选举、改变社会观点。为了系统评估这一威胁，需要专门针对事实性观点编辑的评估框架。

Method: 提出了Factual Opinion Editing with Evidence (FOE)基准测试，涵盖261位公众人物、19个议题类别、2,178条完整观点记录；提出了Self-Generated Evidence-Aligned方法，通过无需显式指令的机制实现观点与证据的对齐。

Result: 评估表明现有编辑技术在处理事实性观点时表现不佳，往往仅实现表面修改且无法保持编辑后的观点与模型生成的支持证据之间的一致性。

Conclusion: FOE基准测试和自生成证据对齐方法共同为理解LLM中事实性观点编辑的新兴安全影响提供了基础，有助于未来研究如何安全地处理此类敏感操作。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into various domains, making knowledge editing techniques crucial yet potentially hazardous. Current editing methods primarily target atomic facts, overlooking the significant risks associated with manipulating factual opinions, e.g., documented stances of public figures on societal issues. Such manipulation could reshape public images, influence elections, and alter societal views. To systematically assess this threat, we introduce the Factual Opinion Editing with Evidence (FOE) benchmark, which encompasses 261 public figures, 19 issue categories, and 2,178 complete opinion records. Our evaluations demonstrate that current editing techniques struggle significantly with factual opinions, often achieving only superficial changes while failing to preserve consistency between the edited opinion and the supporting evidence generated by the model. To address this limitation, we further propose a simple yet effective Self-Generated Evidence-Aligned method that achieves opinion-evidence alignment without relying on explicit instructions. Together, our benchmark and method provide a foundation for understanding the emerging security implications of factual opinion editing in LLMs.

</details>


### [44] [PhotoCraft: Agentic Reasoning with Hierarchical Self-Evolving Memory for Deep Image Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03099)
*Kailin Lyu,Zhiqiang Yuan,Jianwei He,Qiwei Yan,Xuanbo Su,Nanxing Hu,Yang Liu,Ce Hao,Shengqian Qin,Lianyu Hu,Jinchao Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhotoCraft是一种无需训练的层级记忆系统，为图像搜索代理提供工作记忆、情景记忆和语义记忆，解决大模型在多步推理中的执行漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的图像搜索代理大多是无状态的、反应式的，缺乏持久化记忆来维持长时序上下文或跨任务的经验迁移，导致执行漂移和经验隔离等关键瓶颈。

Method: 受人类认知启发，PhotoCraft为多模态大语言模型配备工作记忆、情景记忆和语义记忆，在推理过程中动态调用这些记忆以保持逻辑一致性并实现知识迁移。

Result: 在DISBench基准测试中，PhotoCraft显著提升了上下文感知检索能力，在多种多模态大语言模型骨干上取得最高18.5%的性能增益，有效缓解了无记忆深度图像搜索的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: PhotoCraft为构建可靠且可泛化的多模态搜索代理提供了实用路径，证明了层级记忆系统在复杂推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Deep Image Search requires multi-step reasoning over rich contextual cues, such as time, location, and event relations. However, most existing LLM-based agents are stateless and reactive, lacking persistent memory to maintain long-horizon context or transfer experience across tasks, which often leads to execution drift and experience isolation. To address these limitations, we propose PhotoCraft, a training-free, hierarchical memory system for photo-search agents. Inspired by human cognition, PhotoCraft equips MLLMs with working, episodic, and semantic memory, which are dynamically invoked during reasoning to preserve logical consistency and knowledge transferability throughout multi-step reasoning and answer generation. Extensive experiments on DISBench demonstrate that PhotoCraft consistently improves context-aware retrieval across diverse MLLM backbones, achieving gains of up to 18.5\% and effectively mitigating key bottlenecks in memoryless deep image search, offering a practical path toward reliable and generalizable multimodal search agents.

</details>


### [45] [Small RL Controller, Large Language Model: RL-Guided Adaptive Sampling for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03102)
*Runpeng Dai,Tong Zheng,Rui Liu,Chengsong Huang,Hongtu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将自适应采样建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过强化学习训练一个轻量级采样控制器，在答案正确性、延迟和计算成本之间取得平衡，实现测试时推理的高效资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（test-time scaling）虽然能提升大语言模型的推理性能，但会带来显著的计算和延迟开销。现有的自适应采样方法通过动态决定何时停止采样来部分缓解该问题，但通常依赖启发式规则或分布假设，缺乏理论支撑和灵活性。

Method: 将自适应采样形式化为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），利用强化学习（RL）训练一个轻量级采样控制器。该控制器在每一轮决定是停止采样还是继续获取额外样本，仅需依赖最终答案的统计信息，可在CPU上训练和部署。此外，该框架可被解释为带有显式预算约束的受限优化问题的拉格朗日松弛形式。

Result: 在与ASC和ESC等强基线方法的对比实验中，所提方法在答案正确性、采样轮数和总样本需求之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: 通过将自适应采样建模为MDP并用RL求解，本文提供了一种轻量、理论可解释的自适应采样控制方案，有效降低了测试时扩展的计算和延迟成本，同时保持或提升了答案正确性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves the reasoning performance of large language models but incurs substantial cost in both total computation and latency. Existing adaptive sampling methods partially mitigate this issue by dynamically deciding when to stop sampling, yet they typically rely on heuristic rules or rely on distribution assumptions. In this work, we formulate adaptive sampling as a Markov decision process (MDP). We train a lightweight sampling controller with reinforcement learning (RL) to jointly balance answer correctness, latency, and computation cost. At each round, the controller decides to stop sampling or to acquire additional samples. Our method is lightweight which only relies on statistics of final answers, and can be trained and deployed on CPU. We further show that the resulting framework admits an interpretation as the Lagrangian relaxation of a constrained optimization problem with explicit budget constraints. Experiments against strong baselines such as ASC and ESC show that our method achieves improved trade-offs among answer correctness, sampling rounds, and total samples required.

</details>


### [46] [Experience-Driven Dynamic Exits for LLMs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03113)
*Yanyu Zhu,Hoilam Pao,Niu Hu,Wei Guo,Shaoxiong Zhan,Boyu Lai,Zitai Wang,Yongqin Zeng,Hai-Tao Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LEDE框架，利用离线强化学习将自推测解码的优化建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），动态选择最优退出层和推测长度，从而加速大语言模型的自回归推理。在Llama-2和Llama-3上实现了2.0×~2.7×的加速，且比静态推测基线额外提升17%的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的自回归推理速度缓慢。现有的自推测解码方法虽然能加速推理，但其效率受限于静态配置（如固定的退出层和推测长度），无法根据生成序列的局部上下文动态调整，导致加速效果受限。

Method: 将推测解码的优化问题重新建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并提出LEDE框架，利用离线强化学习训练策略网络。该策略在每一步根据生成序列的局部上下文，动态选择最优的退出层和推测长度，在计算成本与草稿质量之间取得平衡。

Result: 在Llama-2和Llama-3模型上的全面评估表明，LEDE相比自回归解码实现了2.0×~2.7×的加速，同时相比静态推测解码基线获得了额外17%的速度提升。

Conclusion: LEDE通过强化学习驱动的动态配置策略，有效克服了自推测解码中静态配置的局限性，显著提升了大语言模型的推理效率，为推测解码的自适应优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models suffer from slow autoregressive inference. While self-speculative decoding accelerates this process, its efficiency is hampered by static configurations like fixed exit layers and speculation lengths. We reframe this optimization as a \textbf{Markov Decision Process} and propose \textbf{LEDE}, a framework that uses offline reinforcement learning. LEDE learns a policy to dynamically select the optimal exit layer and speculation length based on the local context of the generated sequence at each step, balancing computational cost and draft quality. Comprehensive evaluations on Llama-2 and Llama-3 models show LEDE achieves up to a $2.0\times$$\sim$$2.7\times$ speedup over autoregressive decoding and and provides an additional 17\% speedup over the static speculative baselines.

</details>


### [47] [DMT-CBT: Longitudinal Therapeutic State Modeling for CBT Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03132)
*Chang Liu,Shuyi Zhang,Changsheng Ma,Yongfeng Tao,Minqiang Yang,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DMT-CBT框架，用于在CBT心理咨询中动态建模跨会话的演变治疗状态，结合多模态行为基础和工具增强干预，并构建了配套的多会话多模态CBT数据集DMTCorpus，实验表明该框架在咨询保真度和治疗联盟方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM心理咨询方法将咨询视为局部回复生成问题，仅关注短文本、单次会话内的共情回复，这与真实心理治疗本质不匹配。真实CBT是一个纵向过程，治疗师需要跨会话持续推断、更新和干预不断演变的治疗状态，涉及多模态推理和跨会话延迟干预效果，需要在部分可观测条件下捕捉治疗状态的纵向演变。

Method: 提出DMT-CBT框架：(1) 跨会话维护结构化治疗状态；(2) 融合多模态行为基础（图像驱动的来访者行为）；(3) 引入工具增强干预以支持自适应治疗推理。基于该框架构建DMTCorpus——一个合成多会话多模态CBT咨询数据集，包含演变的治疗状态、图像基础的来访者行为和跨会话干预连续性。

Result: 实验结果表明，DMT-CBT在咨询保真度和治疗联盟方面表现更优，产生了更有利的纵向情感轨迹，并且比事后提取方法更忠实地保持了治疗状态。

Conclusion: DMT-CBT框架通过显式建模跨会话治疗状态的动态演变、引入多模态信息与工具增强干预，弥合了现有LLM咨询方法与真实CBT临床实践之间的差距，为纵向心理咨询建模提供了新的范式和数据基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) counseling. However, most existing approaches still formulate counseling as a local response generation problem, focusing on empathetic replies within short, text-only, or single-session interactions. We argue that this formulation fundamentally mismatches the nature of real psychotherapy. In clinical CBT, therapy is a longitudinal process in which therapists continuously infer, update, and intervene on evolving therapeutic states across sessions. Realistic CBT further involves multimodal inference and delayed cross-session intervention effects, requiring models to capture longitudinal therapeutic state evolution under partial observability. We propose DMT-CBT, a framework for Dynamic Modeling of evolving Therapeutic states in CBT counseling. DMT-CBT maintains structured therapeutic states across sessions while incorporating multimodal behavioral grounding and tool-augmented intervention to support adaptive therapeutic reasoning. Based on this framework, we construct DMTCorpus, a synthetic multi-session multimodal CBT counseling dataset featuring evolving therapeutic states, image-grounded client behaviors, and cross-session intervention continuity. Experimental results show that DMT-CBT improves counseling fidelity and therapeutic alliance, produces more favorable longitudinal affective trajectories, and preserves therapeutic states more faithfully than post-hoc extraction approaches.

</details>


### [48] [HyperPatch: Sequential Knowledge Editing Under n-ary Structural Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03179)
*Yu-Kai Chan,Wen-Sheng Lien,Dong-Ting Yao,Bo-Kai Ruan,Kwan-Yeung Lin,Hong-Han Shuai,Meng-Fen Chiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyperPatch是一种参数保持的框架，通过超图方法解决LLM知识编辑中的N元结构漂移问题，实现高阶相关性建模、拓扑编辑和结构条件推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要知识编辑来保持时效性，但现实知识本质上是N元的。在非平稳环境中，对复杂关系的顺序更新会诱导N元结构漂移，即把N元事件重新表述为三元组会破坏关系原子性，导致结构条件知识转移失败，常被误诊为参数幻觉。

Method: HyperPatch将顺序知识编辑重新表述为超图流形上的稳定性问题。包含三个阶段：(1)结构先验初始化：通过HGNN上的对比学习建立拓扑感知的嵌入空间以捕获高阶相关性；(2)顺序拓扑编辑：采用双阶段机制，使用基于SimHash的拓扑对齐进行快速冲突解决，以及拓扑LoRA适应来跟踪漂移而不需要骨干网络重新训练；(3)结构条件推理：融合语言和结构流形的全局一致证据。

Result: 在MQuAKE-CF和MQuAKE-T基准测试中，HyperPatch相对于最强基线分别实现了96.24%和21.06%的跳数准确率(H-Acc)相对提升。消融实验表明其在连续N元更新流下具有卓越的可靠性，而标准基于知识图谱的变体因结构不匹配导致H-Acc崩溃高达88.3%。

Conclusion: HyperPatch成功解决了LLM知识编辑中的N元结构漂移和结构条件知识转移失败问题，通过超图方法保持了事件完整性，证明了参数保持框架在处理复杂关系顺序更新时的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on Knowledge Editing (KE) to maintain temporal validity, yet real-world knowledge is inherently n-ary. We demonstrate that in non-stationary environments, sequential updates to complex relations induce N-ary Structural Drift, a phenomenon where the binary reification of n-ary events into triples fractures relational atomicity. This precipitates Structure-Conditioned Knowledge Transfer Failure, a systematic mis-grounding of the retriever frequently misdiagnosed as parametric hallucination. To tackle this, we propose HyperPatch, a parameter-preserving framework that reformulates sequential KE as a stability problem over hypergraph manifolds. HyperPatch preserves event integrity through three phases: (i) Structural Prior Initialization, establishing a topology-aware embedding space via contrastive learning on a Hypergraph Neural Network (HGNN) to capture high-order correlations; (ii) Sequential Topology Editing, utilizing a dual-stage mechanism that employs SimHash-based Topological Alignment for rapid conflict resolution and Topological LoRA Adaptation to track drift without backbone retraining; and (iii) Structure-Conditioned Reasoning, which integrates globally consistent evidence from fused linguistic and structural manifolds. On the MQuAKE-CF and MQuAKE-T benchmarks, HyperPatch achieves relative gains in Hop-wise Accuracy (H-Acc) of 96.24% and 21.06% over the strongest baseline, respectively. Further ablations demonstrate superior reliability under continuous n-ary update streams, whereas the standard KG-based variant suffers H-Acc collapses of up to 88.3% due to structural misalignment.

</details>


### [49] [SenseJudge: Human-Centric Preference-Driven Judgment Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03189)
*Rui Li,Junfeng Liu,Xiangwen Kong,Linhai Xu,Zhifang Sui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SenseJudge是一个基于人类偏好驱动的可定制评判框架，结合SenseBench基准测试，在个性化评判和模型排序任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评判方法依赖固定偏好数据，难以捕捉多样化用户偏好且无法适应真实世界人机对话场景。

Method: 提出SenseJudge框架（基于人类偏好的可定制评判）和SenseBench基准测试（源自真实多轮交互的多样化指令跟随测试集），应用于个性化评判和模型排序两大任务。

Result: 在个性化评判任务中超越其他评判方法和模型，在模型排序中与人类偏好高度一致；位置偏差和一致性分析及消融实验证实了SenseJudge的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SenseJudge有效解决了LLM评判中偏好多样性和场景适应性不足的问题，是一种鲁棒且有效的评判框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) as judges across various scenarios such as assessing model responses is becoming an increasingly accepted paradigm. However, existing judgment approaches often rely on trained judgers using fixed preference data, which tend to overlook diverse user preferences and struggle to adapt to real-world human-AI dialogue scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose SenseJudge, a customizable judgment framework driven by human preferences and SenseBench, a diverse and challenging instruction-following benchmark derived from real-world multi-turn interactions. We applied the automatic judgment framework and benchmark to two tasks: (1) LLMs as personalized judges, and (2) model ranking. We conducted extensive experiments, and the results demonstrate that the SenseJudge framework surpasses other judgment methods and models in the LLMs-as-personalized-judges task and achieves model ranking that aligns with real human sense. Additionally, we conducted analyses on position bias and consistency, alongside ablation studies, which affirmed the robustness of SenseJudge.

</details>


### [50] [MemTrain: Self-Supervised Context Memory Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03197)
*Ziheng Li,Xingrun Xing,Haoqing Wang,Zhi-Hong Deng,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemTrain 是一个针对 LLM 智能体记忆能力的自监督训练框架，通过在维基百科上设计两个耦合代理任务（端到端掩码重构和中间记忆召回）并使用 GRPO 联合优化，显著提升了下游长文本问答和基于搜索的问答任务中记忆密集型推理性能，最高提升 17.67 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的记忆智能体训练方法需要昂贵的高质量标注数据，且训练数据多样性不足，难以覆盖通用的记忆行为，因此亟需一种低成本、通用的记忆能力增强方案。

Method: MemTrain 在未标注的维基百科语料上引入两个耦合代理任务：(1) 端到端掩码重构，要求模型在多轮记忆更新后恢复被遮蔽的实体，从最终结果视角鼓励记忆保持；(2) 中间记忆召回，要求模型利用中间记忆状态重构被遮蔽的历史信息，鼓励忠实压缩和记忆完整性。两个目标使用 GRPO 联合优化。

Result: 在长文本问答和基于搜索的问答基准上的广泛实验表明，MemTrain 在不同模型上均能一致提升下游记忆密集型推理性能，相比直接任务特定微调最高提升 17.67 点。

Conclusion: MemTrain 提供了一种有效的自监督训练框架，能够通用地增强 LLM 智能体的上下文记忆能力，为记忆密集型任务提供了可扩展的预训练方案。

Abstract: Memory is an indispensable capability for long-horizon LLM agents, enabling them to preserve and utilize information accumulated across extended interactions. Existing memory-agent approaches are typically trained end-to-end with reinforcement learning on downstream tasks. However, collecting high-quality annotated problems for memory-intensive scenarios is costly, and the resulting training data often lack sufficient diversity to cover general memory behaviors. In this work, we propose MemTrain, a self-supervised training framework for generally enhancing the context-memory capability of LLM agents for more effective downstream post-training. MemTrain introduces two coupled proxy tasks over unlabeled Wikipedia corpora: (1) an end-to-end masked reconstruction objective, which requires the model to recover masked entities after multiple rounds of memory updates, thereby encouraging memory maintenance from the final outcome perspective; and (2) an intermediate memory recall objective, which requires the model to reconstruct masked historical information using intermediate memory states, encouraging faithful compression and memory completeness throughout the interaction process. The two objectives are jointly optimized using GRPO. Extensive experiments on long-text QA and search-based QA benchmarks demonstrate that MemTrain consistently improves downstream memory-intensive reasoning performance across different models, achieving gains of up to 17.67 points over direct task-specific post-training.

</details>


### [51] [SEA-NLI: Natural Language Inference as a Lens into Southeast Asian Cultural Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03284)
*Peerawat Chomphooyod,Jian Gang Ngui,Yosephine Susanto,Attapol T. Rutherford,Alham Fikri Aji,Sarana Nutanong,Can Udomcharoenchaikit,Peerat Limkonchotiwat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SEA-NLI，一个面向东南亚（SEA）八国、覆盖英语和本地区域语言的原生文化自然语言推理（NLI）基准，用于评估前沿大语言模型在非西方文化语境下的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿大语言模型在西方语境下表现良好，但在东南亚等代表性不足的文化场景中缺乏充分测试。现有NLI基准大多以西方文化为中心、通过翻译获得或仅支持单语，无法有效衡量文化相关的推理能力。

Method: 构建SEA-NLI基准，覆盖八个东南亚国家，使用英语及本地区域语言，内容由母语者验证。在17个编码器和解码器模型上进行系统评估，并通过SEA适配模型、文化感知提示和思维链（CoT）提示等策略分析失败原因。

Result: 所有模型在SEA-NLI上表现均较低，尤其在语言、科技等知识密集型类别上表现不佳。分析表明，失败主要源于模型缺乏东南亚文化知识；SEA适配模型和文化感知提示能提升性能，而CoT提示效果有限。

Conclusion: 现有大语言模型在东南亚文化知识方面存在显著不足，亟需开发更具文化包容性的模型和基准。SEA-NLI为评估和改进模型的文化推理能力提供了重要工具，文化适配策略比通用推理提示更为有效。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs perform well in Western contexts, but remain poorly tested on underrepresented cultures such as those in Southeast Asia (SEA). Existing NLI benchmarks are largely Western-centric, translation-derived, or monolingual, limiting their ability to measure culturally grounded reasoning. We introduce SEA-NLI, a native, culturally grounded NLI benchmark covering eight SEA countries in English and native regional languages, verified by native speakers. Across 17 encoder and decoder models, we observe a low performance from all models, especially for knowledge-intensive categories such as Languages and Science and Technology. Our analysis shows that failure cases mainly stem from missing SEA cultural knowledge: SEA-adapted models and culture-aware prompting improve performance, while CoT prompting offers limited gains.

</details>


### [52] [Multilingual Unlearning in LLMs: Transfer, Dynamics, and Reversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03291)
*Chaoyi Xiang,Olga Ohrimenko,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein,Lea Frermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究多语言大语言模型的知识遗忘，发现跨语言遗忘能力受语言关系影响，遗忘主要在后期解码层实现表层抑制，可通过推理时引导方法恢复大量遗忘知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型遗忘研究主要聚焦英语，缺乏对多语言遗忘机制和跨语言遗忘能力的系统性研究；LLM可能记忆敏感事实，需要有效的无重训练遗忘方法。

Method: 将TOFU基准扩展到5种语言，对不同语言组合进行微调、遗忘和查询实验；通过层间分析研究遗忘机制；提出推理时引导方法恢复遗忘知识。

Result: 跨语言遗忘能力高度可变，与语言共享脚本和语系呈正相关；遗忘主要发生在后期解码层，早期层共享跨语言潜在空间保持完整；推理时引导方法可恢复Qwen 50%和Gemma 90%的遗忘知识。

Conclusion: 大语言模型遗忘并非真正删除知识而是表层抑制；遗忘在共享跨语言潜在空间中具有结构可恢复性；语言关系是影响跨语言遗忘效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can memorize sensitive facts, motivating unlearning methods that remove targeted knowledge without costly retraining. However, unlearning research remains heavily English-centric. We study multilingual unlearning by extending the TOFU benchmark to five languages, and fine-tune, unlearn, and query our models with different permutations of languages. We find that unlearning transfer, the ability of an unlearned model to "forget" facts in languages other than the unlearning language, is highly variable: e.g., it is strongest between languages sharing scripts and families, and we show that the unlearning language predicts which query languages are most likely to yield the strongest transfer. Layer-wise analysis reveals that unlearning leaves the shared cross-lingual latent space largely intact in early layers, instead operating primarily in later decoding layers. This suggests that unlearning does not truly erase knowledge, but rather induces superficial suppression. Exploiting this structure, a single inference-time steering direction reverses much of this suppression across languages, recovering 50% (Qwen) and 90% (Gemma) of the unlearned knowledge.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Ideal Instruction: A Comprehensive Framework for Evaluating LLMs in Realistic Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03318)
*Xuan Yang,Hao Xu,Tingfeng Hui,Hongsheng Xin,Kaike Zhang,Chunxiao Liu,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RUT-Bench，一个专门用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在真实用户工具调用场景下表现的基准测试，涵盖理想与非理想用户行为模式，实验表明现有LLM在真实场景下的整体成功率均低于40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM工具使用评估基准大多基于理想化的用户假设进行模拟，缺乏面向真实体验的评估。这些基准未能涵盖真实用户常见的模糊性、不合作行为及意图变化等特征，导致评估结果与真实场景存在显著差距。

Method: 提出RUT-Bench基准，支持高保真模拟，涵盖单轮和多轮对话中的理想理性模式与异构非理想行为模式。对19个主流的开源和闭源LLM进行了全面评估，测试其在多样化真实用户工具调用场景下的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，没有任何被测试的LLM整体成功率超过40%；几乎所有模型在面对更复杂的非理想用户输入时都出现了明显的性能下降。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在真实世界工具调用场景中的能力仍然有限，尤其在应对模糊、不合作及意图变化的非理想用户行为时表现不佳，亟需进一步提升模型对真实用户行为的适应能力。

Abstract: Despite great advances in tool-use capabilities of large language models (LLMs), existing evaluation benchmarks struggle to fully align with real-world scenarios. Such benchmarks mostly rely on simulated idealized user assumptions and lacks experience-oriented evaluation. These limitations fail to account for the ambiguity, uncooperative behaviors, and shifting intentions characteristic of real-world users. To fill this gap, we propose RUT-Bench, a dedicated benchmark designed to assess LLMs under diverse Real-world User Tool calling scenarios. RUT-Bench supports high-fidelity simulations covering both ideal rational patterns and heterogeneous non-ideal behaviors across single-turn and multi-turn dialogues. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on 19 widely adopted open-source and proprietary LLMs using our benchmark. Experimental results reveal that no tested LLMs achieve an overall success rate above 40%, and nearly all of them experience noticeable performance drops when facing more complicated non-ideal user inputs. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/TorresYangX/RUT-Bench.

</details>


### [54] [Evaluating LLMs' Effectiveness on Real-World Consumer Device Repair Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03331)
*Atm Mizanur Rahman,Md Arid Hasan,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Sharifa Sultana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了针对消费电子设备维修的大语言模型评估基准，包含991个来自Reddit的真实维修问题（涵盖手机、电脑和数据恢复），在英语和孟加拉语环境下测试了6个最先进模型，结果显示LLM在高风险维修任务中仍存在显著不稳定性，特别是手机维修领域错误最多，且孟加拉语表现普遍低于英语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费电子设备维修需要处理不完整的问题描述、硬件特定诊断、可操作的故障排除以及安全关键决策，错误建议可能导致设备损坏、电池危险或永久数据丢失，因此维修任务成为LLM评估的重要但未被充分探索的测试平台。

Method: 创建包含991个真实维修问题的基准数据集（来自Reddit的手机维修、电脑维修和数据恢复问题），每个问题配有技术人员编写的参考解决方案，提供孟加拉语翻译以评估跨语言性能；在英语和孟加拉语环境下使用四个维修特定标准（正确性、完整性、实用性、安全性）评估6个最先进的大语言模型。

Result: LLM能够提供有用的维修协助，但在高风险真实维修任务中仍不可靠；手机维修是最困难和最敏感的领域，所有模型在电路板级诊断、维修优先级排序和安全恢复程序方面都存在重大错误；孟加拉语响应普遍表现比英语差；GPT-5.4在所有评估模型中表现最佳。

Conclusion: LLM在真实世界维修任务中需要严格的评估和明确的安全保障措施；模型在电路板级诊断、维修优先级和安全恢复程序等关键环节仍存在显著缺陷，特别是在手机维修领域，需要进一步改进以提高可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Consumer device repair is an important but underexplored testbed for large language models (LLMs). Repair tasks require reasoning over incomplete problem descriptions, hardware-specific diagnostics, actionable troubleshooting, and safety-critical decisions, where incorrect advice can cause device damage, battery hazards, or permanent data loss. We introduce a benchmark of 991 real-world repair questions from Reddit spanning phone repair, computer repair, and data recovery, each paired with technician-written reference solutions, and provide Bangla translations to evaluate cross-lingual performance. We evaluate six state-of-the-art LLMs in English and Bangla using four repair-specific criteria: correctness, completeness, practicality, and safety. Our results show that while LLMs can provide useful repair assistance, they remain unreliable for high-risk real-world repair tasks without rigorous evaluation and explicit safety safeguards. Phone repair is the most difficult and safety-sensitive domain, and all models make substantial errors in board-level diagnosis, repair prioritization, and safe recovery procedures. Across domains and models, Bangla responses consistently perform worse than English responses. Among the evaluated models, GPT-5.4 performs best overall.

</details>


### [55] [EntSQL: A Benchmark for Grounding Text-to-SQL in Long-Context Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03363)
*Chengxi Liao,Tao Xu,Zulong Chen,Chuanfei Xu,Yiyan Wang,Xinyun Wang,Yanlong Zhang,Xiaojun Chen,Zhibo Yang,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EntSQL，一个面向企业场景的Text-to-SQL基准测试，专注于评估模型在私有商业文档上的长上下文知识锚定能力。实验表明，现有最强系统在提供长文档时准确率仅为15.9%，揭示了企业知识驱动的SQL生成任务的巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL基准（如Spider、BIRD、Spider 2.0）主要关注模式泛化、大规模数据库和真实工作流，但忽略了企业场景中SQL生成高度依赖私有商业知识（如内部指标、报表规范、组织规则）的现实。企业场景下，模型需要从专有商业文档中获取领域知识来生成正确的SQL，这一能力尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建了EntSQL基准，包含1,066个中英双语对齐的语义示例，覆盖五个商业领域。大多数示例需要超越问题和模式本身的领域知识，并涉及复杂SQL结构。该基准通过提供长篇商业文档，评估模型在私有企业知识上进行长上下文锚定并生成SQL的能力。

Result: 在英文输入下，最佳评估系统在提供长篇文档时准确率仅为15.9%，表明将SQL生成锚定于企业知识是一项极具挑战性的任务，现有LLM能力远未满足企业级需求。

Conclusion: EntSQL填补了企业场景Text-to-SQL评估的空白，揭示了当前模型在利用私有商业知识生成SQL方面的严重不足，为未来研究提供了具有挑战性的新基准，推动企业级Text-to-SQL系统的发展。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL enables natural language access to databases, and recent LLMs have substantially advanced its capabilities. Existing benchmarks such as Spider, BIRD, and Spider~2.0 evaluate schema generalization, large-scale databases, and realistic workflows, but largely overlook enterprise scenarios where SQL generation depends on private business knowledge, such as internal metrics, reporting conventions, and organizational rules. We introduce EntSQL, an enterprise-oriented Text-to-SQL benchmark for evaluating long-context grounding over proprietary business documents. EntSQL contains 1,066 aligned Chinese-English semantic examples across five business domains, with most examples requiring domain knowledge beyond the question and schema and involving complex SQL structures. On English inputs, the best evaluated system reaches only 15.9\% when long-form documents are provided, highlighting the difficulty of grounding SQL generation in enterprise knowledge.

</details>


### [56] [Large Language Models Are Overconfident in Their Own Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03437)
*Mario Sanz-Guerrero,Manuel Mager,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 指令微调的LLMs校准性比基础模型差，chat模板通过"所有权偏差"加剧了这一问题；提出将模型回答作为用户输入的简单推理策略，无需重新训练即可提升校准度高达26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究表明指令微调的LLMs比基础预训练模型校准性更差，但chat模板对对话LLM校准的影响知之甚少，需要解耦后训练算法和聊天格式的影响来理解这一机制的根源。

Method: 通过实验将后训练算法和聊天格式的影响解耦；在六个开放权重LLMs、三个基准和三种置信度提取方法上进行广泛实验；提出简单的推理时策略：将模型的回答作为用户输入进行置信度提取。

Result: 指令微调根本上损害校准；chat模板通过"所有权偏差"加剧问题，模型对自己回答的置信度比用户提供的相同回答高出高达26%；提出的策略显著减少过自信，提高校准度高达26%。

Conclusion: 无需重新训练即可缩小基础模型和指令微调模型之间的校准差距，证明了所有权偏差是miscalibration的关键机制。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) are less well calibrated than their base pre-trained counterparts. However, little is known about the frequently used chat template's effect on the calibration of conversational LLMs. In this work, we investigate the mechanisms driving this miscalibration by decoupling the effects of the post-training algorithm and the chat format. We find that, while instruction tuning fundamentally harms calibration, the chat template aggravates the issue through an "ownership bias" -- models are significantly more confident in their own answers than in identical answers provided by a user. Extensive experiments across six recent open-weight LLMs, three benchmarks, and three confidence elicitation methods show that models assign up to 26% higher confidence to their own responses. Leveraging this insight, we propose a simple inference-time strategy: framing the model's answer as user input during confidence elicitation. This approach significantly reduces overconfidence and improves calibration by up to 26% without the need for retraining, narrowing the gap between base and instruction-tuned models.

</details>


### [57] [Building Reliable Long-Form Generation via Hallucination Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03628)
*Lin Li,Georgia Channing,Suhaas M Bhat,Gabriel Davis Jones,Yarin Gal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SHARS框架，通过在长文本生成过程中实时检测并拒绝幻觉片段，结合语义不确定性检测器和重新采样机制，有效缓解幻觉雪球效应，提升生成内容的忠实性和事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长文本生成中易出现幻觉累积问题，早期错误会传播并放大，严重影响可靠性。现有方法多依赖外部资源，而SHARS旨在实现无需外部辅助的自纠正机制。

Method: 提出Segment-wise HAllucination Rejection Sampling (SHARS)框架：在生成过程中使用语义不确定性作为幻觉检测器，识别并拒绝幻觉片段，通过重新采样直至生成忠实内容，并保留自信信息作为后续生成的基准。

Result: 在标准化幻觉基准测试中显著降低长文本生成中的幻觉率，同时保持甚至提升生成内容的丰富性；无需外部资源即可实现自我纠正。

Conclusion: SHARS通过推理时自我纠正机制有效缓解幻觉累积，兼容未来与外部资源结合扩展，为提升长文本生成可靠性提供实用方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in open-ended text generation, yet they remain prone to hallucinating incorrect or unsupported content, which undermines their reliability. This issue is exacerbated in long-form generation due to hallucination snowballing, a phenomenon where early errors propagate and compound into subsequent outputs. To address this challenge, we propose a novel inference-time hallucination mitigation framework, named Segment-wise HAllucination Rejection Sampling (SHARS), which uses an arbitrary hallucination detector to identify and reject hallucinated segments during generation and resample until faithful content is produced. By retaining only confident information and building subsequent generations upon it, the framework mitigates hallucination accumulation and enhances factual consistency. To instantiate this framework, we adopt semantic uncertainty as the detector and introduce several vital modifications to address its limitations and better adapt it to long-form text. Our method enables models to self-correct hallucinations without requiring external resources such as web search or knowledge bases, while remaining compatible with them for future extensions. Empirical evaluations on standardized hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that our method substantially reduces hallucinations in long-form generation while preserving or even improving the informativeness of generation. Code is available at: https://github.com/TreeLLi/hallucination-rejection-sampling.

</details>


### [58] [Framing Migration News with LLMs: Structured CoT as a Support for Human Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03761)
*David Alonso del Barrio,Jing Wen,Daniel Gatica-Perez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于开源本地部署LLM的结构化思维链（SCoT）提示方法，用于可解释的框架分析任务，在迁移新闻数据集上评估显示性能提升且保持单GPU可行性，人类评估表明SCoT解释逻辑性强（平均4.1/5分）并能引发对初始解释的反思。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 媒体学者需要准确、透明、可审计且资源受限下可访问的工具来分析迁移新闻的框架。现有LLM方法依赖专有API和大型模型，存在数据隐私、可复现性和研究公平性等担忧。

Method: 提出结构化思维链（SCoT）提示方法，使用Llama3-8B作为本地可部署的开源LLM，通过分步推理和预定义框架类别提供可解释的框架分析辅助工具，支持审计模型输出和探索替代解释。

Result: SCoT在迁移新闻数据集上优于零样本和少样本基线，单GPU即可运行。人类评估显示SCoT解释平均逻辑得分4.1/5分（存在文本间差异），能促进对初始解释的反思。

Conclusion: 结构化推理能提高模型输出的可追溯性并支持批判性解释，但也可能微妙地影响人类判断。通过本地部署和强调人机循环交互，该工作为研究社会影响媒体叙事的负责任且可访问的计算工具做出贡献。

Abstract: Frame analysis of migration news is a socially consequential task: media scholars and researchers who study how migration is narrated need tools that are not only accurate, but transparent, auditable, and accessible within the resource constraints typical of academic research groups. Existing LLM-based approaches rely on proprietary APIs and large models that raise concerns about data privacy, reproducibility and equitable access among media researchers. This work studies how a locally deployable open-source LLM can support interpretable frame analysis as an assistive tool. We introduce a Structured Chain-of-Thought (SCoT) prompting approach using Llama3-8B, enabling step-by-step justifications grounded in predefined framing categories. This structured design allows users to audit model outputs and examine alternative interpretations in a task that is inherently subjective. We evaluate our approach on a dataset of migration-related news and show that SCoT improves classification performance over zero-shot and few-shot baselines while remaining feasible on a single GPU. Then, we conduct a human-centered evaluation in which annotators assess the coherence and influence of "the model's reasoning". Results indicate that SCoT explanations are generally perceived as logical (mean score 4.1/5, though with notable variation across texts) and can prompt reflection on initial interpretations, even when disagreement persists. Our findings highlight both the potential and risks of LLM-assisted frame analysis. While structured reasoning can increase the traceability of model outputs and support critical interpretation, it can also influence human judgment in subtle ways. By enabling local deployment and emphasizing human-in-the-loop interaction, this work contributes to discussions on responsible and accessible computational tools for the study of socially impactful media narratives.

</details>


### [59] [HybridThinker: Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning via Compressed Memory and Transient Thought Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03768)
*Xin Liu,Runsong Zhao,Xinyu Liu,Junhao Ruan,Pengcheng Huang,Shichao Dong,Chunyang Xiao,Chenglong Wang,Changliang Li,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HybridThinker提出了一种混合训练方案，在CoT压缩中同时保留记忆token的紧凑表示和临时保留的思考步骤细节，通过部分掩码思考步骤强制模型学习压缩与检索能力，在4个推理基准上匹配未压缩基线性能，平均准确率超越现有CoT压缩方法5.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展的思维链（CoT）虽然能提升LLM推理能力，但带来显著的计算和内存开销。现有CoT压缩方法通过将思考步骤压缩为紧凑的记忆token表示来缓解此问题，但细粒度信息的丢失会导致后续推理步骤更容易出错。如何在不牺牲细粒度信息的前提下实现高效压缩是核心挑战。

Method: HybridThinker的核心方法包括两部分：(1) 在推理时，除了保留压缩的记忆token表示外，还临时保留思考步骤以提供细粒度细节；(2) 提出混合训练方案——在训练过程中，只有部分思考步骤对后续步骤直接可见（通过注意力机制），其余思考步骤被掩码，从而强制模型学会通过记忆token进行压缩和信息检索，避免模型在训练时绕过记忆token直接从原始步骤获取信息。

Result: 在4个推理基准上，HybridThinker匹配了未压缩基线的性能，在相似推理时间下，平均准确率较现有CoT压缩方法提升5.8个百分点。消融实验验证了临时思考步骤保留和混合训练方案两者均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: HybridThinker通过混合保留策略和混合训练方案，成功在CoT压缩与推理质量之间取得平衡，解决了现有压缩方法中细粒度信息丢失和记忆token训练不足的问题，为高效LLM推理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Extended chain-of-thought (CoT) traces improve LLM reasoning but incur substantial computational and memory costs. While existing CoT compression methods mitigate this by condensing thought steps into compact representations via memory tokens and retaining only these representations at inference time, the loss of fine-grained information makes subsequent steps more error-prone. To alleviate this, we propose \textbf{HybridThinker}, where in addition to preserved these representations, thought steps are also temporarily retained to provide fine-grained details. However, we observe that naively keeping thought steps accessible to subsequent steps \emph{during training} lets the model bypass memory tokens by retrieving information directly from these steps, leaving the model's ability to compress and retrieve information through memory tokens insufficiently trained. We therefore introduce a hybrid training scheme, in which only some thought steps are directly accessible through attention to subsequent steps, while the other thought steps are masked, forcing the model to use memory tokens for compression and retrieval. Across 4 reasoning benchmarks, HybridThinker matches the uncompressed baseline, advancing the state of the art in CoT compression by 5.8 points on average accuracy with similar inference time. Ablation studies confirm that both temporary thought-step retention and the hybrid training scheme contribute to these gains.

</details>


### [60] [Reasoning over Grammar: Can Synthetic Linguistic Reasoning Traces Enhance Low-Resource Machine Translation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03782)
*Renhao Pei,Yihong Liu,Sampo Pyysalo,Hinrich Schütze,Shaoxiong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用语言学推理链（linguistic reasoning traces）来提升大语言模型在极低资源语言机器翻译中表现的方法，发现将推理链作为推理时的引导（ICL）效果最佳，而作为训练数据（SFT/RFT）效果有限且不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）通过上下文学习（ICL）为极低资源语言的机器翻译提供了新途径，但模型在翻译过程中往往无法有效利用语法信息。受链式思维推理（chain-of-thought）进展的启发，作者希望探索结构化的语言学分析和语法推理中间步骤是否能改善低资源机器翻译的效果。

Method: 提出了一种自动化流水线，基于通用依存树库（Universal Dependencies treebanks）、词典和语法规则库，逐步生成语言学推理链。这些推理链在三种设置下进行评估：上下文学习（ICL）、监督微调（SFT）和强化微调（RFT），以锡伯语和Chintang语作为测试案例。

Result: 实验结果表明，语言学推理链作为推理时引导（ICL设置）最为有效，可靠的句子级推理链在大多数模型、语言和评价指标上均显著提升了翻译性能。相反，将推理链作为训练数据（SFT/RFT）仅带来较小且不一致的增益——模型学会了生成推理链的格式，但经常产生错误内容。

Conclusion: 当给定可靠的语言学分析时，LLMs能够有效利用语法信息来改善低资源机器翻译；然而，让模型自主学习生成正确的语言学分析仍是一个重大瓶颈，限制了训练阶段微调方法的效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising approach to machine translation (MT) for extremely low-resource languages by incorporating linguistic resources through in-context learning. However, LLMs often struggle to apply grammatical information effectively during translation. Inspired by recent progress in chain-of-thought reasoning, we investigate whether low-resource MT can benefit from structured intermediate steps of linguistic analysis and grammatical reasoning. We propose a pipeline for automatically generating step-by-step linguistic reasoning traces from Universal Dependencies treebanks, dictionaries, and grammar-rule banks. We evaluate these traces in three settings: in-context learning (ICL), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT), on Xibe and Chintang as test cases. Our results show that linguistic reasoning traces are most effective as inference-time guidance: in ICL, reliable sentence-specific traces substantially improve translation performance across most models, languages, and metrics. In contrast, using the linguistic reasoning traces as training data yields smaller and less consistent gains, as models learn the trace format but often generate erroneous content. These findings suggest that LLMs can leverage grammatical information for low-resource MT when given reliable linguistic analyses, while learning to generate such analyses remains a major bottleneck.

</details>


### [61] [Exploring Adversarial Robustness and Safety Alignment in Multilingual Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03793)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在12种语言上的对抗鲁棒性和多模态安全性，发现浅层多语言适配（如指令微调阶段的翻译数据）会产生虚假的"安全假象"（safety-by-failure），而贯穿训练全阶段的深度多语言整合（如Qwen3-VL）才能实现真正的跨语言安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于MLLM鲁棒性的研究主要集中于英语任务，多语言行为尚未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究MLLM在多语言场景下的对抗鲁棒性和多模态安全性问题。

Method: 在12种多样化语言上评估开源MLLM的对抗鲁棒性和安全性。具体方法包括：(1) 使用基于梯度的对抗攻击生成对抗图像，测试跨语言迁移性；(2) 分别通过文本和图像（排版内容）两种模态嵌入有害指令，分析不同语言下的安全行为差异；(3) 对比浅层多语言适配（仅指令微调阶段）与深度多语言整合（贯穿训练全阶段，如Qwen3-VL）的模型表现。

Result: (1) 对抗图像具有强跨语言迁移性——在一种语言上优化的对抗图像可导致其他语言下的模型失败；(2) 文本模态中，语言基础更强的语言更容易触发不安全输出，弱语言则产生较少不安全内容；(3) 图像模态中，英语文字能被视觉编码器可靠识别和执行，而非英语文字几乎无法被解析；(4) 低资源语言看似更安全，实为理解失败造成的假象（safety-by-failure）；(5) Qwen3-VL等深度多语言整合模型展现出真正的跨语言安全对齐，能在各语言中保持主动拒绝。

Conclusion: 浅层多语言适配（如翻译指令数据微调）仅产生表层理解，在低资源语言中制造虚假的安全感；只有在训练全阶段深度整合多语言能力，才能实现真正的多语言安全对齐，而非以理解失败掩盖安全问题。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models integrate visual perception into language reasoning, introducing a continuous attack surface susceptible to adversarial attacks. Prior work on MLLM robustness has focused largely on English-centric tasks, leaving multilingual behaviour unexplored. We address this gap through a systematic study of adversarial robustness and multimodal safety across 12 diverse languages, evaluating open-source MLLMs that acquire multilingual capability through instruction tuning. Gradient-based attacks reveal a transferable multilingual vulnerability: adversarial images optimized in one language continue to induce failure in others, demonstrating strong cross-lingual transferability. Multilingual safety further varies with how effectively a model retrieves or interprets harmful instructions. When harmful intent is issued through text, languages with stronger linguistic grounding more often elicit misuse-enabling responses, while weaker languages produce fewer unsafe outputs. When embedded in the image as typographic content, English scripts are reliably recognised and followed, whereas non-English scripts are rarely parsed by the vision encoder. Lower-resource languages may therefore appear safer, but this is an artefact of comprehension and visual-grounding failures rather than genuine alignment, a phenomenon we term safety-by-failure. In contrast, MLLMs that build multilingual capability throughout their training stages rather than only at instruction tuning, such as Qwen3-VL, exhibit genuine cross-lingual safety, maintaining active refusal across languages rather than masking comprehension failure. Shallow multilingual adaptation, such as fine-tuning on translated instruction data, may produce surface-level understanding that creates illusory safety in low-resource languages; deeper integration across training stages leads to genuine multilingual safety alignment.

</details>


### [62] [Synthesize and Reward -- Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step Tool Use in Live Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03892)
*Ibrahim Abdelaziz,Asim Munawar,Kinjal Basu,Maxwell Crouse,Chulaka Gunasekara,Suneet Katrekar,Pavan Kapanipathi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PROVE是一个训练LLM进行多步工具编排的框架，通过提供状态化MCP服务器库、自动化数据合成管道和多组件程序化奖励，解决了环境构建成本高、查询与实际状态不匹配、RL奖励导致冗余调用等三个障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练LLM进行多步工具编排受限于三个耦合障碍：1)真实状态化执行环境构建成本高；2)合成训练查询常与服务器实际状态脱节导致调用失败；3)基于召回的RL奖励鼓励冗余的工具调用模式。

Method: PROVE包含三个贡献：(1)20个暴露343个工具的状态化MCP服务器库，支持会话级状态隔离的实时执行RL训练；(2)基于依赖图引导和实时采样服务器状态的自动化数据合成管道，生成验证的多轮工具调用轨迹；(3)多组件程序化奖励系统，包含递进有效性评分、依赖感知覆盖度、自适应效率惩罚、工具名称信号和参数匹配奖励。

Result: 在GRPO训练下，使用~13K示例训练Qwen3-4B/8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Granite-4.1-8B四个模型。PROVE在BFCL Multi-Turn、tau2-bench和T-Eval上分别提升10.2、6.8和6.5分。

Conclusion: 紧凑的程序化奖励可在两个模型家族上对多步工具编排产生一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Training LLMs to orchestrate multi-step tool calls is held back by three coupled obstacles: realistic stateful execution environments are costly to build, synthetic training queries are often detached from the server's actual state (so the generated tool calls fail to execute), and recall-based RL rewards incentivize verbose tool-calling patterns. We present PROVE (Programmatic Rewards On Verified Environments), a framework with three contributions: (1) a library of 20 stateful MCP (Model Context Protocol) servers exposing 343 tools, enabling live-execution RL training with session-scoped state isolation; (2) an automated data synthesis pipeline that generates validated multi-turn tool-call trajectories against these servers via dependency-graph-guided conversation simulation grounded in live-sampled server state, so every generated query references entities that actually exist; and (3) a multi-component programmatic reward - graduated validity scoring, dependency-aware coverage, an adaptive efficiency penalty with a complexity-scaled call budget, a tool-name signal, and an argument-value matching bonus - requiring no external judge model. We train four models (Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Granite-4.1-8B) with GRPO using identical reward hyperparameters and ~13K training examples; only learning rate is tuned per model family from a three-point sweep. On BFCL Multi-Turn, tau2-bench, and T-Eval, PROVE yields improvements of up to +10.2, +6.8, and +6.5 points respectively, demonstrating that a compact programmatic reward yields consistent gains on multi-step tool orchestration across two model families.

</details>


### [63] [Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering for Efficient and Controllable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03965)
*Yu Xia,Zhouhang Xie,Xin Xu,Byungkyu Kang,Prarit Lamba,Xiang Gao,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ACTS（Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering），一种基于马尔可夫决策过程的推理引导框架，通过控制器智能体在推理过程中自适应地引导冻结的推理模型，实现预算感知的高效推理，在大幅节省token的同时保持与完整推理链相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过扩展思维链推理提升了最终答案准确率，但存在token使用效率低、推理时缺乏可控性等问题。现有高效推理方法（如缩短、提前停止、压缩推理轨迹）对模型"如何思考"的控制是隐式的，无法显式地引导推理策略。

Method: 将推理引导建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）：一个控制器智能体（controller agent）在每一步观察推理轨迹和剩余思考预算，输出由推理策略和引导短语组成的引导动作，以启动推理模型的下一步生成。控制器从构建的合成引导轨迹（含多预算增强）初始化，并通过强化学习（预算条件奖励塑形）进一步优化。推理模型本身保持冻结，生成连续性得以保留。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，ACTS在大幅节省token的情况下匹配了完整思维链推理的性能，并支持在不同推理模型和任务间实现可控制的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: ACTS提供了一种显式、可控的推理引导范式，通过控制器智能体实现预算感知的策略控制，在不修改推理模型的前提下兼顾推理效率与准确性，为推理时的高效可控推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models improve final-answer accuracy through extended chain-of-thought reasoning, but often spend tokens inefficiently and offer little inference-time control. Existing efficient reasoning methods control thinking length by shortening, early-stopping, or compressing traces, leaving how the model thinks implicit. In this paper, we propose Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering (ACTS), which formulates reasoning steering as a Markov decision process where a controller agent adaptively steers a frozen reasoner during inference. At each step, the controller observes the reasoning trace and remaining thinking budget, then issues a steering action consisting of a reasoning strategy and a steering phrase that initiates the next reasoner step. This enables budget-aware strategy control for efficient reasoning while preserving the reasoner's generation continuity. We initialize the controller agent from our constructed synthetic steering trajectories with multi-budget augmentation, and further optimize it via reinforcement learning with budget-conditioned reward shaping. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that ACTS matches full-thinking performance with substantial token savings, and enables controllable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across different reasoners and tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/Andree-9/ACTS.

</details>


### [64] [AlignAtt4LLM: Fast AlignAtt for Decoder-Only LLMs at IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03967)
*Quentin Fuxa,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlignAtt4LLM，一个用于IWSLT 2026英到德/意/中同声语音翻译的级联系统，首次将AlignAtt策略应用于decoder-only LLM（Gemma-4），在英到德和英到意方向上优于基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AlignAtt策略依赖encoder-decoder的cross-attention来实现同步翻译中的读写策略，但decoder-only LLM中不存在这种cross-attention结构，因此需要新的方法将AlignAtt适配到decoder-only LLM上，以实现高质量的同步语音翻译。

Method: 系统采用同步级联架构：Qwen3-ASR结合强制对齐生成增量更新的源语言转录，Gemma-4 E4B-it在MT侧使用AlignAtt策略翻译前缀。为适配decoder-only LLM，提出了四项关键技术：(1)在prompt中引入显式源文本span；(2)离线选择翻译专用的注意力头（alignment heads）；(3)对draft-to-source注意力块进行选择性qk-fast重放；(4)运行时query/key捕获，确保模型输出比特级一致。

Result: 在IWSLT 2026开发集上，AlignAtt4LLM在英到德和英到意两个欧洲目标语言上，无论在约2秒的低延迟还是低于4秒（CU-LongYAAL）的高延迟场景中，均优于提供的基线系统。英到中结果较为混合。

Conclusion: AlignAtt4LLM方法不绑定于Gemma-4模型，仅需确定性prompt布局、校准的注意力头和query/key捕获三个条件，因此同一策略可重新应用于更强的翻译专用decoder-only MT骨干网络，以提升非欧洲目标语言的翻译效果。

Abstract: We describe AlignAtt4LLM, an IWSLT 2026 simultaneous speech translation system for English to German, Italian, and Chinese. The system is a synchronous cascade: Qwen3-ASR with forced alignment produces an incrementally updated source transcript, and Gemma-4 E4B-it translates that prefix under an MT-side AlignAtt policy.
  To our knowledge, this is the first application of AlignAtt to a decoder-only LLM, where the encoder-decoder cross-attention used by earlier AlignAtt systems is absent. We recover a usable policy by proposing (1) an explicit source span in the prompt, (2) offline selection of translation-specific alignment heads, (3) selective qk-fast replay of the draft-to-source attention block, and (4) runtime query/key capture that preserves model outputs bit-identically.
  On the IWSLT 2026 development set, AlignAtt4LLM outperforms the supplied baselines for the European target languages, English to German and English to Italian, in both the low-latency regime around 2 seconds and the high-latency regime below 4 seconds CU-LongYAAL. Results for English to Chinese are more mixed, but the method is not tied to Gemma-4: because AlignAtt4LLM only requires a deterministic prompt layout, calibrated attention heads, and query/key capture, the same policy can be reapplied to stronger translation-focused decoder-only MT backbones for non-European target languages.

</details>


### [65] [Quantifying Faithful Confidence Expression in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03969)
*Areeb Gani,Asal Meskin,Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新框架来系统量化大推理模型的置信度校准问题，发现忠实置信度表达是LRMs的重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠置信度传达对LLM可信度至关重要；现有测量范式在LRMs的长链式输出上泛化不佳；LRMs的推理轨迹缺乏清晰步骤边界和一致结构。

Method: 框架分析三种内部不确定性（标记概率、隐藏状态、采样响应一致性）下的语言决断性；设计前缀条件采样方法控制轨迹间条件和结构变化。

Result: 对多个领先模型、数据集和提示词测试显示：忠实置信度表达是重大挑战；推理行为不自动转化为更好FC；提示干预在推理设置中无效；不同置信度估计器评估结果分歧，揭示先前方法脆弱性。

Conclusion: FC已成为LRMs的独特可靠性和对齐目标，特别随着LRMs越来越多部署在高风险环境中。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty communication is critical to the trustworthiness of LLMs, yet faithful calibration (FC)--the alignment between models' intrinsic and (linguistically) expressed confidence--is a persistent failure mode. This challenge is key for large reasoning models (LRMs), whose extended reasoning traces are often interpreted by users as evidence of deliberation, competence, and confidence. Despite the importance of FC and wide usage of LRMs, the extent to which LRMs can faithfully express their confidence remains poorly understood. Moreover, the prevailing paradigm to measure FC does not generalize well to the long chain-of-thought outputs generated by LRMs, which tend to lack clear step boundaries, involve inconsistent step structure, and encode complex conditional dependencies throughout the trace--complicating estimation of intrinsic confidence. To address this challenge, we introduce a novel framework to systematically quantify FC of LRMs. Our framework analyzes linguistic decisiveness relative to three sources of internal uncertainty, based on token probabilities, hidden states, and sampled response consistency. We also devise a prefix-conditioned sampling approach to control for conditional and structural variation across traces. Applying our framework to a diverse suite of leading models, datasets, and prompts, we find that faithful confidence expression is a significant challenge for LRMs. Reasoning behaviors do not automatically translate to improved FC, and prompt interventions for non-reasoning models do not improve faithfulness in the reasoning setting. Different confidence estimators further produce divergent assessments of the same traces, revealing fragility in prior evaluation methodologies. Taken together, our work establishes FC as a distinct reliability and alignment target for LRMs, particularly as such systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes contexts.

</details>


### [66] [From Script to Semantics: Prompting Strategies for African NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03304)
*Anuj Tiwari,Terry Oko-odion,Hannah Nwokocha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在纯提示（无微调）条件下，大语言模型在低资源非洲语言（斯瓦希里语、约鲁巴语、豪萨语）自然语言推理任务中的表现，发现对比提示（Contrastive Prompting）是最稳定有效的策略，且精心设计的提示足以超越使用少样本和思维链的更强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多语言环境下的评估日益增多，但其在低资源非洲语言上的推理行为（尤其是在纯提示、无微调条件下）仍鲜有研究。现有工作未充分探索提示策略对低资源语言NLI性能的影响。

Method: 基于AfriXNLI基准，在斯瓦希里语、约鲁巴语和豪萨语上评估五种提示策略：基线（零样本）、脚本感知、语言特定、对比提示和原生标签自翻译（NL-STP），使用Llama3.2-3B和Gemma3-4B两个中等规模开源模型。为隔离提示设计的影响，排除了少样本示例和思维链推理的干扰。

Result: （1）不同策略间类别级性能差异显著，部分配置出现严重的中性类崩溃和高预测偏斜；（2）对比提示是最可靠、跨语言和跨模型稳定提升的策略，在类别行为平衡和整体准确率提升方面表现最优；（3）精心构造的纯提示即可超越配备少样本和思维链的更强基线。

Conclusion: 提示构造对低资源语言多语言NLI至关重要，语言感知的决策结构化方法能够在资源匮乏场景下有效增强模型的鲁棒性，无需微调或复杂推理增强即可获得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated in multilingual settings, yet their inference behavior in low-resource African languages remains underexplored especially under pure prompting without fine-tuning. We present a systematic study of prompting strategies for Natural Language Inference (NLI) in Swahili, Yoruba, and Hausa using the AfriXNLI benchmark. We evaluate five prompting strategies Baseline (zero-shot), Script-Aware, Language Specific, Contrastive, and Native-Label Self-Translation (NL-STP) across two mid-sized open weight models (Llama3.2-3B and Gemma3-4B). To isolate the effect of prompt design, the effect of few-shot examples and Chain-of-Thought reasoning is eliminated in our study. We find a significant difference in performance of class wise across strategies with highly neutral class collapse and high prediction skew in some configurations. Contrastive prompting proves to be the most reliable and steadily improving strategy over language and model and has better balance of class behavior and balance of overall accuracy gains. Notably, well-constructed prompts are sufficient to beat more powerful baselines that are provided with few-shot prompts and Chain-of-Thought prompts. We have found that prompt formulation is essential to multilingual NLI with low-resource languages and that language aware decision structuring can be used to meaningfully enhance robustness in resource challenged settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [67] [Visual Graph Scaffolds for Structural Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02673)
*Runlin Lei,Xiaokui Xiao,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将图结构作为LLM的内部推理辅助（类似人类思维导图），而非仅作为外部知识源。研究发现，在多跳问答任务中，视觉图引导比文本化的图引导更有效，即使去除直接答案提示后仍能保持优势，表明图应被视为组织推理的视觉脚手架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要将图作为外部知识源在测试时提供给LLM，但图对LLM的价值不仅在于提供信息，还在于组织推理。受人类使用图结构思维导图组织分支与汇聚思维的启发，作者探索图能否作为LLM内部推理辅助形式，以提升多跳问答中的推理质量。

Method: 在多跳问答任务上，将教师提供的推理轨迹重写为图思维导图，用于指导学生模型。对比研究了两种图引导方式：1）将图结构展平为文本输入；2）以视觉形式提供图引导。在去除直接答案提示的抽象引导设置下，评估推理效率和答案质量，并进一步验证在监督微调（SFT）和基于KL散度的知识蒸馏后的效果。

Result: 1）存在显著的模态差距：当图结构被展平为文本且去除直接答案提示后，推理效率和答案质量均大幅下降，收益有限。2）视觉图引导在无直接答案线索时仍然有效，且其优势在监督微调和KL蒸馏后持续存在。

Conclusion: 图结构不应仅被研究为LLM的外部知识结构，更应被视为组织推理的视觉脚手架（visual scaffolds）。视觉形式的图引导在抽象推理场景下显著优于文本化图引导，为图增强LLM推理提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: Graphs have been used to enhance large language models (LLMs) for structured reasoning, mostly as external knowledge sources are provided to models at test time. In this paper, we take a different view: the value of graphs for LLMs lie not only in supplying information, but also in organizing reasoning. Inspired by how humans use graph-structured mind maps to organize branching and converging thoughts, we ask whether graphs can serve as an internal form of reasoning assistance. We study this question on multi-hop question answering tasks, where teacher-provided reasoning traces are rewritten as graph mind maps and used to guide a student model. Our experiments reveal a clear modality gap. When graph structures are flattened into text, their benefits become limited once direct answer hints are removed. Under this abstract guidance setting, both reasoning efficiency and answer quality degrade substantially. In contrast, visual graph guidance remains effective without direct answer clues, and its advantage persists after supervised fine-tuning and KL-based distillation. The above findings support the claim that graphs should be studied not only as external knowledge structures for LLMs, but also as visual scaffolds for organizing reasoning.

</details>


### [68] [ChatHealthAI: Aligning Electronic Health Record Representations with Large Language Models for Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02802)
*Bo-Hong Wang,Baicheng Peng,Ruilin Wang,Jun Bai,Ziyang Song,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChatHealthAI，一个多模态推理框架，通过将预训练的EHR基础模型的结构化电子健康记录（EHR）表示与冻结的大语言模型（LLM）语义空间对齐，实现了兼具临床预测准确性和可解释自然语言推理的临床决策支持系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在自然语言推理方面表现出色，但难以有效建模结构化的纵向电子健康记录（EHRs）；而EHR基础模型虽能学习预测性患者表示，却缺乏可解释的基于语言的推理能力。两者各有优劣，亟需弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出ChatHealthAI多模态推理框架，核心方法包括：(1) 利用预训练EHR基础模型提取结构化纵向EHR的患者表示；(2) 通过任务感知重采样器（task-aware resampler）将EHR表示与冻结LLM的语义空间进行对齐；(3) 将纵向患者表示与精炼的临床事件描述相结合，实现基于临床依据的自然语言推理。

Result: 在EHRSHOT基准的三个临床预测任务上进行评估，ChatHealthAI在提升推理质量和可解释性的同时，保持了具有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: 将EHR基础模型与预训练LLM相结合是可行的，能够实现兼具准确性和可解释性的临床预测，为临床决策支持提供了新的多模态融合范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong natural-language reasoning abilities for clinical decision support, but struggle to effectively model structured longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs). In contrast, EHR foundation models can learn predictive patient representations, yet lack interpretable language-based reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose ChatHealthAI, a multimodal reasoning framework that aligns structured EHR representations from a pretrained EHR foundation model with the semantic space of a frozen LLM through a task-aware resampler. By integrating longitudinal patient representations with refined clinical event descriptions, ChatHealthAI enables clinically grounded natural-language reasoning while maintaining accurate patient prediction. We evaluated ChatHealthAI on three clinical predictive tasks from the EHRSHOT benchmark. Results show that ChatHealthAI improves reasoning quality and interpretability while preserving competitive predictive performance. These findings highlight the potential of integrating EHR foundation models with pretrained LLMs for interpretable clinical prediction.

</details>


### [69] [Traj-Evolve: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for Patient Trajectory Modeling in Lung Cancer Early Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02812)
*Sihang Zeng,Matthew Thompson,Ruth Etzioni,Meliha Yetisgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Traj-Evolve提出了一种自进化多智能体系统，通过经验池（ExPool）和非参数记忆与多智能体强化学习（MARL）相结合，解决从稀疏、噪声的长上下文多模态电子病历中建模患者轨迹的挑战。在肺癌预测任务中，该方法优于9个强基线，并验证了两种机制在提高特异性和敏感性方面的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统虽然解决了上下文长度问题，但患者处理相互独立，无法像临床医生那样利用先前类似病例的积累经验。建模患者轨迹需要处理稀疏、噪声和长上下文多模态序列，这是一个未被充分解决的挑战。

Method: Traj-Evolve包含两个互补的进化机制：1）经验池（ExPool）作为非参数记忆，通过拒绝采样推理轨迹索引来检索类似患者作为少样本上下文；2）通过奖励排名微调的多智能体强化学习（MARL）优化智能体间和智能体-记忆的协作。采用留一法交叉检索策略统一两种机制，使训练和推理时的行为保持一致。

Result: 在利用最多5年多模态EHR的肺癌预测任务上，Traj-Evolve在整体人群和具有挑战性的从不吸烟者人群中均优于9个强基线。分析显示：扩大ExPool使最优检索从多样化样本转向特定样本；MARL中管理者智能体的预测损失快速收敛，而工作者智能体的时序推理持续从更多验证患者中受益；两种机制在预测风险上具有互补性，ExPool提高特异性，MARL提高敏感性。

Conclusion: Traj-Evolve证明了自进化多智能体系统在EHR患者轨迹建模中的有效性。研究揭示了经验池扩展和MARL的不同作用机制，以及两者在提高模型性能方面的互补性。该方法为处理长上下文、稀疏噪声的医学数据提供了一种新的解决方案，展示了多智能体协作与经验积累相结合的潜力。

Abstract: Modeling patient trajectories from longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) requires reasoning over sparse, noisy, and long-context multimodal sequences. Existing LLM-based multi-agent systems address context length but process patients in isolation, failing to mirror how clinicians leverage accumulated experience from similar prior cases. We present Traj-Evolve, a self-evolving multi-agent system with two complementary evolving mechanisms. First, an Experience Pool (ExPool) acts as a non-parametric memory, indexing rejection-sampled reasoning traces to retrieve similar patients as few-shot contexts. Second, multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) via reward-ranked fine-tuning parametrically optimizes inter-agent and agent-memory collaboration. A leave-one-out cross-retrieval strategy unifies the two, aligning training- and inference-time behavior under retrieval augmentation. On a lung cancer prediction task utilizing up to five years of multimodal EHRs, Traj-Evolve outperforms 9 strong baselines on the overall population and a challenging never-smoker population.
