Abstract: Despite the pivotal role of numerical reasoning as the cornerstone of mathematical capabilities in large language models (LLMs) across applications, few benchmarks evaluate LLMs by integrating numerical processing and mathematical reasoning, hindering the interpretability of failures in math tasks. We introduce PyraMathBench, a comprehensive hierarchical benchmark with 32,505 questions derived from 7,404 math word problems, spanning 4 key cognitive aspects, 14 subcategories, and 2 modalities. Experiments reveal that LLMs' performance is severely compromised by inadequate numerical computation and weak handling of abstract numerical questions. To address this, we propose the Smart Optimization & Learning-based VErsatile module (SOLVE) and Interactive Relative Policy Optimization (IRPO), which enhance LLMs' numerical-mathematical synergy via efficient tool calls (fuzzy matching and low-quality call rejection). Comparative experiments show Qwen-2.5 achieves a 5.0 score improvement with SOLVE and IRPO training.

</details>


### [104] [Reasoning Structure of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03883)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Fabian Farestam,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将大推理模型（LRM）的非结构化推理轨迹转换为可验证推理图的可扩展基准与流水线，并定义了推理效率指标，从而以结构化拓扑分析揭示传统指标（准确率和token数）所掩盖的推理行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估大推理模型主要依赖最终答案准确率或token数量等指标，但这些指标相同的模型可能具有根本不同的推理结构。作者认为现有评估方法无法区分推理过程中的结构性差异，因此需要一种更细粒度、可量化的方法来揭示推理的真实质量。

Method: 1) 构建了一个基于逻辑谜题的可扩展LRM基准；2) 设计了一条流水线，将非结构化的推理轨迹转换为由声明（claims）和依赖关系（dependencies）组成的可验证推理图；3) 基于推理图的拓扑结构定义了推理效率指标（reasoning efficiency metric），量化模型逻辑流的集中程度；4) 在开源推理模型上进行结构化分析。

Result: 对开源推理模型的分析表明，结构化测量能够有效区分token数量和准确率所混淆的不同行为模式，为诊断推理失败模式以及比较推理随谜题难度变化的扩展规律提供了实用工具。

Conclusion: 通过将推理转化为结构化、可测量的图对象并进行拓扑分析，可以超越传统的准确率和token数指标，更深入地理解和诊断大推理模型的推理行为，为模型评估和改进提供了新的结构化视角。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are often evaluated using metrics such as final-answer accuracy or token count. However, identical scores on these metrics can hide fundamentally different reasoning structures. To address this limitation, we introduce a scalable LRM benchmark of logic puzzles and a pipeline that converts unstructured traces into verifiable reasoning graphs of claims and dependencies. This turns reasoning into a structured, measurable object whose topology can be quantitatively analyzed. Building on this, we define a reasoning efficiency metric that quantifies how concentrated the model's logical flow is. Our analysis on open-source reasoning models shows that structural measurements separate behaviors that token count and accuracy conflate, providing a practical tool for diagnosing failure modes and comparing how reasoning scales with puzzle difficulty.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [ModuLoop : Low-Level Code Generation using Modular Synthesizer and Closed-Loop Debugger for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03047)
*Gina Yoon,Sumin Lee,Joo Yong Sim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Closed-Loop Modular Code Synthesizer框架，利用预训练大语言模型（无需任务特定微调）进行模块化代码规划与生成，并通过闭环迭代执行和调试，实现LLM在机器人低层精确控制任务中的有效应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成和问题求解等领域表现出色，但其在机器人控制中的应用，尤其是在需要精确操作、实时反馈和依赖环境执行的低层任务中，仍然受限。本文旨在解决LLM在机器人低层控制任务中应用不足的问题。

Method: 提出Closed-Loop Modular Code Synthesizer框架：1）利用预训练LLM（无需微调）进行模块化代码规划与生成；2）迭代执行生成的代码并插入调试探针以观察行为；3）通过闭环结构实现系统化的调试与代码优化，最终生成可执行的控制程序。

Result: 在RGB-D相机与机械臂标定任务中验证了框架的有效性，并在后续的抓取放置（pick-and-place）任务中展示了标定精度及框架的可扩展性。两个任务均取得了高执行精度和高自主性。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了基于LLM的机器人控制方法的实用性和可扩展性，证明了通过闭环模块化代码合成，预训练LLM无需微调即可在真实世界的机器人低层精确控制任务中实现高效、自主的控制程序生成与执行。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance across various domains, including code generation and problem solving. However, their application in robotic control, particularly in low-level tasks that require precise manipulation, real-time feedback, and environment-dependent execution, remains limited. To address this challenge, we propose the Closed-Loop Modular Code Synthesizer framework. This framework leverages a pre-trained LLM without any task-specific fine-tuning to perform modular code planning and generation, and iteratively executes the generated code while inserting debugging probes to observe its behavior. This closed-loop structure facilitates systematic debugging and refinement, ultimately producing executable control programs. We apply the proposed framework to the calibration of an RGB-D camera and a robotic arm, validating its effectiveness in real-world settings. Furthermore, through a subsequent pick-and-place task, we demonstrate not only the accuracy of the calibration but also the potential extensibility of the framework. Across both tasks, the framework achieved high execution accuracy and autonomy, illustrating the practicality and scalability of LLM-based robotic control using our framework.

</details>


### [106] [Bridging Predictive Uncertainty and Safe Action: Sample-Conditioned Differentiable Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03296)
*Chengzhen Meng,Pei Liu,Zhiyu Huang,Chen Lv,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于条件扩散模型的样本条件可微分规划框架，通过CVaR尾部风险约束优化安全可解释轨迹，显著提升自动驾驶规划性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂动态交互式驾驶环境存在普遍不确定性，当前系统在高表达能力的不确定性建模与可解释、安全的运动规划之间存在根本瓶颈

Method: 1. 利用条件扩散模型生成多样化未来场景样本；2. 将样本直接输入可微分规划器；3. 采用经验条件风险价值(CVaR)尾部风险约束缓解预测不确定性；4. 引入有向图表示场景上下文

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion和Argoverse 2数据集上进行广泛开环和闭环评估，显著优于现有最先进基线，在安全性、效率和乘坐舒适性方面均有提升

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥合了不确定性建模与安全运动规划之间的鸿沟，能够优化出对罕见但安全关键交互具有鲁棒性的物理可解释轨迹

Abstract: Complex, dynamic, and interactive driving environments pose significant challenges for autonomous driving, primarily due to the pervasive uncertainty of surrounding traffic. A fundamental bottleneck in current systems is the disconnect between highly expressive uncertainty modeling and interpretable, safe motion planning. In this paper, we propose a novel sample-conditioned differentiable planning framework that bridges this gap by explicitly incorporating diffusion-generated future trajectories into the optimization process. Rather than compressing predictions into a single deterministic future or relying on black-box end-to-end architectures, our approach leverages a conditional diffusion model to generate a diverse set of plausible future scenarios. Crucially, these samples are directly fed into a differentiable planner, which explicitly mitigates predictive uncertainty via an empirical Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) tail-risk constraint. This allows the planner to optimize a physically interpretable trajectory that is robust to rare yet safety-critical interactions. Furthermore, we introduce a directed graph representation for scene context that yields substantial improvements in both predictive effectiveness and computational efficiency. Validated through extensive open-loop and closed-loop evaluations on the Waymo Open Motion and Argoverse 2 datasets, our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in safety, efficiency, and ride comfort.

</details>


### [107] [CANMOT: Class-Aware Noise Modeling for Multi-Object Tracking in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03590)
*Timo Osterburg,Stefan Schütte,Torsten Bertram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CANMOT，一种面向基于卡尔曼滤波的3D多目标跟踪（MOT）的类别感知与物体对齐噪声建模框架，通过引入类别特定的过程和测量协方差矩阵（可选地在物体坐标系中表示），显著提升了跟踪性能并减少了身份切换，同时揭示了标准KF-MOT基线存在严重过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于卡尔曼滤波的多目标跟踪（MOT）系统通常使用全局共享的过程噪声和测量噪声协方差，假设所有异构交通参与者（如车辆、行人等）具有相同的不确定性特征。这一假设忽略了不同类别物体在运动特性和观测不确定性上的差异，导致跟踪性能受限且不确定性估计存在严重失准。

Method: 提出CANMOT框架，引入类别特定的对角过程协方差和测量协方差矩阵，并可选择将其在物体坐标系中表示，以保持纵向-横向的各向异性（anisotropy）。该方法不修改底层卡尔曼滤波框架，仅通过更合理的噪声建模提升跟踪与校准表现。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上的系统实验表明：1）类别感知和物体对齐的噪声建模显著提升了跟踪性能，大幅减少了与SotA相比的身份切换；2）通过ANEES和χ²违反测试分析不确定性一致性，发现标准KF-MOT基线存在严重的过度自信问题；3）所提方法改善了校准效果但仍有显著不一致性。

Conclusion: 类别感知和物体对齐的噪声建模能有效提升KF-based 3D MOT的跟踪性能与不确定性校准，但即使如此，估计不确定性仍存在较大不一致性，表明该领域仍需进一步深入研究。

Abstract: Kalman filter (KF)-based multi-object tracking (MOT) remains a strong baseline for autonomous driving due to its strong performance, computational efficiency and interpretability. In most practical systems, the process noise and measurement noise covariances are defined globally and shared across object classes, presuming identical uncertainty characteristics across heterogeneous traffic participants.
  This work revisits this assumption and proposes CANMOT, a class-aware and object-aligned noise modeling framework for KF-based 3D MOT. Class-specific diagonal process and measurement covariance matrices are introduced and optionally expressed in the object coordinate frame to preserve longitudinal-lateral anisotropy.
  Systematic experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that class-aware and object-aligned noise modeling improves tracking performance and substantially reduces identity switches compared to state-of-the-art (SotA). In addition, the consistency of the estimated uncertainty is analyzed using the Average Normalized Estimation Error Squared (ANEES) and $χ^2$-based violation tests. The results reveal severe overconfidence in standard KF-based MOT baselines. While the proposed formulation improves calibration without modifying the underlying filtering framework, it still exhibits substantial inconsistency, highlighting the need for further research in this area.
  Code is available at https://github.com/rst-tu-dortmund/learned-3d-nms.

</details>


### [108] [Face versus Body Tracking for Human-Robot Interaction: An Egocentric Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03694)
*Jessica Wenninger,Gabriel Skantze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对人机交互（HRI）中机器人因身份频繁切换（IDSW）而丢失对话上下文的问题，构建了一个基于Furhat机器人的自定义第一视角（egocentric）数据集，系统评估了检测、跟踪、空间记忆和外观重识别（ReID）对跟踪稳定性的影响，最终优化后的管线将IDSW降低了49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉模型主要针对监控或自动驾驶场景优化，不适用于社交机器人面临的第一视角挑战（如人体弹跳、相互遮挡、离开画面等）。频繁的身份切换会导致机器人在对话中途失去上下文，严重影响人机交互的连续性和质量。现有基准测试缺乏密集的近距离遮挡场景，无法有效验证HRI感知模型。

Method: 1）通过Furhat机器人收集并标注了一个第一视角社交动态数据集；2）系统性评估框架，分离检测误差与跟踪逻辑的影响；3）对比人脸跟踪与人体跟踪；4）评估扩展空间记忆和外观重识别（ReID）的作用；5）综合优化跟踪管线。

Result: 1）扩展空间记忆可缓解长时间遮挡，但在复杂动态事件中失效；2）引入ReID解决了复杂的身份切换，但呈现相反效果——显著提升人体跟踪稳定性，却因侧面角度敏感性导致人脸IDSW激增；3）优化后的管线整体将IDSW降低49%，有效减少交互中断。

Conclusion: 标准基准测试缺乏密集近距离遮挡场景，无法真正验证HRI感知模型的有效性。本文强调了原生采集社交动态数据的关键必要性，并证明通过合理整合空间记忆与ReID可显著提升社交机器人的多目标跟踪稳定性，为人机交互的持续性和自然性提供了重要技术支撑。

Abstract: To enable meaningful human-robot interaction (HRI), a robot must continuously assess engagement by consistently tracking users over time. State-of-the-art computer vision models, however, are heavily optimized for surveillance or autonomous driving. A social robot faces distinct egocentric challenges, such as humans bouncing, obstructing each other, or leaving the frame. Frequent identity switches (IDSW) cause the robot to lose its footing mid-conversation. To address this, we introduce a novel, custom-annotated egocentric dataset collected via the Furhat robot to capture complex social dynamics. We present a systematic evaluation isolating detection errors from tracking logic, comparing face versus body tracking, and assessing the impact of extended spatial memory and appearance re-identification (ReID). Results indicate that increasing spatial memory mitigates prolonged occlusions but fails on complex dynamic events. Integrating ReID resolves complex switches but exhibits opposing effects: it substantially improves body tracking stability, yet causes facial IDSW to spike due to profile angle sensitivity. Ultimately, our optimized pipeline reduces IDSW by 49\%, mitigating interaction breakdowns. Because standard benchmarks lack dense, close-quarter occlusions, this work highlights the critical need for natively captured social dynamics to truly validate HRI perception models.

</details>


### [109] [Preference-Calibrated Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03949)
*Zeyi Liu,Guangyao Liu,Yinuo Qu,Yuquan Xue,Bofang Jia,Chunhua Yang,Weihua Gui,Keke Huang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了PACT（Preference-calibrated Actor-Critic Training）框架，通过利用人类干预中隐含的偏好信号对次优轨迹段进行信用重新分配，并直接引导策略训练，从而解决人在环强化学习（HIL-RL）中次优动作导致Q值高估和策略误导的问题。在5个真实机器人操作任务上，平均成功率提升24.5%，收敛速度提升1.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HIL-RL方法对成功轨迹中包含的次优动作段（偏离期望任务执行路径、需要人类干预的片段）采用一致的信用分配原则，将折扣终端奖励均匀传播到所有转移中，忽略了每个转移对任务成功的实际贡献。这导致评论家学习中的Q值高估，并间接误导演员更新朝向次优行为模式。

Method: PACT框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 设计一个进度模型，从人类演示中学习并识别次优轨迹段用于信用校正；(2) 利用干预状态下的人类动作和重采样的策略动作构建偏好对，定义反事实优势来惩罚次优段的Bellman目标，实现方向性信用校准；(3) 在有界均值空间中直接将策略与人类纠正动作对齐，提供超越评论家引导更新的额外信号。

Result: 在5个真实机器人操作任务上，PACT相比基线方法平均成功率提升24.5%，收敛速度提升1.3倍，同时提高了强化学习的样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: PACT通过利用人类干预隐含的偏好信号进行信用重新分配和策略直接对齐，有效解决了HIL-RL中次优轨迹段导致的Q值高估和策略偏差问题，在真实机器人操作中显著提升了样本效率和任务性能。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) improves sample efficiency in real-robot manipulation through online human intervention. However, successful trajectories may include suboptimal actions that deviate from the desired task-execution path and force human intervention. Existing HIL-RL methods typically apply the consistent credit assignment principle to all transitions, uniformly propagating discounted terminal rewards through suboptimal segments, ignoring the actual contribution of each transition to task success. This overestimates Q-values for critic learning and indirectly misguides actor updates toward suboptimal behavior patterns. To this end, we propose PACT, a Preference-calibrated Actor-Critic Training framework that leverages the implicit preference signals induced by intervention to perform credit reassignment on identified suboptimal segments while directly guiding policy training for unbiased critic-actor learning. Specifically, we first design a progress model that learns from human demonstration and identifies suboptimal segments for credit correction. Then, from the human action and resampled policy action at the intervention state, we build preference pairs to define a counterfactual advantage that penalizes Bellman targets of the identified suboptimal segment, enabling directional credit calibration. Moreover, we directly align the policy with human corrective actions in the bounded mean space, providing an additional signal beyond critic-guided updates. Across five real-robot manipulation tasks, PACT improves the average success rate by 24.5% and achieves 1.3 times faster convergence, thereby improving both RL sample efficiency and performance. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/HILRL-A1X-BC05.

</details>


### [110] [Self-Refining Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Conditioned UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03963)
*Roohan Ahmed Khan,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgenticRL，一个基于多模态GPT智能体引导的强化学习框架，用于无人机（UAV）导航任务中的奖励函数自动设计、策略优化和真实世界部署，实现了闭环自我改进和仿真到现实的迁移，真实世界成功率达91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在自主机器人导航任务中展现出强大潜力，但其实际应用仍严重依赖人工设计的奖励函数和反复手动调优，耗时且无法保证任务的高成功率。因此需要一种能够提高奖励设计和策略优化自主性的方法。

Method: AgenticRL框架利用多模态生成式预训练Transformer（GPT）智能体：1）解读任务信息和视觉场景观测，自动生成任务特定的奖励函数；2）使用PPO算法训练策略；3）通过诊断包评估训练策略，生成反馈；4）基于反馈识别失败模式，在闭环自我改进过程中优化奖励函数；5）在推理阶段，利用真实世界图像和自然语言任务信息自动识别当前场景并选择合适的训练策略执行。

Result: 在多个导航任务（穿越门、避障、跨墙着陆、轨迹跟随、运动行为学习）上评估，闭环优化过程相比初始奖励使策略行为提升了71%；仿真到现实迁移的真实世界成功率达91%，仿真到现实准确率达94%。

Conclusion: AgenticRL框架通过多模态GPT智能体实现了奖励设计、策略训练、评估反馈和策略选择的自动化闭环流程，显著提升了无人机导航任务的自主性和性能，并成功验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has shown strong potential for enabling autonomous robots to learn complex navigational tasks. However, its practical use still depends heavily on human designed reward functions and repeated manual fine tuning, which is time consuming and does not guarantee high success in the desired task. This paper presents AgenticRL, agent guided reinforcement learning framework that increases autonomy in reward design, policy refinement, and real world deployment for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAV) navigation tasks. AgenticRL uses a multimodal generative pre-trained tansformer (GPT) agent to interpret task information and visual scene observations, generate task specific reward functions, train policies using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, and then act as a critic by evaluating the trained policy through diagnosis packets to generate feedback. Based on this feedback, the agent identifies failure modes and refines the reward function in a closed loop self improvement process. To further leverage the multimodal GPT agent during inference, AgenticRL uses real world images and natural language task information to automatically identify the active scenario and select the appropriate trained policy for execution. The framework is evaluated on multiple navigational tasks, including gate traversal, obstacle avoidance, wall barrier crossing with landing, trajectory following, and motion behavior learning. Experimental results show that the closed loop refinement process improves policy behavior compared with initial rewards by 71%. We also demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of the proposed framework, achieving a real world success rate of 91% and a sim-to-real accuracy of 94%.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了开源大语言模型（LLaMA-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B）的隐藏状态中是否存在线性可分的真实性信号，发现在中间网络层使用线性探针即可高效检测幻觉（AUROC达0.904–1.000），远优于基于采样的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的幻觉问题严重影响其可靠性。现有检测方法（如基于采样的自一致性、注意力熵等）效果参差不齐，且对模型内部隐藏状态中是否编码了可利用的真实性信号缺乏系统研究。本文旨在探究隐藏状态中真实性信号的存在性、线性可分性及其在网络深度上的分布规律。

Method: 在三个7B–8B指令微调模型（4-bit NF4量化）上，从四个幻觉基准（TruthfulQA、HaluEval-QA、FEVER及合成数据集）提取逐层隐藏状态，系统比较四种检测方法：线性探针与MLP探针、INSIDE EigenScore、自一致性、注意力熵。重点分析线性可分性及最佳探针层的位置分布。

Result: 1）单层中间网络线性探针AUROC达0.904–1.000，大幅领先采样方法（≤0.541 AUROC）；2）真实性信号近似线性，MLP探针提升不超过0.01 AUROC；3）最佳探针层在不同模型族中一致集中在中间偏后区域（Llama/Mistral第13–18层/32层，Qwen第19–25层/28层）；4）首层注意力熵在知识接地场景下提供互补信号（HaluEval-QA上0.866–0.941 AUROC），无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 开源LLM的隐藏状态中存在强线性可分的真实性信号，集中在中间层，线性探针是最简单高效的幻觉检测手段。采样方法在该协议下表现不佳源于评估方式与信息获取方式的结构性不匹配，而非方法本身的固有局限。代码和数据可在单张8GB GPU上完全复现。

Abstract: We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\,GB GPU.

</details>


### [112] [$Ψ$-Bench: Evaluating Persona-Sensitive Influencing in Persuasive Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02754)
*Peixuan Han,Hongyi Du,Jiayu Liu,Yihang Sun,Yutong Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ψ-Bench基准，用于系统评估LLM在真实对话场景中主动影响和说服用户的能力。实验评估了10个前沿LLM，发现即使最先进的模型在说服力方面仍有较大提升空间，且获取用户画像信息可带来平均18.24%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化语言代理研究主要将代理定位为被动响应用户偏好的角色，缺乏主动与用户交互并提供引导或建议的能力。为了系统评估LLM在真实交互中的主动个性化（即主动说服和影响用户）能力，需要一个专门的基准测试。

Method: 1) 提出Ψ-Bench基准，设计三个涉及说服的真实世界交互场景；2) 通过对话历史衍生的显式用户画像，赋予模拟客户端个人特征；3) 在Ψ-Bench上评估10个前沿LLM的表现；4) 对比有无用户画像信息条件下的模型表现差异。

Result: 1) 大多数模型能够生成连贯且合理的论证，但即使最先进的模型在说服力方面仍有显著提升空间；2) 提供客户端用户画像信息可带来平均18.24%的性能提升，表明用户特定信息对有效说服至关重要。

Conclusion: 人格敏感的影响能力是评估和开发更主动的个性化LLM代理的一个具有挑战性但实用的方向。Ψ-Bench为该方向的研究提供了系统化的评估框架。

Abstract: Personalization is a crucial capability of modern language agents. However, current research primarily positions personalized agents as passive responders to user preferences, limiting their ability to interact with users and provide suggestions or guidance proactively. To systematically evaluate such proactive personalization in realistic interactions, we propose $Ψ$-Bench, a benchmark for assessing LLMs' ability to influence realistic users through conversation. We design three real-world interaction scenarios that involve persuasion in $Ψ$-Bench, and endow simulated clients with personal characteristics through explicit user profiles derived from dialogue histories. We evaluate 10 frontier LLMs on $Ψ$-Bench and find that while most models can produce coherent and reasonable arguments, even state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement in persuasion. We also find that providing access to client profiles yields an average performance gain of 18.24\%, highlighting the importance of user-specific information for effective persuasion. Overall, our work highlights persona-sensitive influencing as a challenging yet practical direction for evaluating and developing more proactive personalized LLM agents. Codes are available at: https://github.com/Hanpx20/Psi-Bench.

</details>


### [113] [Qift: Shift-Friendly No-Zero W2 Post-Training Quantization for Rotated W2A4/KV4 LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02823)
*Chi-Wei Huang,Chia-Chi Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Qift，一种用于Hadamard旋转量化流水线中W2A4/KV4推理的无零点、固定双比特权重级集{-0.5, ±1.5}，通过分析预训练权重经旋转后近似零中心高斯分布的特性，显著改善了双比特量化的精度，且无需训练、无需学习码本、无需分组网格和零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准双比特权重级集{-2,-1,0,+1}在激进的W2A4/KV4量化设置下经常崩溃。作者发现这不是单纯的比特宽度问题，而是重建层级问题——传统非对称W2已显著优于标准级集，表明固定级集的几何设计本身存在优化空间。

Method: 1) 分析Hadamard旋转后权重的统计特性：预训练权重已近似零中心分布，旋转主要使其标准化形状高斯化（超额峰度和Q-Q误差下降数个数量级）；2) 基于该近似零中心高斯源模型，提出Qift固定无零W2级集{±0.5, ±1.5}（等价于半尺度重参数化下的{±1, ±3}），并提供幂二次变体{±1, ±4}用于符号移位解码；3) 通过尺度不变比率分析确定有效内/外质心比范围为0.25~0.33，解释多种方案的优劣；4) 支持纯W2A4和L层混合W2/W4精度策略。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B的所有224个线性模块上：1) 无零级集在纯W2A4困惑度、L层混合W2/W4困惑度、下游任务精度及GPTQ残差行为上均持续优于标准W2级集；2) 在L=16混合精度下，半数Transformer层保持双比特精度即可大幅缩小与W3A4的差距；3) 尺度不变比率分析有效解释了MNZ、Lloyd、NF2、PoT-MNZ等方案表现良好而{±1, ±2}表现不佳的原因。

Conclusion: Qift通过重新设计固定双比特码到级集的映射，提供了一种简单、源感知且部署友好的方案，作为复杂学习型W2码本的替代，在保持半数层双比特精度的同时显著缩小了与W3A4的性能差距。该方法无需训练、无需学习码本、无需分组网格和零点，仅保留标准的逐通道缩放。

Abstract: Two-bit weight quantization is attractive for memory-efficient LLM inference, but the standard W2 level set {-2,-1,0,+1} often collapses under aggressive W2A4/KV4 settings. We study the scalar level-set geometry of two-bit weights in a Hadamard-rotated quantization pipeline. Conventional asymmetric W2 substantially improves over the standard level set, indicating that W2A4 failure is not only a bit-width problem but also a reconstruction-level problem. Across all 224 linear modules in each of LLaMA-2-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B, pretrained weights are already nearly zero-centered, while Hadamard rotation primarily Gaussianizes their standardized shape: excess kurtosis and Q-Q error drop by orders of magnitude. Based on this approximate zero-centered Gaussian-like source model, we propose Qift, a fixed no-zero W2 level set for rotated W2A4/KV4 inference. The main level set is {+/-0.5, +/-1.5}, equivalently {+/-1, +/-3} under a half-scale reparameterization; a power-of-two variant uses {+/-1, +/-4} for sign-and-shift decoded weight application. Qift redesigns the fixed two-bit code-to-level mapping and is training-free, learned-codebook-free, group-grid-free, and zero-point-free, retaining the standard per-channel scale. A scale-invariant ratio analysis identifies an effective inner/outer centroid ratio range of 0.25 to 0.33, explaining why mirror no-zero (MNZ), Lloyd, NF2, and PoT-MNZ perform well while {+/-1, +/-2} does not. On both models, the no-zero level sets consistently improve pure W2A4 perplexity, L-layer mixed W2/W4 perplexity, downstream accuracy, and GPTQ residual behavior over the standard W2 level set. At L=16 mixed precision, they substantially narrow the gap to W3A4 while keeping half of the transformer layers at two-bit precision, giving a simple, source-aware, and deployment-friendly alternative to more complex learned W2 codebooks.

</details>


### [114] [GRZO: Group-Relative Zeroth-Order Optimization for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02857)
*Liyan Tan,Yequan Zhao,Yifan Yang,Ruijie Zhang,Xinling Yu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRZO（Group-Relative Zeroth-Order Optimizer），一种基于组相对归一化的零阶优化器，通过每个mini-batch样本生成一个伪独立扰动，在不增加前向计算成本的前提下将有效梯度方向数从1提升到batch size，显著降低梯度估计方差，在保持推理级内存占用的同时大幅提升大型语言模型微调的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶（ZO）优化是反向传播的内存高效替代方案，适用于大语言模型微调，但其梯度估计的高方差问题严重限制了实际部署效果和性能表现。

Method: GRZO为每个mini-batch样本生成一个伪独立扰动，通过组相对归一化（group-relative normalization）聚合每个样本的损失，从而在不增加额外前向计算开销的情况下将有效梯度方向数量从1提升至batch size，同时保持推理级别的内存占用。

Result: 理论证明GRZO方向无偏且方差随batch size成比例缩小，获得比MeZO更紧的非凸收敛界。实验方面：在RoBERTa-large、Llama3-8B和OPT-13B上，GRZO在Llama3-8B上平均准确率比MeZO高+3.0，峰值GPU内存降低23%；作为MeZO核心的即插即用替代品，可将稀疏、低秩和量化ZO变体的平均性能提升+6.0。

Conclusion: GRZO是一种高效且通用的零阶优化方法，在保持推理级内存优势的同时显著缓解了梯度估计方差问题，能够广泛提升各类零阶优化变体（稀疏、低秩、量化）的性能，具有理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization is a memory-efficient alternative to backpropagation for fine-tuning large language models, but its deployment is limited by the high variance of gradient estimation. We propose GRZO, a Group-Relative Zeroth-Order optimizer that draws one pseudo-independent perturbation per mini-batch example and aggregates the per-example losses through group-relative normalization, raising the effective gradient-direction count from one to the batch size at no additional forward cost while preserving inference-level memory. We prove that GRZO is directionally unbiased with variance shrinking proportionally to the batch size, yielding a tighter nonconvex convergence bound than MeZO. Across RoBERTa-large, Llama3-8B, and OPT-13B over multiple tasks, GRZO improves average accuracy on Llama3-8B by $+3.0$ over MeZO at $23\%$ lower peak GPU memory; as a drop-in replacement for the MeZO core, it lifts sparse, low-rank, and quantized ZO variants by $+6.0$ on average.

</details>


### [115] [KForge: LLM-Driven Cross-Platform Kernel Generation for AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02963)
*Taras Sereda,Burak Bartan,Ankita Nayak,Tom St. John,Natalie Serrino,Zain Asgar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了KForge，一个跨平台、基于双LLM智能体协作迭代优化的自动内核生成框架，能够在多种硬件后端上自动生成高性能推理内核，在NVIDIA B200上端到端吞吐量超越TensorRT-LLM 2.12%，在Intel Arc B580上对37个GEMM+尾操作工作负载实现5.13倍几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产推理越来越面向异构加速器混合部署，智能体流水线中推理、工具调用、多智能体协调等阶段具有不同的计算与内存特征，理想情况下每个阶段应在最适合的加速器上运行。然而，为日益增多的硬件后端和编程模型手写高性能内核耗时且需深厚底层专业知识，难以随内核复杂度增长而扩展。已有利用LLM自动生成内核的方法在底层代码生成和跨后端泛化方面仍面临挑战。

Method: KForge围绕一个迭代精化循环构建，由两个协作的LLM智能体驱动：(1) 生成智能体——利用编译和正确性反馈产生并逐步精化内核；(2) 性能分析智能体——解读从编程API到GUI工具的性能剖析数据，输出指导下一轮合成的优化建议。循环交替执行功能通道（驱动候选内核达到正确性）和优化通道（缩小与手写调优基线的性能差距）。

Result: 在两个基线参考可用性差异较大的后端上进行了评估：(1) NVIDIA B200上，KForge在gpt-oss-20b推理速度基准测试中端到端吞吐量比TensorRT-LLM提升2.12%；(2) Intel Arc B580上，KForge生成的Triton内核在KernelBench Level 2的37个GEMM+尾操作工作负载上，相比PyTorch eager和torch.compile中较快者实现5.13倍几何平均加速，主要得益于算子融合和混合精度执行。

Conclusion: KForge通过双LLM智能体协作的迭代精化框架，有效解决了跨平台高性能内核自动生成中的正确性与性能优化难题，在主流GPU平台上均展现出优于或接近手写基线的性能，证明了LLM驱动内核生成在实际生产推理部署中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Production inference increasingly targets a heterogeneous mix of accelerators. Agentic pipelines interleave reasoning, tool calls, and multi-agent coordination, each with distinct compute and memory profiles. For optimal efficiency, each stage should run on the accelerator best suited to it. This creates a systems challenge: each pipeline now requires high-performance kernels across a growing set of hardware backends and programming models. Writing these kernels by hand is time-consuming, demands deep low-level expertise, and does not scale as kernel complexity grows. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been leveraged for automatic kernel generation, but challenges in low-level code generation and cross-backend generalization persist. We present KForge, a cross-platform framework built around an iterative refinement loop driven by two collaborating LLM-based agents: a generation agent that produces and progressively refines kernels using compilation and correctness feedback, and a performance-analysis agent that interprets profiling data, from programmatic APIs to GUI-based tools, and emits recommendations that steer the next round of synthesis. The loop alternates between functional passes, which drive a candidate to correctness, and optimization passes, which close the performance gap to hand-tuned baselines. We evaluate KForge on two backends with very different baseline reference availability. On NVIDIA B200, KForge achieves a 2.12$\%$ improvement in end-to-end throughput compared to TensorRT-LLM on the gpt-oss-20b inference speed benchmark. On Intel Arc B580, KForge generates Triton kernels achieving a 5.13$\times$ geometric mean speedup over the faster of PyTorch eager and torch.compile on 37 GEMM + tail-ops workloads from KernelBench Level 2, primarily via operator fusion and mixed-precision execution.

</details>


### [116] [How Quantization Changes Interpretable Features: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03002)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过在Pythia-70M和Gemma-2-2B模型上测试INT8到INT4的量化，发现SAE特征在量化后存在系统性的降级而非完全失效，特征存活率随量化精度下降而递减（Pythia-70M在INT6下存活62.4%，Gemma-2-2B在INT6下存活51.3%），特征存活可从全精度统计中预测（AUC 0.92-0.97），且任务指标（如困惑度）可能无法检测到这种损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型量化是部署大语言模型的常见手段，但通常仅通过困惑度或下游任务准确性来判断模型是否可接受，很少测试可解释特征在权重舍入后是否仍然保持。随着安全审计和引导干预越来越多地依赖这些特征，量化后特征存活情况对可解释性至关重要。

Method: 使用冻结的稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为固定测量基，对相同token的全精度模型和最近邻舍入（RTN）量化模型进行编码，通过皮尔逊相关系数量化每个特征的存活情况。在Pythia-70M和Gemma-2-2B模型上从INT8到INT4进行比特宽度扫描，同时测试量化与匹配困惑度幅值剪枝对特征集的影响。

Result: 特征存活是分级的：特征系统性地降级而非全部同时失效；62.4%的活跃特征在Pythia-70M的INT6下存活，51.3%在Gemma-2-2B的INT6下存活；大多数未存活特征是模糊而非被破坏；存活率可从全精度统计预测（交叉验证AUC 0.92-0.97），峰值激活是最强的边际预测因子；任务指标可能遗漏这种损害（如Gemma-2-2B的INT7改善困惑度但退化18.7%的特征）；量化和匹配困惑度剪枝损坏强烈重叠的特征集（Jaccard重叠0.79-0.86，损伤分数Spearman相关0.98）。

Conclusion: 行为等价性不足以证明可解释性发现能迁移到量化部署中，需要针对压缩进行特征级别的审计。

Abstract: Quantization is a standard path to deploying large language models, and a quantized model is typically judged acceptable when its perplexity or downstream accuracy stays close to the full-precision original. Whether the model still computes in the same way, or whether the interpretable features identified in the full-precision model survive weight rounding, is rarely tested, even as safety audits and steering interventions increasingly rely on those features. We ask whether sparse autoencoder (SAE) features extracted from a dense full-precision model remain faithful once that model is quantized. Using a frozen SAE as a fixed measurement basis, we encode full-precision and round-to-nearest (RTN) quantized activations on identical tokens and quantify per-feature survival by Pearson correlation, sweeping bit-widths from INT8 to INT4 on Pythia-70M and Gemma-2-2B. We find that feature survival is graded: features degrade systematically rather than failing all at once, with 62.4 percent of active features surviving at INT6 on Pythia-70M and 51.3 percent surviving at INT6 on Gemma-2-2B, and with most non-survivors blurred rather than destroyed. Survival is predictable from full-precision statistics alone, with cross-validated AUCs of 0.92 to 0.97 and peak activation as the strongest marginal predictor. Critically, task metrics can miss this damage: on Gemma-2-2B, INT7 improves perplexity while degrading 18.7 percent of features. Finally, quantization and matched-perplexity magnitude pruning damage strongly overlapping feature sets, with Jaccard overlap of 0.79 to 0.86 and damage-score Spearman correlation of 0.98, suggesting a shared mode of compression-induced vulnerability. These results show that behavioral parity is insufficient evidence that interpretability findings transfer to quantized deployments, motivating feature-level audits of compression.

</details>


### [117] [MOSAIC: Efficient Mixture-of-Agent Scheduling via Adaptive Aggregation and Inference Concurrency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03014)
*Saptarshi Mitra,Yifan Zhang,Rachid Karami,Phyo Pyae Moe Aung,Nazmul Takbir,Sreetama Sarkar,Souvik Kundu,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC提出了一种面向Mixture-of-Agents工作负载的调度框架，通过ILP优化专家部署和提示词分配，并采用置信度感知的自适应聚合来加速MoA系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoA系统虽然能提高推理准确率，但在有限GPU资源上存在瓶颈：1）技能路由导致专家需求不均衡；2）指令微调LLM与长推理模型结合导致生成长度极端变异；3）传统调度策略因负载不均衡造成GPU空闲和吞吐量下降。

Method: 1）基于整数线性规划（ILP）的调度器，联合优化专家部署和每个工作器的提示词分配，利用离线测量的成本；2）置信度感知自适应聚合，利用专家间共识跳过重型最终聚合器LLM；3）在工作者间复制推理专家同时固定轻量级专家。

Result: 在4-GPU系统上，MOSAIC相比基线调度器达到：专家阶段最高2.5倍加速，聚合阶段4.23倍加速，端到端1.7~2.3倍加速，准确率仅相差0.1pp。

Conclusion: MOSAIC有效解决了MoA系统的调度瓶颈，在保持准确率的前提下显著提升了系统效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) systems improve reasoning accuracy by routing each query to multiple expert LLMs and aggregating their outputs. Efficiently executing this workload on limited GPU resources has bottlenecks. Skill-based routing creates skewed expert demand, and combining instruction-tuned LLMs with long-reasoning models results in extreme variability in generation lengths. Consequently, traditional scheduling strategies suffer from significant GPU idling and throughput collapse due to load imbalances. We present MOSAIC, a scheduling framework to accelerate MoA workloads. First, we formulate an Integer Linear Program (ILP) based scheduler that jointly optimizes expert placement and per-worker prompt assignment from offline-profiled costs, replicating reasoning experts across workers while pinning lightweight ones. Second, MOSAIC uses confidence-aware adaptive aggregation, leveraging inter-expert agreement to bypass the heavy final aggregator LLM for consensus queries. In our 4-GPU system, MOSAIC achieves up to 2.5x expert-stage, 4.23x aggregator-stage and 1.7~2.3x end-to-end speedups over the baseline scheduler, while matching accuracy within 0.1pp.

</details>


### [118] [Rethinking Molecular Text Representations for LLMs: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03057)
*Arun Raja,Garrett M. Morris,Kian Ming A. Chai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统评估了16个LLM在9种分子表示和8种化学任务上的表现，发现性能高度依赖表示方式，CML最优，结构化表示在结构任务中占优，IUPAC在语义任务中表现突出，SMILES表现不佳，建议采用任务感知的表示路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分子任务中应用日益广泛，但不同分子表示方式对性能影响显著，缺乏系统基准评估，且现有评估方法存在表示不变性问题。

Method: 构建系统基准测试，涵盖9种分子表示和8种化学任务，评估16个LLM（包括推理/非推理变体、化学专用模型、前沿闭源模型）；采用LLM-as-a-judge、分词审计、线性探针和注意力分析进行机制研究。

Result: 性能高度依赖表示方式；CML最优，MolJSON、InChI、canonical SMILES次之；结构化表示（CML、MolJSON）主导结构任务；IUPAC主导语义任务（所有16个LLM的分子检索均最优）；SMILES表现不佳；化学专用模型在SMILES上表现好但结构化表示下性能下降。

Conclusion: 反对表示不变性评估，建议采用任务感知的表示路由策略，以提升LLM在化学任务中的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for molecular tasks, but it remains unclear which molecular representation to use. We present a systematic benchmark evaluating LLM molecular competence across nine representations and eight chemical tasks. We benchmark 16 LLMs across five model families, including reasoning and non-reasoning variants, chemistry-specialized LLMs, and closed frontier models. Performance is strongly representation-dependent and no single representation wins across tasks, though CML is the best, followed by MolJSON, InChI, and then canonical SMILES. Explicit structured text representations (CML and MolJSON) dominate structural tasks; IUPAC dominates semantic tasks, winning molecule retrieval for all 16 LLMs; and SMILES variants are rarely optimal despite their prevalence in pretraining. Chemistry-specialized models perform well with SMILES at the cost of large degradations with structured text representations, suggesting SMILES-only evaluation rewards specialization that does not generalize. Using LLM-as-a-judge, we find that IUPAC produces the highest fraction of correct molecule generations. A mechanistic study via tokenization audits, linear probes and attention shows that representations are encoded differently inside the model; for example, structured representations require higher attention across the molecular span. Our results argue against representation-invariant evaluation and motivate task-aware representation routing for LLM-based chemistry.

</details>


### [119] [ASymPO: Asymmetric-Scale Policy Optimization for Asynchronous LLM Post-Training Without Behavior Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03070)
*Zehua Liu,Yuxuan Yao,Xiaojin Fu,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ASymPO（Asymmetric-Scale Policy Optimization）方法，通过仅使用当前策略概率（无需行为策略概率）来稳定异步强化学习中的分布漂移问题，解决了因响应过时而导致的正负损失项尺度不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习通过解耦响应生成和策略优化来提升语言模型后训练的吞吐量，但过时的响应会引入分布漂移。现有方法需要行为策略概率、重要性比率或截断来控制漂移，这要求在rollout系统和learner系统之间保持token对齐、版本一致且数值一致的行为对数概率，系统复杂且难以维护。因此，作者探索是否可以仅用当前策略概率来稳定异步分组相对强化学习。

Method: 作者首先识别出一种"尺度不平衡"失效模式：当过时响应在当前策略下评估时，正负损失项会出现在不同的负对数概率尺度上，导致零和优势不再意味着平衡的损失贡献。基于此，提出ASymPO方法——将每个响应的token损失按其当前平均token负对数概率进行归一化。此外，还提出了SPO（Scaled Policy Optimization）作为固定负缩放基线方法。两种方法均仅需当前策略概率，无需行为策略概率。

Result: 在异步数学推理后训练任务上评估了ASymPO和SPO两种仅依赖当前策略概率的目标函数。ASymPO恢复了响应级别的零和平衡，同时保留了非零学习信号，避免了传统方法中对行为策略概率的依赖。

Conclusion: ASymPO提供了一种简洁有效的方式来稳定异步强化学习中的分布漂移问题，无需行为策略概率即可实现稳定的策略优化，简化了异步训练系统的设计，同时保持了有效的学习信号。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning can improve language-model post-training throughput by decoupling response generation from policy optimization, but stale responses introduce distribution drift. Standard behavior-corrected methods control this drift with behavior-policy probabilities, importance ratios, or clipping, which requires token-aligned, versioned, and numerically consistent behavior log-probabilities across rollout and learner systems. We ask whether asynchronous group-relative RL can instead be stabilized using only current-policy probabilities. We identify a scale-imbalance failure mode: when stale responses are evaluated under the current policy, positive and negative loss terms can appear at different negative log-probability scales, so zero-sum advantages no longer imply balanced loss contributions. We propose Asymmetric-Scale Policy Optimization (ASymPO), which normalizes each response's token loss by its current average token negative log-probability. ASymPO requires no behavior-policy probabilities, restores response-level zero-sum balance, and preserves a nonzero learning signal. We also introduce Scaled Policy Optimization (SPO), a fixed negative-scaling baseline, and evaluate both current-policy-only objectives in asynchronous mathematical reasoning post-training.

</details>


### [120] [Efficient Hyperparameter Optimization for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03073)
*Minping Chen,Bowen Xiao,Du Liang,Chuxuan Zeng,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了JF-HPO（Joint Fidelity Hyperparameter Optimization）方法，通过同时调整模型大小和训练预算作为保真度维度，大幅提升大语言模型强化学习中超参数优化的计算效率，单次试验效率最高提升14.9倍，且在相同时间预算下获得更好或相当的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习对超参数配置高度敏感，超参数优化（HPO）至关重要但计算代价高昂。现有的多保真度HPO方法在面对LLM RL时仍然效率低下，原因在于模型规模庞大且训练周期资源消耗巨大。

Method: 提出JF-HPO方法，核心包含三个设计：(1) 利用目标LLM的小型代理模型进行高效训练与评估；(2) 基于训练动态设计精细的早停策略；(3) 引入高效的检查点机制以消除冗余计算。该方法同时将模型大小和训练预算作为保真度维度进行联合优化。

Result: 与现有HPO方法相比，JF-HPO将单次试验的计算效率提升最高14.9倍，在相同时间预算下取得更好或相当的预测精度。与使用VeRL Recipe的超参数配置相比，JF-HPO带来了5.8%至111.6%的性能提升。

Conclusion: JF-HPO通过联合模型规模与训练预算的双保真度策略，有效解决了LLM强化学习中HPO的计算瓶颈问题，在效率与性能上均展现出显著优势，为大规模LLM训练的超参数优化提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) is highly sensitive to hyperparameter configurations, making hyperparameter optimization (HPO) essential yet computationally expensive. Existing multi-fidelity HPO methods remain inefficient for LLM RL due to the massive model scale and resource-intensive training cycles. In this paper, we propose Joint Fidelity Hyperparameter Optimization (JF-HPO), which simultaneously adapts both model size and training budget as fidelity. JF-HPO is empowered by: (i) it leverages a small proxy model of the target LLM for efficient training and evaluation in each HPO trial; (ii) it integrates carefully designed early-stopping strategies based on training dynamics; (iii) it introduces an efficient checkpointing mechanism to eliminate redundant computations. Compared with existing HPO methods, JF-HPO significantly improves the computational efficiency of each trial (up to 14.9 times), while achieving better or competitive predictive accuracy under the same time budget. Notably, compared with utilizing hyperparameter configurations from the VeRL Recipe, JF-HPO delivers performance improvements ranging from 5.8% to 111.6%.

</details>


### [121] [Libra: Efficient Resource Management for Agentic RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03077)
*Kaiwen Chen,Xin Tan,Jingzong Li,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Libra，一个针对大语言模型（LLM）智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）的资源调度框架，通过周期性全局资源规划器和因果驱动的多级反馈队列（C-MLFQ）调度器，解决智能体RL中长尾轨迹分布、rollout与训练计算不对称、以及策略演化导致轨迹长度漂移三大挑战，在48块A800 GPU上实现了高达3.0倍的吞吐量提升和2.5倍的奖励收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着强化学习（RL）成为大语言模型后训练的标准范式，智能体RL中的rollout阶段会调用工具生成长尾且非平稳的工作负载。这带来了三大核心挑战：（1）长尾分布导致少量轨迹主导rollout完成时间；（2）rollout与训练在计算模式、内存需求和序列长度敏感性上存在严重不对称；（3）随着RL策略演化，轨迹长度分布随时间漂移，使得静态资源分配方案逐渐失效。现有资源管理方案无法有效应对这些问题。

Method: Libra引入两个核心机制：（1）周期性全局资源规划器——通过弹性混合GPU池联合优化rollout和训练集群间的GPU分配，实现轻量级、非阻塞的工作节点重分配；（2）因果驱动的多级反馈队列（C-MLFQ）调度器——基于工具返回结果中提取的因果信号（而非脆弱的长度预测），将请求路由到异构rollout桶中，从而更精准地调度不同复杂度的轨迹生成任务。

Result: 在48块A800 GPU上的实验评估表明，Libra相比基线方法实现了高达3.0倍的吞吐量提升，奖励收敛速度提升高达2.5倍。

Conclusion: Libra通过全局资源规划和因果驱动的调度策略，有效解决了智能体RL中工作负载的长尾性、非平稳性和计算不对称性问题，显著提升了系统效率和训练收敛速度，为大规模LLM智能体强化学习提供了高效的资源管理方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training paradigm for large language models (LLMs), extending beyond preference alignment to complex reasoning and multi-turn agentic behaviors. In agentic RL, the rollout stage generates trajectories while invoking tools, producing long-tailed and non-stationary workloads that challenge conventional resource-management assumptions. Three fundamental challenges arise. First, due to the long-tail distribution, a small fraction of trajectories dominates rollout makespan. Second, rollout and training exhibit strong asymmetry in compute patterns, memory demands, and sensitivity to sequence length. Third, as the RL policy evolves, the trajectory-length distribution drifts over time, rendering any static resource split progressively suboptimal.
  We present Libra, which introduces two core mechanisms. The first is a periodic global resource planner that jointly optimizes GPU allocation across rollout and training clusters. It leverages an elastic hybrid pool to enable lightweight, non-blocking worker reallocation between stages. The second is a causality-driven multi-level feedback queue (C-MLFQ) scheduler, which routes requests to heterogeneous rollout buckets based on causal signals derived from tool-return outcomes, rather than relying on fragile length predictions. Evaluated on 48 A800 GPUs, Libra achieves up to 3.0$\times$ higher throughput and converges up to 2.5$\times$ faster in reward compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [122] [Learning to Solve, Forgetting to Retain: Correct-Set Turnover in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03087)
*Chuanyu Qin,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Naibin Gu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR训练中出现的“正确集转换”现象（即已掌握的问题逐渐退化为不可解），提出一种零开销的保留感知回顾机制\method{}，通过周期性重新引入已掌握提示来维护模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习验证奖励（RLVR）虽能提升大模型能力，但训练过程中存在隐蔽的“正确集转换”现象：已解决的问题随训练进展逐渐退化为不可解。传统RLVR流程未能显式优化保留目标，导致性能损失。

Method: 提出保留感知回顾机制\method{}，通过跟踪已掌握提示并周期性重新引入以提醒模型，结合预推出批次替换实现零额外推出开销。

Result: 在20个跨模态基准（图像-文本、视频、纯文本）及Qwen3-VL、Qwen2.5-Math模型上，\method{}在GRPO、DAPO及重放基线中均取得持续性能提升，展现出跨模态与算法的鲁棒泛化性。

Conclusion: “修复窗口原则”表明延迟修复成本随时间急剧上升，\method{}通过保留感知回顾有效利用低成本窗口，在保持零额外推出开销的同时显著改善模型性能，为RLVR训练中的保留优化提供了通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the ability of large language model, yet headline accuracy gains often conceal a hidden cost: previously solved problems quietly become unsolvable as training proceeds. We frame this phenomenon as \emph{correct-set turnover}, representing the coupled dynamics of solution acquisition and regression over the mastered set. Under this view, retention becomes an explicit optimization target alongside acquisition. We analytically and empirically establish the \emph{repair-window principle}: the cost of restoring a regressed prompt grows sharply with review delay, defining a low-cost window that standard RLVR pipelines fail to exploit. To address this, we propose \textbf{\method{}}, a retention-aware review mechanism that tracks mastered prompts and periodically reintroduces them to \textbf{remind} the model of previous solutions. By utilizing pre-rollout batch replacement, \method{} incurs zero additional rollout overhead. Evaluated across 20 benchmarks spanning image-text, video, and text-only tasks with Qwen3-VL and Qwen2.5-Math, \method{} consistently improves performance over GRPO, DAPO, and replay baselines, demonstrating robust generalizability across modalities and algorithms.

</details>


### [123] [HARVE: Hacking-Aware Reward-Head Vector Editing for Robust Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03131)
*Shuang Liu,Yuxuan Bo,Qiuyang Zhao,Caiyue Huang,Xiaorong Chen,Yanguang Liu,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RewardHackBench基准测试（含13种奖励黑客模式）和HARVE训练无关奖励头编辑方法，用于提升LLM奖励模型的奖励黑客鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型是大型语言模型对齐的核心，但仍然容易受到奖励黑客攻击。为了评估奖励模型的鲁棒性，需要设计专门的基准测试和改进方法。

Method: 1) 构建RewardHackBench基准测试，包含13种覆盖现实生活高风险领域和一般设置的奖励黑客模式；2) 提出HARVE方法，从与选定黑客子类别相关的残差流方向中识别多方向黑客子空间，并移除与该子空间对齐的奖励头向量分量。该方法无需微调，仅使用少量对比正负样本。

Result: 在8个奖励模型上发现特定子类别存在严重失败；HARVE显著提高黑客鲁棒性，优于微调基线，同时保持奖励模型的通用能力。分析表明奖励黑客更适合建模为多维残差空间结构，而非孤立表面线索。

Conclusion: 奖励黑客攻击具有多维特征，HARVE提供了一种无需训练的奖励头编辑方案，有效减少奖励头对黑客相关特征的敏感性。

Abstract: Reward models are central to large language model (LLM) alignment, but they remain vulnerable to reward hacking. To evaluate reward-model robustness, we introduce RewardHackBench containing 13 reward-hacking patterns covering real life high-stakes domains and general settings, and we find severe failures on specific subcategories across eight reward models. To mitigate these failures, we propose HARVE, a training-free reward-head editing method for scalar reward models. Instead of fine-tuning the reward model, HARVE identifies a multi-directional hacking subspace from residual stream directions associated with selected hacking subcategories, and removes the component of the reward-head vector aligned with that subspace. This directly reduces the reward head's sensitivity to hacking-related features using only a small set of contrastive gold-hacked examples, without gradient updates or fine-tuning. Comprehensive experiments across eight reward models indicates that \model improves hacking robustness, outperforms fine-tuning baselines, and preserves reward-models' general capability. Further analyses suggest that reward hacking is better captured as a multidimensional residual-space structure than by isolated surface cues.

</details>


### [124] [Right Makes Might: Aligning Verified Hidden States Empowers RL Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03234)
*Ziyue Wang,Aomufei Yuan,Yongfu Zhu,Shuai Dong,Wenpu Liu,Yiran Yao,Weichu Xie,Yuqi Xu,Caoyuan Ma,Wenqi Shao,Xiaoying Zhang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hidden-Align，一种辅助损失函数，通过在RL训练中对正确rollout的锚点token（答案标记前的位置）进行最后一层隐藏状态对齐，增强大语言模型的数学推理能力。在八个数学推理基准上，该方法在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/14B三个规模上分别比DAPO基线提升了3.8、6.2和5.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的RLVR（可验证奖励的强化学习）方法将每个正确的rollout仅压缩为一个比特的奖励信号，忽略了正确rollout之间隐藏状态所共享的几何结构。作者发现，在锚点token（答案标记前的位置）处，正确rollout的隐藏状态由于需要产生相同答案而自然趋同（余弦相似度约0.84），但仍保留了来自不同推理路径的残余方差。利用这一结构信息有望帮助模型提取统一的"正确决策"表示，减少对具体推理路径的敏感性。

Method: Hidden-Align是一种辅助损失函数，在RL训练过程中对正确rollout在锚点token位置的最后一层隐藏状态进行对齐。该方法鼓励正确rollout在该位置实现完全对齐，促使模型提取统一的正确决策表征，同时不引入任何额外的训练或推理开销。作者还通过消融实验系统分析了损失类型、锚点位置、层深度和损失权重等因素的影响。

Result: 在八个数学推理基准上，Hidden-Align在Qwen3-1.7B、4B和14B三个模型规模上分别比DAPO基线平均pass@1提升了3.8、6.2和5.4个百分点，且在三个规模上均观察到一致的pass@k提升。消融实验进一步验证了损失类型、锚点位置、层深度和损失权重等设计选择的有效性。

Conclusion: Hidden-Align通过利用正确rollout之间隐藏状态的几何结构信息，以零额外开销的方式显著提升了大语言模型的数学推理性能。该方法表明，在RLVR框架中挖掘奖励信号之外的隐藏状态结构具有重要的潜力，为提升模型推理能力提供了一条简洁有效的途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant approach for improving mathematical reasoning in large language models, yet current methods reduce each correct rollout to a single reward bit, ignoring the geometric structure shared among their hidden states. Investigating this structure, we find that at the anchor token (the position immediately before the answer marker), correct rollouts converge naturally because they must produce the same answer (cosine similarity ~0.84), yet each retains residual variance from its unique reasoning path. Encouraging full alignment at this point pushes the model to extract a unified "correct decision" representation, reducing sensitivity to which reasoning path was taken. Based on this observation, we propose Hidden-Align, an auxiliary loss function that aligns the last-layer hidden states of correct rollouts at the anchor token during RL training, with zero overhead in both training and inference. On eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, Hidden-Align improves average pass@1 over the DAPO baseline by 3.8, 6.2, and 5.4 percentage points on Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 14B respectively, with consistent pass@k gains across all three scales, supported by ablations on loss type, anchor position, layer depth, and loss weight.

</details>


### [125] [When RLHF Fails: A Mechanistic Taxonomy of Reward Hacking, Collapse, and Evaluator Gaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03238)
*Zelalem Abahana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对RLHF（基于人类反馈的强化学习）训练过程中的失败模式进行了系统性实证研究，分类、定位并部分预测了奖励黑客（reward hacking）等训练动态失效现象，发现这些失效不仅是最终模型的病态表现，而是可被分类和预见的训练过程动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF通过学习到的代理奖励替代难以精确描述的人类目标，但这种替代引入了结构化的失败面：优化可能提升学习奖励却降低外部质量，或暴露代理奖励的欠对齐问题。现有工作多将奖励黑客视为单一终态事件，缺乏对训练过程中多种失败模式的系统性分类与定位研究。

Method: 构建了一个紧凑的RLHF流水线，包含PPO、DPO、不确定性惩罚PPO（UP-PPO）、奖励模型不确定性、近似策略漂移、多样性与重复诊断，以及两个外部LLM评判器。通过61个检查点行和1920个行级转换，根据学习奖励、评判分数和平均评判分数的方向对检查点间的匹配转换进行分类，并使用转换前的逻辑回归模型预测行级奖励黑客行为。

Result: （1）激进PPO的局部奖励黑客率最高（14.45%，95% CI: 10.16-18.75），UP-PPO在相同激进设置下比率更低（11.33-10.94%）。（2）转换前逻辑回归模型预测行级奖励黑客的ROC-AUC达0.821。（3）行级分析发现3/12个设置中存在检查点平均值无法捕捉的局部奖励黑客现象。

Conclusion: RLHF的失败不仅是最终模型的病理现象，而是贯穿训练过程的动态行为，可以被系统地分类、定位和部分预测。这一方法论结论强调需要关注训练动态而非仅评估最终模型，不确定性惩罚（如UP-PPO）可在一定程度上缓解奖励黑客问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) makes large-scale post-training possible by replacing an underspecified human objective with learned and scalable proxies. The same substitution creates a structured failure surface: optimization can raise the learned reward while external quality falls, degrade both proxy and judge scores, reveal proxy under-alignment, or produce evaluator-specific disagreement. We present an empirical failure-mode study of a compact RLHF pipeline with proximal policy optimization (PPO), direct preference optimization (DPO), uncertainty-penalized PPO (UP-PPO), reward-model uncertainty, approximate policy drift, diversity and repetition diagnostics, and two external LLM judges. Rather than treating reward hacking as a single terminal event, we classify matched transitions between checkpoints using the directions of the learned reward, judge scores, and average judge score. Across 61 checkpoint rows and 1920 row-level transitions, aggressive PPO has the highest localized reward-hacking rate (14.45%; bootstrap 95% CI: 10.16-18.75), while UP-PPO yields lower rates in the same aggressive regime (11.33-10.94%). A pre-transition logistic model predicts future row-level reward hacking with ROC-AUC 0.821, and row-level analysis finds localized reward hacking that checkpoint averages miss in 3 of 12 settings. The central conclusion is methodological: RLHF failures are not only final-model pathologies, but training dynamics that can be classified, localized, and partially anticipated.

</details>


### [126] [When Model Merging Breaks Routing: Training-Free Calibration for MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03391)
*Canbin Huang,Tianyuan Shi,Xiaojun Quan,Jingang Wang,Jianfei Zhang,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE模型合并中的路由崩溃问题，提出HARC训练无关框架，利用二阶曲率信息重新对齐合并后的路由器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并技术难以应用于Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)架构，存在路由崩溃问题：合并后的路由器无法将token调度到合适的专家

Method: 提出Hessian-Aware Router Calibration (HARC)，一种训练无关框架，利用二阶曲率信息重新对齐合并后的路由器，采用闭式解并通过矩阵-free共轭梯度法高效求解

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，HARC有效缓解了路由崩溃问题，在各种MoE合并基线方法上取得了显著性能提升

Conclusion: HARC成功解决了MoE模型合并中的路由崩溃问题，为MoE模型合并提供了有效解决方案，具有实用价值

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a cost-effective approach for consolidating the capabilities of multiple LLMs without retraining. However, existing merging techniques, largely based on linear parameter arithmetic or optimization, struggle when applied to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures. We identify a critical failure mode in MoE merging, termed routing breakdown, in which the merged router fails to dispatch tokens to suitable experts. Routing breakdown stems from the sensitivity of the non-linear softmax and discrete Top-k routing mechanisms to parameter perturbations from merging, a sensitivity further amplified by load-balancing constraints imposed during MoE pretraining. Because fine-tuned experts exhibit distinct specializations, even modest misrouting can cause severe performance degradation. To address this issue, we propose Hessian-Aware Router Calibration (HARC), a training-free framework that leverages second-order curvature information to realign the merged router. This approach admits a closed-form solution that can be efficiently solved using a matrix-free conjugate gradient method. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks show that HARC effectively mitigates routing breakdown across diverse MoE merging baselines and leads to substantial performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/huangcb01/HARC.

</details>


### [127] [KVarN: Variance-Normalized KV-Cache Quantization Mitigates Error Accumulation in Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03458)
*Lorenz K. Muller,Philippe Bich,Chiara Boretti,Hyun-Min Chang,Jiawei Zhuang,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了KVarN，一种无需校准的KV缓存量化方法，通过Hadamard旋转和双尺度方差归一化解决自回归解码中量化误差累积问题，在2-bit精度下于MATH500、AIME24和HumanEval等生成式基准上取得了新的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（test-time scaling）在大语言模型中能获得更好的推理能力，但在长时序解码过程中KV缓存不断增长，导致内存瓶颈。现有KV缓存量化方法主要在类预填充（prefill-like）设置下评估，而在自回归解码中量化误差会跨时间步累积，主要由错误的token尺度引起，现有方法无法有效应对这一问题。

Method: KVarN是一种无需校准的KV缓存量化器，包含两个关键组件：(1) 对K和V矩阵应用Hadamard旋转；(2) 沿K和V矩阵的两个轴进行双尺度方差归一化（dual-scaling variance normalization）。该组合有效修正了异常的token尺度误差，并显著减少误差累积。

Result: KVarN在2-bit精度下于多个生成式基准（MATH500、AIME24、HumanEval）上建立了KV缓存量化的新最优性能，显著优于现有基线方法。此外，KVarN已在vLLM中实现并开源。

Conclusion: KVarN通过Hadamard旋转与双尺度方差归一化的结合，有效解决了自回归解码中KV缓存量化误差累积的核心问题，在极低（2-bit）精度下仍能保持优异的生成性能，为大语言模型长时序推理的内存优化提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is a powerful approach to obtain better reasoning in large language models, but it becomes memory-bottlenecked during long-horizon decoding, as the KV-cache grows. KV-cache quantization can help improve this, but current methods are evaluated under prefill-like settings and errors behave differently under autoregressive decoding. We show that in the latter regime, quantization errors accumulate across timesteps, driven primarily by incorrect token scales. We introduce KVarN, a calibration-free KV-cache quantizer that applies a Hadamard rotation followed by a dual-scaling variance normalization across both axes of the K and V matrices. We find that this combination fixes outlying token-scale errors and substantially reduces error accumulation over existing baselines. KVarN establishes a new state-of-theart for KV-cache quantization on generative benchmarks, including MATH500, AIME24 and HumanEval, at 2-bit precision. A vLLM implementation of the KVarN method is available at https://github.com/huawei-csl/KVarN

</details>


### [128] [CauTion: Knowing When to Trust LLMs for Ensemble Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03602)
*Bo Peng,Kaiwen Wu,Sirui Chen,Zhiheng Wang,Yu Qiao,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CauTion 是一个将大语言模型（LLM）领域知识可靠地整合到统计因果发现算法集成框架中的方法，通过共识过滤和LLM可靠性估计解决现有方法中LLM错误、高token成本和算法偏差等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现面临统计可区分性不足、样本量敏感等挑战；现有LLM增强方法存在LLM错误、高token成本以及过度依赖单一算法导致偏差的问题。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1）算法集成通过共识投票解决96%的共识边，实现近完美精度；2）基于标注无关的信任校准机制估计LLM与算法的相对可靠性，采用信任加权投票限制LLM仅在算法证据不可靠时介入；3）循环修复步骤确保最终因果图的有效无环性。

Result: 在六个数据集上的实验表明，CauTion持续优于数据中心和LLM增强基线，在大图上增益更大，对LLM错误具有强鲁棒性，共识边达到近完美精度。

Conclusion: CauTion成功解决了LLM错误和算法偏差问题，通过可靠整合LLM领域知识与统计推断，在多种数据集上实现了优越的性能和鲁棒性，为因果发现提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data remains challenging due to the fundamental limitations of purely statistical methods, such as statistical distinguishability within equivalence classes and sensitivity to finite sample sizes. While large language models (LLMs) offer a promising source of domain knowledge to complement statistical inference, existing LLM-augmented methods are vulnerable to LLM errors and incur high token costs. Moreover, reliance on a single data-centric algorithm can make results sensitive to algorithm-specific biases. To address these limitations, we propose CauTion, a framework that reliably integrates LLM domain knowledge into an ensemble of statistical causal discovery algorithms through consensus filtering and LLM reliability estimation. CauTion proceeds in three stages. First, an algorithm ensemble utilizes a consensus voting to resolve up to 96% of edges on which algorithms agree, achieving near-perfect accuracy on the filtered consensus edges. Second, a trust-calibrated arbitration mechanism estimates the relative reliability of the LLM and the algorithms via an annotation-free trust calibration procedure, which is then utilized to govern a trust-weighted voting process that restricts LLM arbitration exclusively to edges with unreliable algorithmic evidence. Third, a cycle repair step is applied to guarantee the final causal graph is validly acyclic. Experiments on six datasets demonstrate that CauTion consistently outperforms both data-centric and LLM-augmented baselines, with larger gains on larger graphs and strong robustness to LLM errors. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauTion.

</details>


### [129] [Exploiting Verification-Generation Gap: Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03608)
*Jiahui Li,Jianfeng Shan,Wenpei Chen,Shunyu Wu,Jian Lou,Wenjie Feng,Dan Li,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TTRL-CoCoV（Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification），一种基于置信度自适应的测试时强化学习框架，通过置信度条件化验证机制解决无标签场景下Pass@k优化中低置信样本伪标签错误和高置信样本多样性崩溃的问题，在多个基准上显著提升了Pass@1和Pass@k性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时强化学习（TTRL）研究主要关注Pass@1性能，但在无标签设置下，衡量生成覆盖率的Pass@k指标同样关键却未被充分探索。直接将RLVR中有效的Pass@k优势设计应用于无标签场景效果不佳，原因在于：低置信样本的伪标签估计大概率错误，而高置信样本的候选答案存在严重的多样性崩溃问题。

Method: 提出TTRL-CoCoV框架，核心思想是"验证能力通常领先于生成能力"，据此设计置信度条件化机制：(1) 高置信样本：引导验证器自举并施加探索增强奖励，防止多样性崩溃；(2) 低置信样本：将伪标签选择委托给验证器，过滤错误伪标签；(3) 中等置信样本：完全跳过验证步骤。该框架同时扩大Pass@k覆盖范围并提升Pass@1性能。

Result: 在6个广泛认可的基准测试上超越所有竞争方法；相比TTRL，Pass@1平均绝对提升9.8%，Pass@16平均绝对提升18.7%；与完全监督的RL方法相比，在多个推理基准上Pass@1绝对提升最高达5.0%。

Conclusion: TTRL-CoCoV通过置信度自适应的验证机制有效解决了无标签测试时强化学习中Pass@k优化的两大瓶颈（伪标签错误和多样性崩溃），证明了验证能力可以指导生成能力的提升，在无标签甚至与监督方法对比的场景下均取得了显著的推理性能改进。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing the complex reasoning abilities of large language models in a completely label-free manner. Despite existing studies focusing on Pass@1 performance, optimizing Pass@k remains under-explored yet critical in label-free settings, which measures generation coverage for sustained exploration. Optimizing Pass@k in label-free setting is highly non-trivial, as directly applying the Pass@k advantage designs effective for RLVR yields unsatisfactory performance. Through in-depth empirical analysis, we discover the root causes hindering performance: pseudo-label estimations for low-confidence samples have a high probability of being incorrect, while candidate answers for high-confidence samples suffer from severe diversity collapse. To overcome these hurdles, we propose TTRL-CoCoV (Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification), a novel confidence-adaptive framework that expands Pass@k coverage and improves Pass@1 performance. Based on our key insight that verification capability generally leads generation capability, TTRL-CoCoV employs a confidence-conditioned mechanism: for high-confidence samples, it bootstraps verifier and applies an exploration-enhancing reward to prevent diversity collapse; for low-confidence samples, it delegates pseudo-label selection to the verifier to filter incorrect pseudo-labels; and for medium-confidence samples, it bypasses verification entirely. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TTRL-CoCoV outperforms the best competing methods across 6 widely-recognized benchmarks, achieves average absolute gains of +9.8% in Pass@1 and +18.7% in Pass@16 over TTRL, and even achieves absolute Pass@1 improvements of up to +5.0% across multiple reasoning benchmarks when compared against fully supervised RL methods. Our code repository: https://github.com/shanjf666/CoCoV.

</details>


### [130] [The Shape of Addition: Geometric Structures of Arithmetic in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03645)
*Liuyuan Wen,Xun Zhu,Lihao Huang,Wenbin Li,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型在基础算术中的脆弱性源于内部连续表示与离散输出之间的量化失配，提出了几何框架（IRST结构与Noisy Quantization Model）来解释算术错误的成因，并基于几何一致性检验方法在推理时检测和修正这些错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在基础算术运算（如多位数加法）中表现出令人困惑的脆弱性，暗示模型内部计算与离散输出之间存在脱节。作者希望从几何角度理解这一现象的根本原因，即模型内部如何表示和执行算术运算，以及为什么会产生系统性错误。

Method: 1) 分析残差流几何结构，识别出Iso-Raw-Sum Trajectory (IRST)——一种由语义数字锚定、由连续进位纤维调制的几何结构；2) 提出Noisy Quantization Model，将算术错误建模为内部神经噪声将连续的"Carry Potential"推过量量化阈值而产生的"Geometric Slippage"；3) 基于该框架解释Probe Versatility现象（轻量级探针如何从单一激活向量中分离共存信号）；4) 提出几何一致性检验方法，在推理时检测和修正量化失败。

Result: 1) 发现了多位数加法中残差流的IRST几何结构，揭示了进位信号的连续表示方式；2) Noisy Quantization Model成功将算术错误解释为连续-离散量化边界的噪声漂移；3) 解释了探针多样性现象——同一激活向量中可分离真实信号与幻觉信号；4) 几何一致性检验方法在推理阶段有效检测并修正了量化失败导致的算术错误。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的算术脆弱性本质上是内部连续几何表示向离散符号输出量化过程中的噪声驱动失败。通过几何视角（IRST结构、Carry Potential、Geometric Slippage）可以统一解释算术错误、探针多样性和幻觉共存等现象，并据此设计推理时的检测与修正策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models exhibit paradoxical fragility in fundamental arithmetic, implying a disconnect between internal computation and discrete output. By analyzing the residual stream geometry during multi-operand addition, we identify the Iso-Raw-Sum Trajectory (IRST), a geometric structure where representations are anchored by semantic digits and modulated by continuous carry fibers. We propose the Noisy Quantization Model to explain this geometry, framing arithmetic errors as Geometric Slippages caused by internal neural noise pushing a continuous, latent Carry Potential across quantization thresholds. This geometric framework further elucidates Probe Versatility, explaining how lightweight probes can disentangle coexisting latent signals (such as ground truth versus hallucination) from a single activation vector. Finally, we validate these insights through a geometric consistency check method that effectively detects and corrects these quantization failures during inference. Our code is available at https://github.com/RL-MIND/Shape-of-Addition.

</details>


### [131] [Skill-RM: Unifying Heterogeneous Evaluation Criteria via Agent Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03980)
*Tao Chen,Gangwei Jiang,Pengyu Cheng,Siyuan Huang,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Jiaqi Guo,Mengyu Zhou,Kai Tang,Junling Liu,Qinliang Su,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-RM（Skill Reward Model），将奖励建模重新定义为可复用的"奖励评估技能"的执行过程，通过统一的智能体框架整合异构评估资源（规则验证器、参考答案、程序化检查清单、复杂评分标准等），为大语言模型后训练提供一致且透明的奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型后训练中的奖励评估依赖异构标准（如规则验证器、真实参考答案、程序化检查清单和复杂评分标准），缺乏统一机制来整合所有类型的证据。这种碎片化导致评估不一致、缺乏透明度，限制了奖励模型在强化微调（RFT）和强化学习（RL）等场景中的效果。

Method: 提出Skill-RM框架，将奖励建模重构为结构化智能体任务（agentic task）——即可复用的"奖励评估技能"。该框架提供一致接口来编排异构资源，根据每个输入的具体需求动态选择和聚合证据，从而超越静态评估方式。核心思想是将奖励计算视为一种技能执行过程，实现跨任务的统一性和透明性。

Result: 在奖励基准测试和下游应用（包括Best-of-N选择和强化学习）上的大量实验表明，Skill-RM在多种任务上均持续优于传统判别基线方法，验证了动态证据编排策略的有效性。

Conclusion: Skill-RM不仅为奖励建模提供了统一解决方案，还通过策略性、动态的证据编排实现了更优性能，为LLM后训练中的奖励评估提供了一种新的范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) provide critical feedback signals for LLM post-training, notably in reinforced fine-tuning (RFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) pipelines. However, current reward evaluation relies on heterogeneous criteria such as rule-based verifiers, ground-truth references, procedural checklists, and complex rubrics, where a unified mechanism to integrate all types of evidence remains unexplored. To this end, we propose Skill Reward Model (Skill-RM), a unified framework that reformulates reward modeling as the execution of a reusable Reward-Evaluation Skill. By treating reward computation as a structured agentic task, Skill-RM provides a consistent interface to orchestrate heterogeneous resources, dynamically selecting and aggregating evidence tailored to the specific requirements of each input. This approach enables the reward model to move beyond static evaluation, ensuring consistency and transparency across diverse tasks. Extensive experiments on reward benchmarks and downstream applications, including best-of-N selection and reinforcement learning, demonstrate that Skill-RM consistently outperforms traditional judge baselines. Our findings suggest that Skill-RM not only provides a unified solution for reward modeling but also achieves superior performance through the strategic and dynamic orchestration of evidence. The code is at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/Skill-RM.

</details>


### [132] [A Close Look At World Model Recovery In Supervised Fine-Tuned LLM Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03685)
*Patrick Emami,Nan Qiang,Peter Graf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一系列可解释性实验，研究了经过监督微调（SFT）的大语言模型（LLM）在学习端到端经典规划任务时，是否真正学会了表示和推理规划问题，即是否恢复了内部世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）能提升大语言模型在经典规划任务上的端到端生成能力，但模型是否真正学会了表示和推理规划问题（即内部是否形成了世界模型）仍不清楚。由于经典规划问题的复杂性和端到端规划生成的挑战，这一问题长期难以深入研究。

Method: 设计并执行了一系列可解释性实验，从内部表示和生成能力两个维度全面审视微调后的LLM是否恢复了世界模型。具体包括：1）探测模型内部是否线性编码了动作有效性和部分状态谓词；2）对比模型输出概率分类能力与内部表示分离有效/无效动作的能力；3）比较不同状态空间覆盖范围（如随机游走数据）对世界模型恢复精度的影响。

Result: a）在有效动作序列上进行监督微调后，LLM能够线性编码动作有效性和部分状态谓词；b）即使模型在输出概率上难以准确分类动作有效性，其内部表示仍可能将有效与无效动作区分开来；c）微调时更广泛的状态空间覆盖（如来自随机游走的数据）能更准确地恢复底层世界模型。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一套将可解释性技术应用于规划LLM的方法框架，并产生了关于LLM中知识如何表示的新见解，为理解LLM在规划任务中的内部机制提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) improves end-to-end classical planning in large language models (LLMs), but do these models also learn to represent and reason about the planning problems they are solving? Due to the relative complexity of classical planning problems and the challenge that end-to-end plan generation poses for LLMs, it has been difficult to explore this question. In our work, we devise and perform a series of interpretability experiments that holistically interrogate world model recovery by examining both internal representations and generative capabilities of fine-tuned LLMs. We find that: a) Supervised fine-tuning on valid action sequences enables LLMs to linearly encode action validity and some state predicates. b) Models that struggle to use output probabilities for classifying action validity may still learn internal representations that separate valid from invalid actions. c) Broader state space coverage during fine-tuning, such as from random walk data, yields more accurate recovery of the underlying world model. In summary, this work contributes a recipe for applying interpretability techniques to planning LLMs and generates insights that shed light on open questions about how knowledge is represented in LLMs.

</details>


### [133] [Multi$^2$: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Decision-Making with LLM-Based Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03698)
*Sangeun Park,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Multi²，一个分层多智能体决策框架，通过将智能体行为分解为高层子目标生成（System 1，基于SFT）和底层原子动作执行（System 2，基于离线到在线RL），有效缓解了LLM智能体在长时序交互中的目标漂移问题，并发布了三个分层基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体虽然具备较强的上下文推理能力，但在长时序决策中表现脆弱，经常出现"目标漂移"现象——即目标和计划在长时间交互中逐渐偏离。这是构建可持续与动态环境交互的智能体系统的核心障碍。

Method: 提出Multi²分层多智能体框架，将智能体行为显式分解为两个互补角色：(1) 高层智能体（System 1）：利用监督微调（SFT）进行上下文感知的子目标生成；(2) 底层智能体（System 2）：通过离线到在线强化学习（RL）在交互环境中执行原子动作。这种分层分离设计实现了稳定的长时序控制，减轻了目标漂移，并支持高效适应。

Result: 在多种交互环境中，Multi²持续优于强基线智能体方法，在多轮交互中展现出更强的鲁棒性和协调性。此外，还引入并发布了三个分层基准数据集，填补了LLM智能体分层决策训练与评估的长期空白。

Conclusion: 通过将高层规划与底层执行解耦为互补的多智能体角色，Multi²有效解决了LLM智能体长时序决策中的目标漂移问题，显著提升了鲁棒性和适应性，同时提供的分层基准数据集为该领域的后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: A central goal of large language model (LLM) research is to build agentic systems that can plan, act, and adapt through sustained interaction with dynamic environments. While recent LLM-based agents exhibit impressive contextual reasoning, their long-horizon decision-making remains fragile, often suffering from objective drift, where goals and plans drift over extended interactions. We introduce Multi$^2$, a hierarchical multi-agent decision-making framework that explicitly decomposes agent behavior into complementary roles. A high-level agent (System 1) focuses on context-aware sub-goal generation using supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while a low-level agent (System 2) executes atomic actions through offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) in interactive environments. This separation enables stable long-horizon control, mitigates objective drift, and allows efficient adaptation. Across diverse interactive environments, Multi$^2$ consistently outperforms strong agentic baselines, demonstrating improved robustness and coordination in multi-turn interaction. Beyond performance, we introduce and release three hierarchical benchmark datasets, filling a long-standing gap in training and evaluating hierarchical decision-making for LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [134] [When Graph Tokens Sink: A Mechanistic Analysis of Graph Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03712)
*Ding Zhang,Runtao Zhou,Wenqing Zheng,Rizal Fathony,Bayan Bruss,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图语言模型（GLMs）中LLM如何内部处理图token，发现图token的激活显著性（如"图汇聚token"）与图信息的实际语义利用之间存在解耦现象，即激活值高的图token并非下游预测中最重要的语义或结构信息载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图语言模型（GLMs）通过将图拓扑和节点信息转化为图token，使LLM能够联合处理图结构和文本指令。然而，目前尚不清楚LLM内部如何解读这些图token，以及这些图token是否真正作为图结构的有意义载体。因此，有必要深入分析GLM架构中LLM对图信息的内部处理机制。

Method: 通过分析代表性GLM架构中图token的行为，研究图token在LLM内部的激活模式。具体采用剪枝（pruning）、重新定位（repositioning）和交换（swapping）等干预手段，系统性地测试图汇聚token（graph sink tokens）在下游预测中的语义和结构重要性。同时对比了经典语言模型和视觉语言模型中的注意力汇聚现象。

Result: （1）图汇聚token在激活层面持续表现为异常值，具有大量激活值且偏向早期图token位置；（2）这种激活层面的显著性并不意味着这些token是图信息的主要载体；（3）与经典注意力汇聚不同，图汇聚token不一定吸引最大的注意力权重；（4）通过干预实验证明，图汇聚token并非下游预测中最关键的语义或结构token。

Conclusion: 当前GLMs将图结构映射到LLM token空间后，生成的图token表示并未自然形成完全可用的拓扑感知内部表示，而是表现出激活显著性与图语义效用之间的解耦。这一发现揭示了现有图token构建、放置和对齐机制的局限性，为未来GLM的改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Graph Language Models (GLMs) have become a promising direction for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to graph learning tasks. By transforming graph topology and node information into graph tokens, GLMs allow LLMs to jointly process structured graph inputs and textual instructions. Yet, it remains unclear how LLMs internally interpret these graph tokens and whether graph tokens act as meaningful carriers of graph structure. In this work, we analyze how LLMs process graph information through graph-token behavior in representative GLM architectures.
  Findings. We find that the internal saliency of graph tokens in GLMs is not equivalent to graph information utilization. Graph sink tokens consistently emerge as activation-level outliers: they can be identified by massive activation values along a small set of hidden-state dimensions and are biased toward early graph-token positions. However, this activation-level saliency does not imply that these tokens are the main carriers of graph information. Unlike classical attention sinks in language and vision-language models, graph sink tokens do not necessarily attract the largest attention weights from query tokens. Through pruning, repositioning, and swapping interventions, we show that graph sink tokens are not the most important semantic or structural tokens for downstream prediction.
  Implications. Together, these results suggest that after current GLMs map graph structure into the LLM token space, the resulting graph-token representations do not naturally form a fully usable topology-aware internal representation; instead, they exhibit a decoupling between activation-level saliency and graph-semantic utility. This decoupling points to limitations in existing graph-token construction, placement, and alignment mechanisms.

</details>


### [135] [Tool-Aware Optimization with Entropy Guidance for Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03762)
*Hongye Cao,Nuo Yan,Haoyuan Deng,Ziwei Wang,Tianpei Yang,Jing Huo,Yuyao Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAO-RL是一个统一框架，通过工具感知的轨迹过滤和熵引导探索相结合，提升带工具使用能力的LLM在复杂任务上的推理性能，解决训练不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然代理强化学习使大语言模型具备工具使用能力能显著提升复杂任务推理，但工具集成往往导致训练不稳定：过度依赖工具会引起输入分布偏移，而过度保守的工具使用则限制了有效探索。

Method: TAO-RL包含两个相互增强的组件：1) 数据层面进行工具感知的轨迹过滤，丢弃所有工具调用失败的轨迹，以及所有rollout要么正确要么错误（产生退化的优势估计）的轨迹，保留兼具工具能力和信息性的数据；2) 算法层面引入工具感知的熵引导奖励，在工具调用后的token处重新 shaping 优势函数，鼓励在关键决策点探索更多样化的推理路径。

Result: 在3个模型规模和7个具有挑战性的推理基准上的广泛实验表明，TAO-RL在性能上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了代理强化学习中的训练不稳定问题，通过轨迹过滤建立高质量的训练分布，通过熵引导探索驱动关键工具交互点的更强推理行为，实现了工具使用能力与有效探索的平衡。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) equips large language models (LLMs) with tool-use capabilities that substantially improve reasoning on complex tasks. However, integrating external tools often destabilizes training: over-reliance on tools can induce input distribution shift, while overly conservative tool use limits effective exploration. To address this issue, we propose a unified framework TAO-RL that couples tool-aware trajectory filtering with entropy-guided exploration for efficient policy optimization. Specifically, at the data level, TAO-RL filters rollout trajectories along two criteria: discarding those where all tool invocations fail to execute, and removing those where all rollouts are either correct or incorrect, as both cases yield degenerate advantage estimates that contribute no discriminative learning signal. This joint filtering retains data that are both tool-capable and informative, establishing a high-quality training distribution. At the algorithmic level, we introduce a tool-aware entropy-guided bonus that reshapes the advantage function at post-tool-call tokens, encouraging the policy to explore more diverse reasoning paths at critical decision points. These two components are mutually reinforcing: trajectory filtering establishes a clean and informative training foundation, while entropy-guided exploration drives stronger reasoning behaviors at critical tool-interaction junctures. Extensive experiments on 7 challenging reasoning benchmarks across 3 model scales demonstrate the superiority of TAO-RL over existing methods.

</details>


### [136] [Language Models Need Sleep: Learning to Self-Modify and Consolidate Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03979)
*Ali Behrouz,Farnoosh Hashemi,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受人类学习过程启发的"睡眠"（Sleep）范式，使模型能够将短期上下文记忆蒸馏为稳定的长期知识，并通过"做梦"（Dreaming）过程进行自我改进，从而实现持续学习和知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习模型（包括大语言模型LLMs）虽然在即时预测和上下文学习方面表现优异，但缺乏持续学习的能力，无法有效地将时序上下文知识迁移到长期参数中。这限制了模型在长期、连续学习场景下的表现。

Method: 提出"睡眠"范式，包含两个阶段：（1）记忆巩固——通过"知识播种"（Knowledge Seeding）的自下而上蒸馏过程，将较小模型的记忆蒸馏到更大的网络中，结合在策略蒸馏与基于强化学习（RL）的模仿学习来实现广义蒸馏；（2）做梦——模型利用强化学习自主生成合成数据课程，在无需人类监督的情况下排练新知识并优化现有能力。

Result: 在长时间跨度持续学习、知识整合和少样本泛化等任务上的实验验证了睡眠阶段的重要性，证明了该方法能够有效提升模型的持续学习与自我改进能力。

Conclusion: 受人类学习启发的"睡眠"范式为机器学习模型提供了一种将短期记忆转化为长期知识、并通过自我生成数据进行持续改进的通用框架，对实现模型的持续学习和自主进化具有重要意义。

Abstract: The past few decades have witnessed significant advances in the design of machine learning algorithms, from early studies on task-specific shallow models to more general deep Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite showing promising results in tasks that require instant prediction or in-context learning, existing models lack the ability to continually learn and effectively transfer their temporal in-context knowledge to their long-term parameters. Inspired by human learning process, we introduce a ''Sleep'' paradigm that allows the models to continually learn, distill their short-term fragile memories into stable long-term knowledge with replay, and recursively improve themselves with ''Dreaming'' process. In more detail, sleep consists of two stages: (1) Memory Consolidation: an upward distillation process, called Knowledge Seeding, where the memories of a smaller-self are distilled into a larger network to provide more capacity while preserving the knowledge. As a proof of concept, we present a new Generalized Distillation process for {Knowledge Seeding} (i.e., the combination of on-policy distillation with Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based imitation learning); (2) Dreaming: a self-improvement phase, where the model uses RL to generate a curriculum of synthetic data to rehearse new knowledge and refine existing capabilities without human supervision. Our experiments on long-horizon, continual learning, knowledge incorporation, and few-shot generalization tasks support the importance of the sleep stage.

</details>
